热门甲板游戏与 Trivia 主持手册Deck Games & Trivia Host Guide

实操解析
  • Bingo 宾果
    • 开场先说明卡片玩法（横线/竖线/满卡三档奖金）并示范喊号
    • 每轮摇号喊号两遍（数字+字母），节奏放缓便于核对
    • 有人喊 "Bingo!" 立即暂停，当场核对卡片再宣布
    • 奖品当场发放并拍照记录
    • 主持要点：控场靠固定口号（如 "Eyes on your cards!"），提前备好备用笔与卡片
    • 道具： Bingo 笼、号码球、卡片、笔、小奖品
  • Trivia 知识问答
    • 准备 15–20 题，分 3–4 轮，题目覆盖影视/地理/航海常识
    • 宣布规则：答题纸写队名，每轮结束后交换批改
    • 每题读两遍，留 30–45 秒作答
    • 累计得分，前三名发奖
    • 主持要点：读题语速放慢、答案争议时以裁判身份一锤定音
    • 道具：题库打印稿、答题纸、笔、计时器、奖品
  • Beam Wars 甲板平衡赛
    • 参赛者站上泳池边宽约 20 cm 的平衡线/横梁
    • 主持人发布指令（单脚站、转身、互抛气球等），违规或落梁者出局
    • 坚持到最后的 1–2 人获胜
    • 主持要点：安全第一——梁下铺防滑垫、禁止推挤，声音要盖住甲板背景音乐
    • 道具：防滑垫、气球、哨子、麦克风、奖品
  • Towel Folding Competition 毛巾折叠赛
    • 示范一款简单毛巾动物（如天鹅）分步折一遍
    • 发毛巾，限时 3–5 分钟让宾客照做
    • 全场举作品，主持人点评并让 applause 决出名次
    • 最佳 1–3 名发奖，作品拍照留念
    • 主持要点：示范慢、口令清楚，鼓励不比严格
    • 道具：浴巾每人一条、示范用毛巾、奖品
  • Scavenger Hunt 寻宝赛
    • 提前在甲板/公共区藏 8–10 条线索，首条线索发起点发放
    • 分组（3–5 人/组）按线索逐点解谜
    • 先集齐全部线索/到达终点的组获胜
    • 终点统一核验发奖
    • 主持要点：藏点须在宾客安全区、不进员工区；每点安排一名工作人员值守
    • 道具：线索卡、藏点清单、对讲机、奖品
Deep Dive
  • Bingo
    • Open by explaining card rules (line / two lines / full house prizes) with a demo call.
    • Draw and call each number twice (letter + number), keeping a slow, steady pace.
    • When someone shouts "Bingo!", pause, verify the card on the spot, then announce.
    • Hand out prizes immediately and log the win.
    • Hosting tips: control the crowd with a signature catchphrase (e.g. "Eyes on your cards!"), keep spare pens and cards ready.
    • Props: bingo cage, number balls, cards, pens, small prizes.
  • Trivia
    • Prep 15–20 questions in 3–4 rounds (movies, geography, nautical knowledge).
    • Announce rules: teams write answers on sheets and swap papers to grade after each round.
    • Read each question twice and allow 30–45 seconds.
    • Tally scores; award top three teams.
    • Hosting tips: speak slowly, settle disputed answers decisively as the judge.
    • Props: printed question sheet, answer sheets, pens, timer, prizes.
  • Beam Wars
    • Contestants stand on a 20 cm-wide ledge along the pool deck.
    • The host issues commands (one-foot stand, turn around, balloon toss); falling off or breaking rules means elimination.
    • Last 1–2 players standing win.
    • Hosting tips: safety first — use non-slip mats, ban pushing, project your voice over deck music.
    • Props: non-slip mats, balloons, whistle, microphone, prizes.
  • Towel Folding Competition
    • Demonstrate one simple animal (e.g. swan) step by step.
    • Hand out towels and give guests 3–5 minutes to replicate.
    • Everyone holds up their creation; the host comments and the loudest applause decides winners.
    • Award 1–3 winners and take photos.
    • Hosting tips: demo slowly, call out each step clearly, keep it fun rather than strict.
    • Props: one bath towel per guest, demo towel, prizes.
  • Scavenger Hunt
    • Hide 8–10 clues around guest areas in advance; distribute the first clue at the start point.
    • Teams of 3–5 solve clues location by location.
    • The first team to collect all clues and reach the finish wins.
    • Verify and award prizes at the finish line.
    • Hosting tips: keep clues inside guest-accessible areas only; station a crew member at each checkpoint.
    • Props: clue cards, hide-spot checklist, radios, prizes.

五种经典毛巾动物叠法Five Classic Towel Animals Step by Step

实操解析
  • 天鹅 Swan（1 条浴巾）
    • 浴巾平铺，两角向中线对折成尖角
    • 从两端同时向外卷成对称卷筒，中间留一段平布
    • 将平布段向上对折成为「颈部+头部」，尖端捏出喙
    • 底部翻开撑平作底座，两卷筒即「翅膀」。要求：左右对称、颈部立稳不倒
  • 小狗 Puppy Dog（1 浴巾+1 手巾）
    • 浴巾纵向卷成紧卷作身体
    • 手巾对折再卷成头部，翻出两角作耳朵
    • 头部卡入身体一端，用剩余角掖出「前爪」
    • 可加墨镜作点睛。要求：耳朵对称、头部朝向一致
  • 大象 Elephant（1 浴巾+1 手巾）
    • 浴巾从一角起斜卷成锥形长卷
    • 将长卷对折——折弯处作象鼻，上端分出两片作耳朵
    • 手巾卷一小团塞入头顶作固定
    • 拉松两侧「耳朵」、摆正鼻子方向。要求：鼻子呈自然弯钩、耳朵左右对称
  • 青蛙 Frog（1 浴巾）
    • 浴巾平铺，两侧向中线折成三折窄条
    • 从两端向中间各卷一段至中点相接
    • 将卷筒两端向外翻成「前腿」，顶部鼓起作「头部」
    • 可用两片绿叶/花瓣作眼睛装饰。要求：身形饱满、前后腿对称
  • 螃蟹 Crab（1 浴巾+1 手巾）
    • 浴巾从两端向中间卷成双卷
    • 对折后两卷筒分开呈「蟹钳」向外张开
    • 手巾卷成小圆团塞入中间作身体拱起
    • 调整钳口开合角度、摆上墨镜即成。要求：钳口张开明显、整体放稳不滚动
Deep Dive
  • Swan (1 bath towel):
    • Lay the towel flat and fold both top corners toward the center to form points.
    • Roll both sides outward simultaneously into two even rolls, leaving a flat section in the middle.
    • Fold the flat section up to form the neck and head; pinch the tip into a beak.
    • Spread the base flat as a stand — the rolls become the wings. Keep it symmetrical and the neck upright.
  • Puppy Dog (1 bath towel + 1 hand towel):
    • Roll the bath towel lengthwise into a tight body.
    • Fold and roll the hand towel into the head, turning out two corners as ears.
    • Tuck the head into one end of the body and shape the remaining corners into front paws.
    • Add sunglasses for character. Ears must be even and the head facing one consistent direction.
  • Elephant (1 bath towel + 1 hand towel):
    • Roll the bath towel diagonally from one corner into a long cone.
    • Fold the roll in half — the bend forms the trunk, the top splits into two ears.
    • Tuck a small hand-towel roll on top to hold the shape.
    • Loosen both "ears" and set the trunk direction. The trunk should curve naturally with symmetric ears.
  • Frog (1 bath towel):
    • Lay flat and fold both sides toward the center into a narrow triple-fold band.
    • Roll each end inward to meet at the middle.
    • Flip the roll ends outward to form the front legs; the raised top becomes the head.
    • Decorate with two leaves or flower petals as eyes. The body should look plump and the legs even.
  • Crab (1 bath towel + 1 hand towel):
    • Roll the bath towel from both ends toward the center.
    • Fold in half and separate the two rolls outward as pincers.
    • Roll the hand towel into a small ball and tuck it into the middle as the raised body.
    • Adjust the pincer openings and finish with sunglasses. Pincers must open clearly and the whole figure must sit stable without rolling.

常见酒水品牌、价位与口味Common Brands, Price & Taste Guide

品类代表品牌（常见船载款）船上常见价位（USD/杯或杯规格）口味特征适合人群
红葡萄酒Penfolds Koonunga Hill、Bogle Merlot9–15 / 杯；40–70 / 瓶黑莓果香、单宁适中，酒体中等至饱满配牛排/红肉的正式用餐宾客
白葡萄酒Kim Crawford Sauvignon Blanc、Kendall-Jackson Chardonnay9–14 / 杯；38–60 / 瓶柑橘与热带果香，清爽高酸或圆润带橡木配海鲜、沙拉的轻食宾客
起泡酒Moët & Chandon Brut、Freixenet Cava12–25 / 杯；55–150 / 瓶细腻气泡、青苹果与酵母香，干型爽利庆祝场合、欢迎酒会
威士忌Chivas Regal 12、Johnnie Walker Black Label10–16 / 份（40 ml）谷物与橡木香，顺滑微甜带烟熏尾韵传统烈酒爱好者、绅士型宾客
伏特加Absolut、Grey Goose8–14 / 份（40 ml）干净中性、微谷物甜感，调酒百搭喜清爽鸡尾酒（如 Moscow Mule）的宾客
朗姆酒Bacardi Superior、Captain Morgan8–12 / 份（40 ml）甘蔗焦糖甜香，白朗姆清爽、金朗姆醇厚热带鸡尾酒（Mojito/Piña Colada）爱好者
金酒Gordon's、Tanqueray、Hendrick's8–14 / 份（40 ml）杜松子与香料植物香，干爽微苦回甘G&T 与经典鸡尾酒客群
啤酒Heineken、Budweiser、Corona6–9 / 瓶或罐麦芽清爽、气泡足，苦度低易入口泳池吧休闲、看比赛、派对人群
龙舌兰Jose Cuervo Especial、Don Julio Blanco9–16 / 份（40 ml）龙舌兰草香与胡椒感，白 tequila 锐利、陈酿圆润Margarita 爱好者、派对宾客

价位为 2026 年国际邮轮常见区间参考，实际以船载酒单（bar menu）与套餐政策为准；含酒精饮品均不含 18% 服务费与当地税费。

常见餐酒搭配Food & Wine Pairing Guide

菜品推荐酒款搭配逻辑
牛排（Grilled Steak）Cabernet Sauvignon / Malbec高单宁高酒体化解油脂，肉脂与单宁互相软化
龙虾（Lobster）Chardonnay（橡木款）或香槟黄油质感配奶油酒体，气泡切割浓腻提升鲜甜
烤三文鱼（Grilled Salmon）Pinot Noir 或干型 Rosé中轻酒体不压鱼味，果酸衬托烟熏焦香
番茄底意面（Tomato-based Pasta）Chianti / Sangiovese高酸酒款与番茄酸度对齐，避免酒显「发苦」
奶油意面（Creamy Pasta）Unoaked Chardonnay / Pinot Grigio清爽酸度切开奶油的厚重，解腻不夺味
咖喱（Curry）微甜 Riesling / Gewürztraminer微甜与花香缓冲辛辣，低温口感降温舒缓
巧克力甜点（Chocolate Dessert）Port / Banyuls 加强酒甜度高于甜点才不显苦涩，焦糖坚果香呼应可可
奶酪拼盘（Cheese Platter）干型气泡酒 / 蓝纹配甜酒气泡刷清口腔脂感，甜咸对比放大风味层次

以上为通用搭配原则，实际推荐以当日菜单与船载酒单为准；侍酒服务流程见本页「餐酒服务操作规范」。

邮轮摄影师业务与流程Ship Photographer Workflow

实操解析
  • 登船照 Embarkation Photo：登船日在舷梯口/登船背景板设点位，宾客经过时主动招呼 "Welcome aboard — would you like a boarding photo?"；拍 1–2 张（全身+半身），当场告知取片地点（Photo Gallery）；照片当天按舱房号归档上架。要求：识别新人身份（婚庆/团建）、保持队伍不积压（每人 ≤1 分钟）。
  • 晚宴跟拍 Dining Room Session：开餐时段驻主餐厅入口，逐一为餐桌拍照；流程：问候→询问是否合影→摆位（主位+家人围绕）→快拍 2–3 张→递上取片卡。要求：不打断用餐、避免开闪光惊扰邻桌、记住船长/餐厅经理巡桌时段的合影节点。
  • 岸上跟拍 Excursion Coverage：随指定 excursion 出发，在上下车点、景点标志性背景位定点拍摄；回船后按团号归档。要求：提前核对 excursion 名单与集合时间、穿便装带好电池与双卡备份、不在危险区域取景。
  • 肖像预约 Portrait Sitting：宾客在 Photo Gallery 或 App 预约时段；流程：确认预约单→布置背景灯光→引导姿势（站姿/坐姿/家庭组合）→连拍 8–15 帧供选→当场预览选片。要求：服装建议提前沟通、拍摄 15–25 分钟/组、儿童需家长陪同。
  • 拍摄-销售闭环：拍 → 修（统一调色、裁剪）→ 打印 → 按舱房号陈列上墙 → 宾客自选购买或摄影师主动推销（"Your photos from tonight's dinner are ready in the gallery!"）→ 刷卡结账；摄影师通常有销售提成（比例因船司而异），陈列位光线与人流是转化关键，每晚闭馆前补齐空位。
Deep Dive
  • Embarkation Photo: Set up at the gangway/boarding backdrop on embarkation day; greet each guest — "Welcome aboard — would you like a boarding photo?" Shoot 1–2 frames (full body + half body), tell guests where to view them (Photo Gallery), and file photos by stateroom number the same day. Requirements: spot honeymooners/groups, keep the line moving (≤1 minute per party).
  • Dining Room Session: Stand at the main dining room entrance during dinner; per table: greet → ask for a photo → arrange seating (hosts centered, family around) → shoot 2–3 quick frames → hand over a pick-up card. Requirements: never interrupt the meal, avoid flash disturbing nearby tables, know when the Captain or Maitre d' makes rounds for group shots.
  • Excursion Coverage: Join assigned excursions; shoot at boarding points and iconic landmarks; file photos by tour number after returning. Requirements: verify tour rosters and meeting times beforehand, carry spare batteries and dual-card backup, never position yourself in unsafe spots.
  • Portrait Sitting: Guests book a slot via the Photo Gallery or App; confirm the booking → set backdrop and lighting → guide poses (standing/seated/family grouping) → shoot 8–15 frames → preview and selection on the spot. Requirements: discuss outfits in advance, allow 15–25 minutes per session, children must be accompanied by a parent.
  • Shoot-to-Sale Pipeline: Shoot → edit (consistent color and cropping) → print → display by stateroom number → guests self-select or you actively upsell ("Your photos from tonight's dinner are ready in the gallery!") → card payment. Photographers usually earn sales commission (percentage varies by cruise line); display lighting and foot traffic drive conversion — restock empty spots nightly before closing.

餐酒服务操作规范Wine Service Standards

实操解析
  • 示酒 Label Presentation：酒瓶立于左臂持送，标签朝向点酒人，报出「产区 + 酒庄 + 年份」，确认酒款无误后开始服务
  • 开瓶 Cutting & Opening：刀环沿瓶唇下缘割箔一圈，去箔收袋；螺旋钻对准瓶心旋入约 5 圈（不穿底），轻拔软塞不出巨响；白/起泡酒放冰桶（白 7–10 ℃、起泡 6–8 ℃）
  • 醒酒判断 Decanting：陈年红酒有沉淀须缓倾入醒酒器，瓶底留约 30 ml 弃置；普通餐酒开瓶后直立静置 5–10 分钟即可；醒酒时长红 15–30 分钟、浓酒体可至 1 小时
  • 试酒 Taste Approval：为点酒人斟约杯容量 1/5（≈30 ml）请其验酒；确认后才为其余宾客续斟；若酒有软木塞污染（corked）等瑕疵，礼貌致歉并更换
  • 斟酒顺序 Service Order：女士优先→长者→顺时针绕桌，最后为点酒人补齐；站位在宾客右侧，倒酒时不碰杯口；红葡萄酒斟至杯身约 1/3，白葡萄酒约 1/2（收窄杯），起泡酒沿杯壁慢斟至 3/4–4/5 防溢泡
  • ⑥ 加酒跟进 Refill & Follow-up：杯量低于 1/3 时示意询问再补；换酒须换杯（或换杯垫）；空瓶后主动递上酒单询问是否续瓶；收瓶时致谢并确认挂账房号。
Deep Dive
  • 1. Label Presentation: Carry the bottle upright on your left arm, label facing the host who ordered; announce the region, producer and vintage, and confirm the selection before serving.
  • 2. Cutting & Opening: Cut the foil with one stroke just below the bottle lip and pocket it; insert the corkscrew about five turns into the cork center without piercing through; withdraw the cork quietly. Chill whites and sparkling wines (white 7–10 °C, sparkling 6–8 °C) in the ice bucket.
  • 3. Decanting: Aged reds with sediment should be poured slowly into a decanter, leaving about 30 ml in the bottle; standard house wines need only 5–10 minutes standing after opening. Decant 15–30 minutes for reds, up to 1 hour for full-bodied wines.
  • 4. Taste Approval: Pour about 1/5 of the glass (≈30 ml) for the host to taste; only after approval serve the remaining guests. If the wine is corked or otherwise faulty, apologize and replace it courteously.
  • 5. Service Order: Ladies first, then elders, moving clockwise around the table; top up the host last. Serve from the guest's right without touching the rim. Fill red wine to about 1/3 of the glass, white wine to about 1/2, and sparkling wine slowly down the side to 3/4–4/5 to control the foam.
  • 6. Refill & Follow-up: Offer a refill when a glass drops below 1/3; change glasses when switching wines (or use a fresh coaster). When the bottle is empty, present the wine list and ask about another bottle; thank the guest and confirm the stateroom charge on collection.

温度、斟酒量等参数为国际邮轮通用规范参考，具体标准以各船司餐饮手册（F&B Manual）为准。

客房部6 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

布草与三层铺床Bed Linen & Triple Sheet Method

深度解析
  • 【是什么】三层铺床（Triple Sheet Method）是国际酒店与邮轮客房的标准铺床法：底层包褥单（fitted sheet）+ 中层平单（flat sheet）+ 顶层被套或盖毯（duvet/blanket），英文名 Triple Sheet / Three-Layer Bed Making，核心目的是每位宾客与床品之间隔一层可换洗平单，保证卫生。
  • 【怎么做】先绷紧 fitted sheet 四角包住床垫；铺平单时宽面对脚端，反折盖毯上缘约 20–30 cm 形成装饰边；两侧做 45° 酒店角（hospital corner）收边，拉紧后床面无皱、四边对称；枕套口朝内、枕头捏形立放；换床单全程约 5–8 分钟/床，每天在住舱也要整理一次，换客（turnover）则全部重铺。
  • 【易错点】常见错误：fitted sheet 只塞对角两只角导致起皱、hospital corner 松散、反折宽度左右不一致、枕套口朝外露出内芯。检查要点是「抖单听声绷紧、四角对齐、中线笔直」；面试常被问到铺床步骤与换一间舱的用时，务必给出具体数字。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Triple Sheet Method is the international hotel/cruise standard — a fitted sheet, a flat sheet, and a duvet or blanket. The middle flat sheet creates a fresh, washable barrier between the guest and the bedding.
  • How: Tuck the fitted sheet snugly at all four corners; lay the flat sheet wide hem toward the foot of the bed and fold the top edge 20–30 cm over the blanket for a decorative band; make 45° hospital corners on both sides and pull everything taut — the bed surface should be wrinkle-free and symmetrical; pillowcase openings face inward with pillows shaped upright. A full linen change takes roughly 5–8 minutes per bed; occupied cabins get a daily refresh, while turnovers get a complete remake.
  • Watch out: Common mistakes are tucking only two diagonal corners, loose hospital corners, uneven fold widths, or pillowcases facing out. Inspectors check for a taut "drum-tight" sheet, aligned corners, and a straight center line. Interviewers often ask you to walk through the steps with real timings — know your numbers.

📍 应用场景：客房服务员（Cabin Steward / Room Attendant）每日清洁、换客翻舱与晚间开床的核心技能。

毛巾艺术Towel Art / Towel Origami

深度解析
  • 【是什么】用浴巾（bath towel）、手巾（hand towel）与面巾（washcloth）折叠成天鹅、大象、螃蟹、猴子、蝎子等造型，点缀床面或梳妆台，英文名 Towel Art / Towel Origami，是邮轮客房标志性的惊喜服务（wow moment），常配墨镜、花瓣或贺卡。
  • 【怎么做】常用基础手法是三折卷（fan fold）与对折卷（roll fold）：如大象=两条卷手巾作腿+浴巾折头+面巾作耳；天鹅=浴巾对角卷成「鹅颈」再回折定型。要求对称、挺括、一次成型，不重复翻动弄皱毛巾；一般每舱 1–2 分钟完成，晚间开床或航程首日摆放，配件按船司特色（如 Carnival 常配墨镜）。
  • 【易错点】错误：折一半拆掉重折导致毛巾起皱、造型歪斜不居中、用了宾客用过的毛巾（必须用干净布草）、香熏花瓣掉进枕缝。检查要点是造型居中、五官配件端正、毛巾无污渍；面试可提「我会在 1 分钟内完成一个天鹅」这类具体展示。
Deep Dive
  • What: Towel Art (or Towel Origami) is folding bath towels, hand towels, and washcloths into swans, elephants, crabs, monkeys, scorpions and other figures to decorate the bed or vanity — a signature cruise "wow moment," often dressed with sunglasses, flower petals, or a welcome card.
  • How: The core techniques are the fan fold and the roll fold — for example, an elephant uses two rolled hand towels for legs, a folded bath towel for the head, and washcloth ears; a swan is a diagonally rolled bath towel with the "neck" looped back and set. Figures must be symmetrical, firm, and made in one pass without re-folding and creasing the towel. Allow 1–2 minutes per cabin, placed during turndown or on embarkation day, with line-specific props (e.g., sunglasses on Carnival).
  • Watch out: Avoid halfway re-folds that crease towels, off-center or lopsided figures, or using a guest's used towel — always clean linen. Check for centering, straight accessories, and spot-free towels. In interviews, a concrete claim like "I can fold a swan in under a minute" lands well.

📍 应用场景：客房服务员晚间开床（Turndown）、航程首日与节庆日客舱布置。

分色抹布Color-Coded Cleaning Cloths

深度解析
  • 【是什么】分色抹布制度是国际邮轮防交叉污染（cross-contamination prevention）的强制卫生规范，英文名 Color-Coded Cleaning Cloths，即按清洁区域指定专用颜色抹布，全屋一块抹布通擦被视为严重违规。
  • 【怎么做】常见配色（各船司略有差异，以船上 SOP 为准）：蓝=客舱家具与桌面、红=仅限马桶及马桶周边、黄=仅限浴缸与洗手盆、绿=镜面与玻璃。每舱开工前按色挂好或取用，用后按色分洗消毒（通常高温机洗 ≥60°C 或按消毒剂浓度浸泡），破洞褪色即报废换新。
  • 【易错点】错误：红黄抹布混用（把马桶区细菌带入浴缸）、擦完镜子顺手用蓝布擦马桶盖、抹布叠放混色。检查要点：主管查房时会核对「哪个区域用哪块布」并抽查卫生培养结果；面试常问「为什么马桶抹布必须专用」，答出 cross-contamination 与 bacteria transfer 即可。
Deep Dive
  • What: Color-coded cloths are a mandatory hygiene standard on international cruise ships to prevent cross-contamination. Each cleaning zone has a designated cloth color; wiping an entire cabin with one cloth is a serious violation.
  • How: Typical color logic (varies slightly by line — follow the ship's SOP): blue for cabin furniture and desks, red for toilets and surrounding surfaces only, yellow for bathtubs and sinks only, green for mirrors and glass. Lay out cloths by color before starting; launder them separately by color, usually hot-washed at 60°C or above or soaked to the sanitizer's dilution; retire any torn or faded cloths immediately.
  • Watch out: The classic mistakes are mixing red and yellow cloths (carrying toilet-area bacteria into the tub) or grabbing a blue cloth for the toilet seat out of habit, plus storing mixed colors together. Supervisors check which cloth went to which zone and may spot-check with swab tests. Interviews often ask "why must toilet cloths be dedicated?" — answer with cross-contamination and bacteria transfer.

📍 应用场景：客房服务员、公共卫生（Public Health / Sanitation）日常清洁与 USPH 检查准备。

开床服务Turndown Service

深度解析
  • 【是什么】开床服务（Turndown Service）是晚间入睡前提供的迷你整理服务，源于把被角「翻下来」（turn down the covers）这一动作，是邮轮与高端酒店区分服务档次的标志性环节，通常由客房服务员在 17:30–20:00 完成。
  • 【怎么做】标准动作：翻开上层被角约 30 cm（折角可做小造型）、收走用过的杯具与垃圾、清点并补齐毛巾（按客舱定员标准）、放拖鞋与晚安卡、放置次日活动日程表（Next Day's Program）、调暗或关闭多余灯光、制作毛巾艺术；每舱用时约 5–7 分钟，单班每人负责 12–18 间舱时需按楼层顺序推进。
  • 【易错点】错误：宾客在舱内时直接开铺（应先礼貌确认是否方便）、挂 DND 牌时强行服务（应在门缝留卡、次日补做）、忘收晚饭叫餐托盘、毛巾数量与定员不符。检查要点：晚安卡与日程表必须当日更新；面试常问「宾客挂 DND 怎么办」——答案是留下留言卡并上报主管，绝不敲门打扰。
Deep Dive
  • What: Turndown Service is the evening refresh performed before guests turn in — named after literally turning down the top of the covers. It is a signature touch that separates cruise/high-end hotel service tiers, typically completed between 17:30 and 20:00 by cabin stewards.
  • How: Standard routine: fold back the top covers about 30 cm, clear used glassware and trash, count and replenish towels to the cabin-occupancy standard, place slippers and a good-night card, set out the Next Day's Program, dim or switch off extra lights, and create towel art. Allow 5–7 minutes per cabin; with 12–18 cabins per steward per section, you work the deck in a fixed order.
  • Watch out: Never start turndown while guests are inside without politely checking first; never force service on a DND cabin — leave a card in the door slot and return next day; don't forget dinner tray collection, and match towel counts to occupancy. The good-night card and program must be current every evening. Interviewers love "what do you do about a DND sign?" — answer: leave a note and report to your supervisor, never knock.

📍 应用场景：客房服务员每日 17:30–20:00 晚班流程，尤其主餐厅晚市高峰时段的错峰安排。

清洁剂安全Chemical Safety / SDS

深度解析
  • 【是什么】清洁剂安全（Chemical Safety）要求所有清洁化学品按其化学品安全说明书（Safety Data Sheet，SDS，旧称 MSDS）规定的稀释比例、用途与储存方式使用，是邮轮职业安全（US OSHA 类规范与船旗国要求）与公共卫生（USPH/VSP）检查的重点。
  • 【怎么做】使用前「三查」：查标签（label）、查浓度（dilution ratio，如消毒剂常见 1:64 或 1:256，按产品而定）、查有效期；稀释用冷/温水并用量杯或内置配比器（dispenser）计量；全程佩戴手套等 PPE，必要时加护目镜；含氯（chlorine）与酸性（acid，如洁厕剂）药剂严禁混用——混合会释放有毒氯气（toxic chlorine gas）；化学品单独上锁存放、容器贴原标签，空瓶不得 repurpose 装其他液体。
  • 【易错点】错误：凭手感「多倒一点」超浓（腐蚀表面且留药残）、两种药剂倒进同一个桶、无标签瓶拿去装别的清洁剂、手套破洞继续作业。检查要点：PH 官员会现场问「这种药的稀释比例和洗好了怎么处理」；面试常考「洁厕剂和漂白水能不能混」——必须答不能，并说明会产生毒气。
Deep Dive
  • What: Chemical Safety means using every cleaning chemical strictly per its Safety Data Sheet (SDS, formerly MSDS) — dilution ratios, intended use, and storage. It is a key focus of both crew safety regulations (OSHA-style and flag-state requirements) and public health inspections (USPH/VSP).
  • How: Run the "three checks" before use: label, dilution ratio (sanitizers are commonly mixed at 1:64 or 1:256, depending on the product), and expiry date. Dilute with cold or lukewarm water, measured by cup or built-in dispenser; wear gloves and other PPE at all times, plus goggles where needed. Never mix chlorine-based and acid-based products (e.g., toilet bowl cleaner) — the reaction releases toxic chlorine gas. Store chemicals locked and separately, keep original labels on, and never repurpose empty bottles for other liquids.
  • Watch out: Classic errors are "eyeballing" an extra pour (corrodes surfaces and leaves residue), combining two products in one bucket, storing unlabeled mystery bottles, and working with torn gloves. Inspectors will ask on the spot for the dilution ratio and disposal method. The interview staple: "Can you mix bleach and toilet cleaner?" — the answer is a firm no, because it releases toxic gas.

📍 应用场景：客房服务员与公共区域清洁岗的每日配药操作、USPH/VSP 卫生检查迎检。

客舱状态表Stateroom Report

深度解析
  • 【是什么】客舱状态表（Stateroom Report，也称 Housekeeping Report）是客房部与前台每日核对的客舱状态总表，统一使用行业通用状态码，是排班、清洁优先级与可售房管理的唯一依据。
  • 【怎么做】核心状态码：Clean/Ready（已清洁可售）、Occupied（在住）、Departed/Check-out（已退房待清扫）、Out of Order（OOO，维修停用，不可售）、Out of Service（OOS，临时停用，可快速恢复）、Do Not Disturb（DND，免打扰不入内）；清洁顺序按优先级：Departed 舱 → Arriving/VIP 舱 → Occupied 舱；换客日（turnover day）全部 departed 舱须在宾客登船前完成，通常每人 14–20 间、限时 5–7 小时内清完。
  • 【易错点】错误：把 OOO 与 OOS 混用（OOO 影响可售统计）、DND 舱未经主管授权开门、状态未及时更新导致前台把脏房卖给宾客。检查要点：每晚与前台核对一次状态表，次日一早执行；面试常问「优先打扫哪种房间」——答 Departed 优先、VIP 其次，并说明理由（登船高峰时限）。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Stateroom Report (also called the Housekeeping Report) is the daily cabin-status master list reconciled between Housekeeping and the Front Desk. It uses industry-standard status codes and drives staffing, cleaning priority, and sellable-room management.
  • How: Core codes: Clean/Ready, Occupied, Departed/Check-out (vacated, awaiting service), Out of Order (OOO — maintenance, not sellable), Out of Service (OOS — temporarily blocked, quick to restore), and Do Not Disturb (DND). Cleaning priority runs Departed cabins first, then arriving/VIP cabins, then occupied cabins. On turnover day every departed cabin must be finished before guests board — typically 14–20 cabins per steward within a 5–7 hour window.
  • Watch out: Don't confuse OOO with OOS (it skews sellable-room counts), never enter a DND cabin without supervisor approval, and update statuses in real time or the Front Desk may sell a dirty room. Reconcile the report with the Front Desk each evening and act on it first thing. Interview favorite: "Which rooms do you clean first?" — Departed, then VIP, and explain the embarkation-day time pressure.

📍 应用场景：客房部与前台每日交接核对、换客日清洁排班与房态管理。

前台宾客服务部6 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

船卡系统Cruise Card / Sail & Sign Card

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船卡（Cruise Card）是宾客在船上的三合一身份卡：身份证件凭证、舱房钥匙、船上消费账户载体，各船司品牌名不同——Carnival 叫 Sail & Sign Card，Royal Caribbean 叫 SeaPass，MSC 叫 Cruise Card，英文名统称 Cruise Card / Guest Key Card。
  • 【怎么做】登船时在 Embarkation 柜台核验证件后制卡，卡片绑定 Onboard Account，全船酒吧、餐厅、商店、Spa 刷卡挂账、离船统一结算；门锁读取通常为 RFID 或磁条，制卡机每分钟可出卡约 30–60 张；宾客报失时流程是：先在前台挂失冻结（deactivate）→ 核对身份与 folio → 补制新卡（re-issue），全程约 2–5 分钟，绝不能先补卡再挂失。
  • 【易错点】错误：未挂失直接补新卡（旧卡仍可开舱门与消费，安全风险）、把卡号/舱号大声复述给大堂里的他人听到（隐私）、给非登记宾客开门。检查要点：开门前核对姓名+舱号+照片（如有）；面试常问「宾客丢了卡怎么办」——标准答案是先冻结旧卡再制新卡并重新验证身份。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Cruise Card is the guest's three-in-one onboard identity card — proof of identity, cabin key, and the vehicle for the onboard expense account. Brand names vary: Carnival's Sail & Sign Card, Royal Caribbean's SeaPass, MSC's Cruise Card; generically, Cruise Card or Guest Key Card.
  • How: Cards are issued at the Embarkation desk after document verification and linked to the guest's Onboard Account; all bars, dining, shops, and spa charges post to the card and settle at disembarkation. Door locks read RFID or magnetic stripes, and card printers turn out roughly 30–60 cards per minute. When a guest reports a lost card: deactivate the old card first, verify identity and folio, then re-issue — about 2–5 minutes end to end. Never re-issue before freezing the old card.
  • Watch out: Re-issuing without deactivating the old card leaves a working key and charging card in someone else's hands; avoid repeating cabin numbers aloud in a busy atrium (privacy); never open a door for a guest who isn't registered to that cabin. Before granting entry, verify name + cabin number (+ photo where shown). Interview staple: "A guest lost their card" — answer: freeze the old card, re-verify identity, then issue a new one.

📍 应用场景：前台宾客服务部（Guest Services）登船制卡、挂失补办、舱门纠纷与身份核验。

船载账户Onboard Account / Guest Folio

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船载账户（Onboard Account）是为每位客舱设立的船上消费总账，其账单即 Guest Folio，英文名 Onboard Account / Guest Folio；餐饮酒水、免税店、Spa、岸上观光、洗衣等全部挂账，功能相当于酒店的 city ledger。
  • 【怎么做】登船时以信用卡预授权或现金存款开户；系统每晚自动对账（night audit），消费明细实时可查；离船前一晚（Disembarkation Eve）逐舱发放 final statement，宾客如对账单有异议须在指定窗口期内（通常离船前夜至结账柜台关闭前，各船司不同）提出复核，前台须会调取单笔消费的签字小票（signed receipt）核对。
  • 【易错点】错误：把他人消费错挂到同舱宾客账户（同舱账户共用但需区分持卡人）、离船日拒收争议而不留记录、忘记向宾客解释 15–18% 自动服务费。检查要点：每晚核对 folio 与 POS 流水差异并当日处理；面试常问「宾客说有笔消费不是他的」——答：调取签单与消费时间地点核实，无法核实则按船司规程冲销或上报。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Onboard Account is the running expense ledger set up for each cabin; its printed bill is the Guest Folio. Bar and dining charges, duty-free shops, spa, shore excursions, and laundry all post to it — the cruise equivalent of a hotel's city ledger.
  • How: The account opens at embarkation with a credit-card pre-authorization or a cash deposit; the system runs an automatic night audit, and itemized charges are viewable in real time. On Disembarkation Eve, a final statement is delivered to every cabin. Billing disputes must be raised within the line's review window (usually that evening until the settlement desk closes), and agents must be able to pull the signed receipt for any charge.
  • Watch out: Posting one guest's charge to the wrong cabinmate, brushing off disputes on disembarkation day without logging them, and forgetting to explain the 15–18% automatic service charge are common errors. Reconcile folio against POS slips nightly and resolve variances same-day. Interview favorite: "The guest says a charge isn't theirs" — pull the signed receipt with time and venue; if it can't be substantiated, follow the line's procedure to credit or escalate.

📍 应用场景：前台宾客服务部开户、对账、账单争议处理与离船结算（Disembarkation Eve 与离船日）。

预授权押金Pre-authorization (Pre-auth)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】预授权（Pre-authorization，简称 Pre-auth）是登船时在宾客信用卡上做的临时冻结额度，作为船上消费担保，英文名 Pre-authorization；以现金形式存入的等额担保则称 cash deposit；冻结（hold）不是扣款（charge）是必须向宾客讲清的核心概念。
  • 【怎么做】常见冻结额度 US$100–200/航程（以船司为准），现金存款常见 US$300 起；操作：POS 输入预授权金额→打印凭条→宾客签字→存档；离船结账时实际消费金额转为正常扣款，未用余额的预授权自动解除，银行释放冻结通常需 3–7 个工作日（视发卡行）；前台须能解释「为什么我的账单只有 X 美元却冻结了更多」。
  • 【易错点】错误：把预授权说成「已扣款」引发投诉、忘记告知宾客冻结释放需数个工作日、宾客用借记卡（debit card）时未提示银行可能延长冻结期。检查要点：凭条编号与 folio 关联存档可追溯；面试常问「客人质问为什么多冻结了钱」——答：解释 hold vs. charge、给出发卡行释放时间区间、必要时提供预授权凭条副本。
Deep Dive
  • What: A Pre-authorization (pre-auth) is a temporary hold placed on the guest's credit card at embarkation as security for onboard spending; the cash equivalent is a cash deposit. The key concept guests must understand: a hold is not a charge.
  • How: Typical hold amounts run US$100–200 per voyage (line-specific); cash deposits commonly start around US$300. Procedure: enter the pre-auth amount on the POS, print and file the signed slip. At disembarkation the actual charges convert to a normal payment and the unused hold releases automatically — though the guest's bank may take 3–7 business days to free the funds. Agents must be able to explain "why was more held than my bill?"
  • Watch out: Calling the pre-auth a "charge" triggers complaints; forgetting to mention the multi-day bank release confuses guests; debit-card users need a heads-up that some banks extend holds. File each slip traceably against the folio. Interview favorite: "Why did you hold extra money?" — explain hold vs. charge, quote the release timeframe, and offer a copy of the pre-auth slip.

📍 应用场景：前台宾客服务部登船开户、离船结算与账单疑问解释。

应急集合站Muster Station

深度解析
  • 【是什么】集合站（Muster Station）是 SOLAS（《国际海上人命安全公约》）强制规定的应急集合点，每位宾客与船员都有指定站点；英文名 Muster Station，相关演习称 Muster Drill / Safety Drill；「登船 24 小时内完成集合演习」是 SOLAS 硬性要求（现代邮轮多在离港前完成）。
  • 【怎么做】前台员工必须背记自己负责区域内所有客舱对应的集合站编号与行走路线（船卡与舱门图上均有标注）；演习时提前 15 分钟到位，穿好救生衣（lifejacket）、佩戴职责卡；紧急真实状态下职责：按应急部署表（Muster List）引导宾客沿疏散标识（exit signs）至集合站、协助清点人数（headcount）并上报；广播术语如 "Attention all guests, please proceed to your muster stations."
  • 【易错点】错误：答不出随机一位宾客的集合站位置（面试与实际检查均会抽问）、演习时让宾客自己找路而不引导、忘了带宾客名单（guest list）去清点。检查要点：每航次演习亲自走一遍路线；面试必问「你的 Muster Station 在哪、走哪条路」——必须脱口而出。
Deep Dive
  • What: A Muster Station is the emergency assembly point required by SOLAS (the International Convention for the Safety of Life at Sea). Every guest and crew member has an assigned station; the related drill is the Muster Drill, and completing it within 24 hours of embarkation is a SOLAS requirement (modern ships usually complete it before departure).
  • How: Front-desk staff must memorize the muster station number and walking route for every cabin in their assigned zone (marked on cruise cards and cabin-door plans). Arrive 15 minutes early for drills in a lifejacket with your duty card. In a real emergency, follow the Muster List: guide guests along exit signage to their station, assist with headcount, and report. Standard announcement: "Attention all guests, please proceed to your muster stations."
  • Watch out: Not knowing a random guest's muster station (interviewers and inspectors will spot-check), letting guests find their own way during drills, and forgetting the guest list for headcount. Walk your route personally every voyage. The guaranteed interview question: "Where is your muster station and how do you get there?" — answer instantly.

📍 应用场景：前台宾客服务部登船日安全演习引导、应急部署与日常宾客咨询。

登船/离船流程Embarkation / Disembarkation

深度解析
  • 【是什么】登船（Embarkation）与离船（Disembarkation）是航次首尾的两个全船大流量日，也是前台宾客服务部（Guest Services）工作量峰值日，英文名合称 Embarkation/Disembarkation，港口作业则称 turnaround day。
  • 【怎么做】登链条：证件核验（护照+签证/健康申报）→ 安检 → 制卡绑定账户 → 安全演习；离链条：前一晚发放行李牌（luggage tag）按颜色/数字分批 → 宾客行李放门外 → 按组下船（debark by group）→ 海关申报 → 结清 folio；高峰日一名前台每天服务 150–300 名宾客，单笔事务应控制在 2–3 分钟内；离船日重点防「漏乘」（no-show at gangway）——名单不符时广播寻人并上报。
  • 【易错点】错误：证件看错有效期或签证页、行李牌颜色发错导致行李滞留、离船日未核对下船名单就放行。检查要点：登船日在岗前先过一遍当日 to-do（账户、演习、特殊需求宾客）；面试常问「离船日最怕什么」——答漏乘与 folio 未结清，并说明防止措施。
Deep Dive
  • What: Embarkation and Disembarkation are the two highest-traffic days of any voyage — the front-loaded and back-loaded peaks for Guest Services. Combined with port operations, the whole day is called a turnaround day.
  • How: Embarkation chain: document verification (passport, visas, health forms) → security screening → card issuance linked to the onboard account → muster drill. Disembarkation chain: color/number-coded luggage tags issued the night before → bags placed outside cabins → guests debark by group → customs declaration → folio settled. On peak days one agent serves 150–300 guests, so keep each transaction within 2–3 minutes. The critical disembarkation risk is a no-show at the gangway — page the guest and escalate immediately if the manifest doesn't match.
  • Watch out: Misreading passport expiry or visa pages, issuing the wrong luggage-tag color (stranded bags), and clearing guests without checking the debarkation manifest. On embarkation morning, review the day's to-dos first: accounts, drill, guests with special needs. Interview favorite: "What worries you most on disembarkation day?" — no-shows and unsettled folios, plus your prevention steps.

📍 应用场景：前台宾客服务部航次首日与末日全流程，含证件核验、制卡、行李与下船名单管理。

服务补救Service Recovery / LEARN Model

深度解析
  • 【是什么】服务补救（Service Recovery）是投诉发生后挽回宾客信任的系统方法，行业通用模型为 LEARN：Listen 倾听、Empathize 共情、Apologize 致歉、Resolve 解决、Notify/Next 跟进，英文名 Service Recovery / LEARN Model，是前台与所有对客岗位的必修课。
  • 【怎么做】五步实操：停下手中事、身体前倾听完整（不打断）；用 "I understand how frustrating…" 共情；真诚致歉（不推责船司/部门）；在授权范围内立即解决——一线通常有即时补救授权如果盘（comped dessert）、免房账小额冲销（约 US$25–50，以船司为准）；24 小时内回访确认并记录到宾客档案（guest profile），重大补偿（退款、换舱）升级至 Guest Services Manager。
  • 【易错点】错误：只道歉不解决（宾客二次投诉）、超出授权随意承诺补偿、书面记录缺失导致同舱投诉重复处理。检查要点：每起投诉当班内闭环或在系统中移交；面试必问「宾客对菜品非常愤怒你怎么办」——按 LEARN 顺序完整演示并给出你的授权边界。
Deep Dive
  • What: Service Recovery is the systematic way to win back guest trust after a failure. The industry-standard model is LEARN: Listen, Empathize, Apologize, Resolve, Notify/Next — a core competency for Guest Services and every guest-facing role.
  • How: Stop what you're doing and let the guest finish without interrupting; empathize with lines like "I understand how frustrating that must be…"; apologize sincerely without blaming another department; resolve immediately within your authority — front-line staff typically carry instant recovery powers such as a comped dessert or a small folio credit (around US$25–50, line-dependent). Follow up within 24 hours, log it in the guest profile, and escalate major remedies (refunds, cabin changes) to the Guest Services Manager.
  • Watch out: Apologizing without fixing (invites a second complaint), promising compensation beyond your authority, and skipping the written log so the same issue gets handled twice. Close every complaint within your shift or hand it over in the system. The guaranteed interview question: "A guest is furious about a dish" — walk through LEARN in order and state your authorization limits.

📍 应用场景：前台宾客服务部投诉柜台与全船对客岗位的通用补救流程。

餐饮部21 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

摆台Cover / Table Setting

深度解析
  • 【是什么】摆台指按标准把餐具、杯具、口布布置到每一个餐位（cover），是餐厅每轮开餐前的基础作业，也是面试实操高频考点。
  • 【怎么做】刀叉按上菜顺序由外向内摆放；餐刀刃口朝内朝盘；面包碟置于餐叉左上方，水杯在刀刃右上方约 1–2 cm 处；杯具一律持杯柄摆放；口布折花居中或置于餐盘上，桌布中线对齐桌沿。正式晚宴一 cover 距桌沿约 2.5 cm（一指宽）。
  • 【易错点】刀叉方向反、左右两侧不对称、忘记摆备餐具（配套 knife & fork on bread plate 边上的 side plate）——开餐前领班会按 cover 逐位巡检，缺一件即返工。
Deep Dive
  • What: A cover is one complete place setting. Setting covers correctly before each meal service is the foundation of restaurant work and a common practical-interview topic.
  • How: Silverware goes outside-in in the order courses are served, knife edges facing the plate; bread plate sits upper-left of the forks, water glass upper-right of the knives about 1–2 cm above them; handle glassware by the stem; fold napkins at the center or on the plate; tablecloth centerline flush with the table edge. In formal dining a cover sits roughly 2.5 cm (one finger) from the table edge.
  • Watch out: Wrong fork/knife orientation, uneven left-right spacing, or a missing side piece — supervisors inspect every cover before service, and one missing item means redoing the whole setting.

📍 应用场景：餐厅服务生（Waiter/Assistant Waiter）每轮开餐前与摆台检查环节；面试实操常考。

酒水单Wine List / Beverage Menu

深度解析
  • 【是什么】餐厅酒水单通常按红葡萄酒、白葡萄酒、起泡酒、啤酒、烈酒、鸡尾酒与软饮分区，是二次推介与配餐建议的依据。
  • 【怎么做】必须熟记单上每款的产区、品种、价格档位与配餐建议；点菜后先做酒水推介（wine pairing），如牛排推 Cabernet Sauvignon、海鲜推 Chardonnay 或 Pinot Grigio；船上有按杯售（by the glass）与整瓶售（by the bottle）两种口径，报价前确认清楚。
  • 【易错点】宾客问「推荐一款配龙虾的白葡萄酒」时答不上来，或把 by the glass 价格报成整瓶价——酒水单不熟 = immediate red flag。
Deep Dive
  • What: The wine list is typically organized by reds, whites, sparkling, beer, spirits, cocktails and soft drinks — the basis for pairing suggestions and upselling.
  • How: Memorize each item's region, grape, price tier and pairing; offer wine pairing right after taking the food order (steak → Cabernet Sauvignon; seafood → Chardonnay or Pinot Grigio). Drinks are sold by the glass or by the bottle — confirm which price you are quoting.
  • Watch out: Being unable to recommend a white wine for lobster, or quoting the bottle price for a glass — not knowing the wine list is an instant red flag.

📍 应用场景：餐厅服务生点单与推介环节；酒吧调酒员也需掌握酒单全貌。

二次推介Upselling / Suggestive Selling

深度解析
  • 【是什么】在宾客点单基础上主动推荐更优选择与加购项（推荐酒款、餐后甜点、精品咖啡、特色饮品），是餐饮岗位的核心业绩指标。
  • 【怎么做】时机：点单时推酒、主菜后推甜品、结账前推咖啡；话术用选择问句而非是非问句——"Would you care for a glass of the Prosecco or perhaps a cocktail to start?"；每条推荐都要带一个具体理由（配餐/当天特色/today's special）。
  • 【易错点】机械背诵式硬推、一次推三个项目、忽略过敏与忌口就推酒——面试会问 "How do you upsell without being pushy?"，标准答案是 recommendation + reason + choice。
Deep Dive
  • What: Suggestive selling means recommending upgrades and add-ons (wine, dessert, specialty coffee) on top of the guest's order — a core performance metric for F&B roles.
  • How: Pitch wine while taking the order, dessert after the main course, coffee before the check; use choice questions instead of yes/no questions ("Would you care for a glass of the Prosecco or perhaps a cocktail to start?"); every recommendation needs a reason (pairing, today's special).
  • Watch out: Robotic hard selling, pitching three items at once, or ignoring allergies — interviews ask "How do you upsell without being pushy?"; the answer is recommendation + reason + choice.

📍 应用场景：餐厅服务生、酒吧服务生日常点单全程；直接影响小费与绩效评估。

翻台Turnover / Table Reset

深度解析
  • 【是什么】两轮用餐之间在限定时间内完成清台、消毒与重新摆台，保证下一轮宾客准时就座；主餐厅两轮（seatings）间隔通常只有 45–60 分钟。
  • 【怎么做】流程：收走全部脏餐具 → 撤桌布 → 桌面椅面消毒（sanitize）→ 换新桌布 → 按标准重新摆台 → 领班巡检；团队分工（一人清、一人摆）比单打独斗快一倍；脏餐具按类别分筐送洗碗间。
  • 【易错点】只擦不消毒、漏换烟灰缸/盐胡椒罐位、摆台漏项；高峰期抢速度牺牲质量是新人最大扣分点。
Deep Dive
  • What: Turnover is clearing, sanitizing and resetting tables within a tight window between two seatings — main dining room seatings are usually only 45–60 minutes apart.
  • How: Clear all soiled items → strip the tablecloth → sanitize table and chairs → re-cloth → set covers to standard → supervisor check. Team splits (one clears, one sets) roughly double the speed; sort dirty dishes into racks by type for the wash-up.
  • Watch out: Wiping without sanitizing, missing ashtray/salt-and-pepper spots, incomplete covers — sacrificing quality for speed at peak time is the most common new-hire deduction.

📍 应用场景：主餐厅两轮正餐（first/second seating）之间与自助餐厅高峰换座时段。

备餐台Side Station / Bus Stand

深度解析
  • 【是什么】服务生负责区段（station）内的移动服务台，存放备用餐具、口布、面包篮、调味品与冰水，让补货不必往返洗碗间。
  • 【怎么做】开餐前按配额（par stock）补齐：每 cover 备用刀叉 2–3 套、口布 5–10 条、面包篮 2 个、糖包奶包满格；台面分区固定摆放，脏餐具暂存筐与净具隔离；服务中随用随补，绝不空台接客。
  • 【易错点】开餐时才去补货、净脏混放、忘记冰水壶续冰——领班检查 station 时第一件事就是查 side station 配额。
Deep Dive
  • What: The side station is the rolling service stand in your station where backup cutlery, napkins, bread baskets, condiments and ice water are stocked, so you don't run to the wash-up mid-service.
  • How: Stock to par stock before service: 2–3 sets of backup silverware per cover, 5–10 napkins, 2 bread baskets, full sugar and creamer caddies; fixed zones for each item, soiled rack kept separate from clean stock; restock continuously during service.
  • Watch out: Restocking only after service starts, clean and soiled items mixed, or forgetting to top up ice water — the first thing a supervisor checks on a station is the side station par levels.

📍 应用场景：餐厅服务生每轮开餐前的 station 准备与服务中补货。

客账签单Guest Check / Signature Required

深度解析
  • 【是什么】邮轮上餐饮消费记入宾客海务卡（cruise card / key card）账户，服务生出单（print the check）后需宾客本人在账单上签字确认，账单随后入船账系统结算。
  • 【怎么做】送单时双手递单、笔尖朝自己；核对桌号、人数、品项与 POS 一致；宾客签完即刻收回并核对签名与卡面姓名相符；加收服务费按船司比例（通常 15%–18%）已在单上列明，需口头说明。
  • 【易错点】让宾客之间互相代签、忘记核对签名、收错单据夹；签错单直接影响对账，累计错账会写进绩效与处分记录。
Deep Dive
  • What: On board, F&B charges go to the guest's cruise card account; the server prints the check and the guest must sign it personally before the bill posts to the shipboard account.
  • How: Present the check holder with both hands, pen facing yourself; verify table number, covers and items against the POS; after signing, collect immediately and match the signature to the name on the card; the service charge (usually 15%–18%, varies by line) is pre-printed — mention it verbally.
  • Watch out: Letting one guest sign for another, skipping the signature check, or losing a check holder — mis-signed checks break the daily audit and count against your performance record.

📍 应用场景：餐厅/酒吧每桌消费结算环节；收费酒水与特色餐厅尤其严格。

常见葡萄品种Grape Varieties

深度解析
  • 【是什么】餐饮服务必须掌握的入门葡萄品种：红——Cabernet Sauvignon（赤霞珠）、Merlot（梅洛）、Pinot Noir（黑皮诺）；白——Chardonnay（霞多丽）、Sauvignon Blanc（长相思）、Pinot Grigio（灰皮诺）；起泡——Prosecco、Champagne。
  • 【怎么做】按「品种→风格→配餐」三段式记忆：赤霞珠酒体饱满单宁重配牛排羊排；黑皮诺酒体轻、果香细，配三文鱼与鸭肉；霞多丽中等偏饱满，配禽类与奶油类菜；长相思清爽高酸配海鲜沙拉；灰皮诺干型轻体配开胃小食；普罗塞克作开胃酒或配甜点。
  • 【易错点】把品种和产区混为一谈（Chablis 是产区、Chardonnay 是品种）；面试最常问 "What would you pair with steak?"——必须 3 秒内给出品种+理由。
Deep Dive
  • What: The starter set of grape varieties for F&B: reds — Cabernet Sauvignon, Merlot, Pinot Noir; whites — Chardonnay, Sauvignon Blanc, Pinot Grigio; sparkling — Prosecco, Champagne.
  • How: Learn each as variety → style → pairing: full-bodied, tannic Cabernet with steak and lamb; light, aromatic Pinot Noir with salmon or duck; medium-to-full Chardonnay with poultry and cream sauces; crisp, high-acid Sauvignon Blanc with seafood and salads; dry, light Pinot Grigio with appetizers; Prosecco as an aperitif or with dessert.
  • Watch out: Confusing region with grape (Chablis is a region, Chardonnay a grape); the classic interview question is "What would you pair with steak?" — you must answer variety + reason within three seconds.

📍 应用场景：餐厅服务生酒水推介、酒类主管（Sommelier）配餐建议；面试必考。

酒液侍温Service Temperature

深度解析
  • 【是什么】每类葡萄酒都有标准出品温度：红酒 16–18°C、白酒 8–12°C、起泡酒 6–8°C；侍温错误是最常见也最容易避免的服务事故。
  • 【怎么做】出酒前核对酒柜温度（红低于 18°C 时避免室温直出）；白酒与起泡酒提前入冰桶（ice bucket，水加冰约 15 分钟可达适饮温度）；起泡酒全程留置冰桶内；白酒温度过低香气封闭，可稍回温再侍。
  • 【易错点】红酒室温直出（夏季船舱可达 25°C+）、白酒冻过头（4°C 以下香气全无）、起泡酒放常温致开瓶喷溅；面试问 "At what temperature do you serve red wine?" 必须答出区间。
Deep Dive
  • What: Every wine style has a standard service temperature: reds 16–18°C, whites 8–12°C, sparkling 6–8°C; wrong temperature is the most common — and easiest to avoid — service fault.
  • How: Check the wine cabinet before service; chill whites and sparkling in an ice bucket (water + ice, about 15 minutes to reach drinking temperature); keep sparkling on ice throughout; whites served too cold close up — let them warm slightly before pouring.
  • Watch out: Serving reds at room temperature (ship cabins can exceed 25°C), over-chilling whites below 4°C (aromas vanish), or leaving sparkling warm so the cork flies — "At what temperature do you serve red wine?" is a standard interview question.

📍 应用场景：餐厅服务生与酒吧出酒环节；特色餐厅（Specialty Dining）出酒前必查。

开瓶与醒酒Opening & Decanting

深度解析
  • 【是什么】葡萄酒的标准开瓶服务流程与陈年红酒的换瓶醒酒操作，是判断服务生专业度的经典场面。
  • 【怎么做】开瓶：切箔→螺旋钻居中旋入约 6 圈→轻拔瓶塞→用口布擦净瓶口；先向点酒的 host 呈示酒标（present the label）确认酒款；倒约一盎司（30 ml）请 host 试酒（tasting pour），认可后按座次从右侧顺时针斟酒，红斟至杯腹约 1/3、白酒 1/2、起泡酒 3/4 至杯身留气。醒酒（decant）：陈年红酒缓慢一次性倒入醒酒器，分离瓶底沉淀，静置 15–30 分钟让香气打开。
  • 【易错点】瓶塞拔出「砰」一声、不呈示酒标直接倒、忘记先请 host 试酒就给全桌斟；面试会考口述整套 wine service 流程。
Deep Dive
  • What: The standard still-wine opening service and decanting of aged reds — the classic test of a server's professionalism.
  • How: Cut the foil → center the worm and twist in about six turns → ease out the cork → wipe the rim with a napkin; present the label to the host first; pour a one-ounce tasting pour for the host to approve, then pour clockwise from the right — red about one-third of the bowl, white half, sparkling three-quarters. To decant an aged red, pour slowly in one motion to separate the sediment and let it breathe 15–30 minutes.
  • Watch out: A loud "pop" from the cork, skipping the label presentation, or pouring the whole table before the host's tasting pour — interviews often ask you to talk through the full wine service sequence.

📍 应用场景：特色餐厅与主餐厅整瓶酒服务；Som­melier 与 Waiter 面试实操重点。

葡萄酒杯型Glassware

深度解析
  • 【是什么】不同酒款对应不同杯型：红酒用大肚宽碗杯聚拢香气并帮助醒酒；白酒用较小杯身保持低温；起泡酒用细长 flute 保住气泡；水杯最大且最先摆放。
  • 【怎么做】持杯一律捏杯柄（stem）或杯底（foot），指印留在杯身即返工；斟酒后转杯（twist）收口防滴漏；上桌时杯柄朝宾客右前方；回收时按杯型分筐，防碰碎。
  • 【易错点】红酒倒满杯、拿杯子拿杯身、用 flute 上红酒；摆台时杯口朝下搭在桌上——这些细节领班一眼即见。
Deep Dive
  • What: Each wine style has its glass: reds in a large-bowl glass to concentrate aromas and help the wine breathe; whites in a smaller bowl to stay cool; sparkling in a narrow flute to preserve the bubbles; the water glass is the largest and sits first.
  • How: Always hold by the stem or foot — fingerprints on the bowl mean redoing it; give a slight twist after pouring to prevent drips; set glasses handle-end toward the guest's upper right; sort glasses by type into racks when clearing.
  • Watch out: Filling a red glass to the rim, grabbing the bowl, or serving red wine in a flute — and never stack glasses mouth-down on the table; supervisors spot these instantly.

📍 应用场景：摆台、斟酒与收台全程；西餐厅与酒吧通用基本功。

啤酒与烈酒基础Beer & Spirits

深度解析
  • 【是什么】餐饮与酒吧岗位的酒精饮品两大板块：啤酒按发酵方式与风格分类，烈酒按原料与蒸馏方式分类，是酒水推介的底层知识。
  • 【怎么做】啤酒：Lager（拉格，清爽底发酵）、Pale Ale/IPA（麦香带苦度，上发酵）、Stout（黑啤，烘烤咖啡香）、Wheat Beer（小麦啤，果香）；杯装通常冷饮，倒酒留约两指泡沫。烈酒六大类：Vodka、Gin、Rum、Whisky、Tequila、Brandy，可纯饮（neat）、加冰（on the rocks）或作基酒；Gin 带杜松子香、Tequila 由龙舌兰制成——说错原料 = instant red flag。
  • 【易错点】把 Stout 说成 ale 家族、不知道 Gin 的核心是 juniper（杜松子）、整杯啤酒倒满不留泡沫头（head）。
Deep Dive
  • What: The two backbone categories of alcoholic beverages for F&B and bar roles: beers classified by fermentation and style, spirits by raw material and distillation.
  • How: Beer — Lager (crisp, bottom-fermented), Pale Ale/IPA (malty with hop bitterness, top-fermented), Stout (dark, roasty coffee notes), Wheat Beer (fruity); serve chilled and leave about two fingers of head. Spirits — the six majors are Vodka, Gin, Rum, Whisky, Tequila, Brandy, served neat, on the rocks or as a cocktail base; Gin is juniper-led and Tequila is made from agave — wrong terminology is an instant red flag.
  • Watch out: Calling a Stout an ale, not knowing Gin's core botanical is juniper, or pouring beer with no foam head.

📍 应用场景：酒吧服务生（Bar Waiter）与调酒员（Bartender）推介环节；Waiter 面试也会问。

鸡尾酒基酒Cocktail Base Spirits

深度解析
  • 【是什么】六大基酒构成几乎所有经典鸡尾酒的骨架，宾客点单时第一反应就是「基酒是什么」。
  • 【怎么做】背「基酒→代表作」对：Vodka→Cosmopolitan / Bloody Mary；Gin→Gin & Tonic / Martini；Rum→Mojito / Piña Colada；Whisky→Whisky Sour / Old Fashioned；Tequila→Margarita；Brandy→Sidecar。宾客说「来点清爽的」可反推 Gin & Tonic 或 Mojito；宾客点代表作时能即时报出品名与基酒。
  • 【易错点】把 Mojito 记成 Gin 基、把 Margarita 记成 Vodka 基——经典题 "Which spirit is in a Margarita?" 答错即出局。
Deep Dive
  • What: The six base spirits form the skeleton of nearly every classic cocktail — a guest's order is first a question of "which base?"
  • How: Memorize base → signature drink: Vodka → Cosmopolitan / Bloody Mary; Gin → Gin & Tonic / Martini; Rum → Mojito / Piña Colada; Whisky → Whisky Sour / Old Fashioned; Tequila → Margarita; Brandy → Sidecar. When a guest asks for "something refreshing", translate that into a Gin & Tonic or a Mojito and name the base instantly.
  • Watch out: Calling Mojito a Gin drink or Margarita a Vodka drink — "Which spirit is in a Margarita?" is a classic screening question.

📍 应用场景：酒吧服务生点单转达与调酒员出品；面试酒水知识必考环节。

无酒精饮品Mocktails & Soft Drinks

深度解析
  • 【是什么】不含酒精的饮品体系：Mocktails（无酒精鸡尾酒，用果汁、糖浆、苏打与香草调制）与 Soft Drinks（汽水、鲜榨果汁、矿泉水 sparkling/still），是儿童、孕期宾客与戒酒宾客的接待重点。
  • 【怎么做】推介时把 Mocktail 当正式出品介绍（Virgin Mojito、Fruit Punch、Shirley Temple）；矿泉水必问 still or sparkling；备餐台常备冰块、柠檬片、吸管与糖浆；点单时确认是否含糖、儿童是否要无吸管杯。
  • 【易错点】对不喝酒的宾客只甩一句「我们只有可乐」、混淆 still 与 sparkling 的英文、Mocktail 里不小心加了含酒精的糖浆（有些风味糖浆含微量酒精）。
Deep Dive
  • What: The non-alcoholic lineup: mocktails (fruit juice, syrups, soda and herbs shaken like real cocktails) and soft drinks (sodas, fresh juices, mineral water in sparkling or still) — key for children, pregnant guests and non-drinkers.
  • How: Pitch mocktails as a proper product (Virgin Mojito, Fruit Punch, Shirley Temple); always ask "still or sparkling?" for mineral water; keep the side station stocked with ice, lemon wedges, straws and syrups; confirm sugar preferences and kids' cups when taking the order.
  • Watch out: Offering "just a Coke" to a non-drinking guest, mixing up still and sparkling, or accidentally adding an alcohol-based syrup to a mocktail.

📍 应用场景：全船酒吧与餐厅；家庭客群航次（family cruising）用量极大。

上酒顺序Service Order

深度解析
  • 【是什么】同桌多位宾客时斟酒、上菜的次序规范，直接体现服务教养，面试口述必考。
  • 【怎么做】次序：先宾后主（guests before the host）→ 女士优先 → 长者优先 → 最后 host；从宾客右侧上饮品上菜、左侧撤盘；多人同桌按顺时针逐一完成，不跳位不漏位；每上一份报菜名。
  • 【易错点】先给点酒的 host 倒满再给其他人、左右侧混乱上菜、顺时针转一半跳过儿童位——被宾客投诉最多的低级错误之一。
Deep Dive
  • What: The sequence for serving drinks and courses to a full table — a direct display of service etiquette and a must-know for interviews.
  • How: Serve guests before the host, ladies first, elders next, host last; serve drinks and food from the right, clear from the left; work clockwise around the table without skipping anyone; announce each dish as you set it down.
  • Watch out: Filling the host first, switching between left and right sides mid-table, or skipping a child's place while going clockwise — among the most commonly complained-about rookie mistakes.

📍 应用场景：主餐厅与特色餐厅每轮斟酒上菜；西式服务（Western service）基本功。

九大过敏源Major Allergens

深度解析
  • 【是什么】国际公认的九大致敏食物：奶（milk）、蛋（egg）、鱼（fish）、贝甲壳类（crustaceans & shellfish）、坚果（tree nuts）、花生（peanuts）、小麦/麸质（wheat/gluten）、大豆（soy）、芝麻（sesame）——任何一道菜的过敏源问题都以这九类为基准回答。
  • 【怎么做】熟记菜单每道菜的过敏源构成；宾客问询时若不确定，标准动作是查配方表（recipe/ingredient sheet）或问厨房，绝不凭印象猜；回答用肯定句而非模糊句——"This dish contains milk and gluten" 优于 "I think it's fine"。
  • 【易错点】把 shellfish 与 fish 混为一类、忽略「微痕」（may contain traces）风险、凭经验说「应该没有坚果」——面试必问 "What do you do if a guest asks about allergens and you are not sure?"，正确答案是 verify first, never guess。
Deep Dive
  • What: The nine internationally recognized major allergens: milk, egg, fish, crustaceans & shellfish, tree nuts, peanuts, wheat/gluten, soy and sesame — every menu-allergen answer is benchmarked against these nine.
  • How: Know the allergen profile of every dish on your menu; when unsure, check the recipe/ingredient sheet or ask the galley — never answer from memory; use definitive language: "This dish contains milk and gluten" beats "I think it's fine".
  • Watch out: Lumping shellfish into "fish", ignoring "may contain traces" risk, or saying "it should be nut-free" from experience — the interview classic is "What do you do if a guest asks about allergens and you are not sure?"; the answer is verify first, never guess.

📍 应用场景：点单、上菜与宾客问询全程；邮轮 F&B 培训与食品安全审计重点。

过敏标记Allergy Alert

深度解析
  • 【是什么】在 POS 点单系统给对应宾客账单打上的过敏警示标记，让厨房出品的每一个环节都能看到该餐位的过敏信息。
  • 【怎么做】点单时：宾客报过敏 → 在 POS 对应菜品打 allergy alert → 口头向宾客复述过敏源与对应菜品确认无误 → 与厨房交接时再次口头强调；上菜时明确告知哪一份是过敏餐。
  • 【易错点】只口头告诉厨房不打 POS 标记（换班即断档）、复述时漏掉严重程度（如 anaphylaxis 过敏性休克风险）、上菜时与普通餐混淆；面试问 "Walk me through an allergy order" 就考这一整条链路。
Deep Dive
  • What: An allergy alert flag placed on the guest's check in the POS system, so every step of the galley can see the allergen linked to that cover.
  • How: When the guest discloses an allergy → flag the item in the POS → repeat the allergen and dish back to the guest to confirm → brief the galley verbally as well; when serving, clearly identify which plate is the allergy-safe one.
  • Watch out: Telling the kitchen verbally but skipping the POS flag (the chain breaks at shift change), not capturing severity such as anaphylaxis risk, or mixing allergy plates with regular covers — "Walk me through an allergy order" tests this entire chain.

📍 应用场景：点单系统操作环节；与「与厨房确认」「专用器具」条目构成完整过敏链路。

菜单问询话术Allergy Inquiry Script

深度解析
  • 【是什么】点单开始时的标准开场问句，用于主动排查过敏与忌口，是国际邮轮点单 SOP 的第一句。
  • 【怎么做】标准句式："Do you have any food allergies or dietary restrictions?"；若宾客报出过敏，追问一句确认严重程度："Is it a severe allergy — could you tell me more about your allergy?"；儿童桌问家长不问孩子；单边耳朵/噪音环境放慢语速复述。
  • 【易错点】跳过这句直接点单、把过敏问句问成是非题带情绪（"Nothing I should worry about, right?"）、听到过敏后不追问严重程度就下单；面试英文自我介绍后最常追问的就是这句台词。
Deep Dive
  • What: The standard opening question at the start of every order, proactively screening for allergies and dietary needs — the first line of the international cruise-line order-taking SOP.
  • How: Say: "Do you have any food allergies or dietary restrictions?"; if the guest discloses one, confirm severity: "Is it a severe allergy — could you tell me more about your allergy?"; ask parents, not children, at family tables; slow down and repeat in noisy dining rooms.
  • Watch out: Skipping the question and going straight to the order, phrasing it as a leading yes/no ("Nothing I should worry about, right?"), or taking the order without clarifying severity — after your self-introduction, this exact line is the most-followed-up interview item.

📍 应用场景：餐厅/酒吧每次点单第一句；面试英文口语高频抽问。

与厨房确认Chef Confirmation

深度解析
  • 【是什么】过敏单在下单出餐前必须经厨师长（Chef / Head Chef）确认可安全出品的强制环节；服务生没有权限自行判断「应该没问题」。
  • 【怎么做】流程：POS 打标记 → 亲自到厨房向 Chef 口头说明桌号、餐位号、过敏源与菜品 → Chef 确认替代做法或否决 → 把 Chef 的答复回传宾客 → 上菜时由出餐口明确交接这是 allergy plate。
  • 【易错点】隔着吧台喊一嗓子就算确认、Chef 没回应就默认通过、宾客追问时服务生替厨房做安全承诺；这是食品安全红线，违反可直接导致处分（disciplinary action）。
Deep Dive
  • What: A mandatory step in which the Chef / Head Chef confirms an allergy order can be safely produced before it goes to the kitchen; servers have no authority to decide "it should be fine".
  • How: Flag it in the POS → go to the galley and tell the Chef the table number, cover number, allergen and dish → the Chef either approves a safe preparation or rejects it → relay the Chef's answer back to the guest → at the pass, confirm the plate is handed over as an allergy plate.
  • Watch out: Shouting across the line and calling it confirmed, treating no reply as approval, or personally guaranteeing safety to the guest — this is a food-safety red line that can lead to disciplinary action.

📍 应用场景：过敏单流转的核心环节；与 POS 标记、专用器具条目构成安全链路。

专用器具Dedicated Pans & Boards

深度解析
  • 【是什么】过敏餐使用单独锅具、砧板与器具（dedicated pans, boards and utensils），在独立区域制作，从硬件上隔离过敏源。
  • 【怎么做】厨房端：过敏单触发专用器具流程，蓝/紫砧板等色标体系区分用途；服务端：用专用托盘单独递送过敏餐，不与其他餐盘同托混送；交接时口头声明 "This is the allergy plate"。
  • 【易错点】服务生把过敏餐和普通餐叠放在同一托盘送出去——前厅这一下就毁掉厨房全部隔离努力；面试问 "How is an allergy meal handled?" 要能答到器具与递送两层。
Deep Dive
  • What: Allergy meals are prepared with dedicated pans, boards and utensils in a separate area, physically isolating the allergen from regular production.
  • How: Galley side — an allergy order triggers the dedicated-equipment routine, often with color-coded boards (blue/purple); service side — carry the allergy plate on its own tray, never stacked with other covers, and announce "This is the allergy plate" at handover.
  • Watch out: Servers stacking the allergy plate with regular dishes on one tray — front-of-house can undo all the galley's isolation in one move; "How is an allergy meal handled?" should be answered on both the equipment and delivery levels.

📍 应用场景：厨房出品环节（配合餐饮部执行）与托盘递送交接环节。

交叉接触Cross-contact

深度解析
  • 【是什么】Cross-contact 指过敏源微量沾染到其他食物或接触面（共用炸油、台面、刀具、手套），与微生物层面的 cross-contamination（交叉污染）是两个概念——用错术语在英文面试中等于暴露专业度不足。
  • 【怎么做】识别高风险点：共用炸锅炸油、同一烤架、共用搅拌机、篡改食材自选组合；应对：明确告知宾客 "We cannot guarantee zero cross-contact for fried items"（如实告知能力边界）、推动厨房用专用设备、点单时提醒宾客该类出品的风险。
  • 【易错点】把两个术语混用、以为「高温烹炸能杀死过敏原」（过敏原是蛋白质，油炸不破坏）、宾客有坚果过敏还推荐坚果装饰的甜品。
Deep Dive
  • What: Cross-contact means trace amounts of an allergen transfer to other food or surfaces (shared fryer oil, worktops, knives, gloves); it is not the same as cross-contamination (microbial) — mixing up the terms in an English interview signals weak professionalism.
  • How: Know the high-risk points: shared fryers and oil, the same grill, shared blenders, made-to-order substitutions; be honest about limits ("We cannot guarantee zero cross-contact for fried items"), push for dedicated equipment, and flag the risk when taking the order.
  • Watch out: Using the two terms interchangeably, assuming "frying kills allergens" (they are proteins — frying does not destroy them), or recommending a nut-garnished dessert to a nut-allergic guest.

📍 应用场景：食品安全培训与宾客问询答复；英文面试鉴别「真服务生」的术语考点。

特殊饮食Dietary Restrictions

深度解析
  • 【是什么】过敏之外的饮食要求体系：素食 vegetarian / 纯素 vegan、清真 halal、犹太洁食 kosher、无麸质 gluten-free、低盐低糖等医疗性饮食（medical diets）。
  • 【怎么做】收集点：登船前的线上预订问卷（dietary request form）与点单环节；流程：收集 → 录入宾客档案（guest profile）→ 通知厨房统一安排 → 每餐由固定服务生跟进同一宾客。要能区分 vegetarian（可食蛋奶）与 vegan（全植物），kosher 的核心是肉类与乳制品不混食。
  • 【易错点】把 vegan 上成带芝士的菜、宗教饮食（halal/kosher）临时在菜单里「替换」而非走厨房安排、医疗性饮食不当成个人偏好处理；面试常问 "What is the difference between vegetarian and vegan?"。
Deep Dive
  • What: Dietary requirements beyond allergies: vegetarian / vegan, halal, kosher, gluten-free, and medical diets such as low-sodium or diabetic — a structured system, not ad-hoc requests.
  • How: Collected at pre-cruise booking questionnaires (dietary request form) and at point of order; flow: capture → log in the guest profile → brief the galley for consistent arrangements → ideally the same server follows the same guest each meal. Know vegetarian (may include egg and dairy) vs. vegan (fully plant-based), and that kosher separates meat and dairy.
  • Watch out: Serving a vegan a dish with cheese, "swapping" items on the fly for halal/kosher instead of routing it through the galley, or treating medical diets as personal preference — interviews often ask "What is the difference between vegetarian and vegan?".

📍 应用场景：登船前预订、点单与宾客档案管理；特殊饮食宾客的全程服务跟进。

厨房部 Galley29 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

危险温度带Temperature Danger Zone

深度解析
  • 【是什么】食品安全核心概念：5–60°C（41–135°F）之间细菌（如沙门氏菌、金黄色葡萄球菌）呈指数级繁殖，是 HACCP 体系里最主要的监控区间，也是 USPH 卫生检查的重点科目。
  • 【怎么做】熟食热存必须始终高于 60°C（140°F），冷藏必须低于 5°C（41°F）；食材在危险温度带内累计停留不得超过 4 小时（2 小时原则更严的船司要求 2 小时内回冷）；冷却热菜须在 2 小时内从 60°C 降到 21°C、再 4 小时内降到 5°C 以下，用浅盘（shallow pans）、冰浴（ice bath）或急冻柜（blast chiller）加速降温；每台冰箱、热汤池每日多次测温并记录在温度日志（temperature log）上。
  • 【易错点】新人常把热菜"先放室温晾一晾"再进冰箱——这正好把食物泡在危险温度带里；正确做法是分装浅盘急冷。检查要点：冰箱门是否关严、温度计是否校准、记录是否连续。面试常被问"什么是 danger zone"，5–60°C / 41–135°F 必须脱口而出。
Deep Dive
  • What: The core food-safety concept — between 5–60°C (41–135°F), bacteria such as Salmonella and Staphylococcus multiply exponentially. It is the most heavily monitored range under HACCP and a top USPH inspection item.
  • How: Keep hot food above 60°C (140°F) and cold food below 5°C (41°F) at all times; total exposure in the danger zone must not exceed 4 hours (stricter ships enforce the 2-hour rule). Cool hot food from 60°C to 21°C within 2 hours, then to below 5°C within another 4 hours, using shallow pans, ice baths or a blast chiller. Take and log temperatures on every fridge and hot well several times a day.
  • Watch out: New crew often "let food cool at room temperature" before refrigerating — that is exactly how food gets soaked in the danger zone; portion into shallow pans and chill fast instead. Check: fridge doors fully closed, thermometers calibrated, logs continuous. In interviews, "What is the danger zone?" must be answered instantly: 5–60°C / 41–135°F.

📍 应用场景：所有厨房岗位（厨师、助理厨师、洗碗工）的日常温度记录与冷藏/热存操作；USPH 迎检

危害分析与关键控制点HACCP

深度解析
  • 【是什么】Hazard Analysis and Critical Control Points，国际通行的预防性食品安全管理体系：先分析各环节可能出现的生物/化学/物理危害，再在关键控制点（CCP）上设限值并持续监控。邮轮厨房强制执行，同时须满足 USPH（美国公共卫生署 Vessel Sanitation Program）检查标准。
  • 【怎么做】船上一线员工的具体动作：收货时验温（冷藏货 ≤5°C、冷冻货 ≤-18°C，拒收温度超标货）、存储按生熟分区与先进先出、烹饪达中心温度（禽肉 74°C、绞肉 68°C、整块牛肉 63°C 以上）、热存 ≥60°C、留样贴标，并把每个关键点的温度/消毒数据实时填写进 HACCP 日志（HACCP log），检查官可随时抽查数周的连续记录。
  • 【易错点】最常见失分是"事后补记录"——记录必须与操作同步，涂改要划线签名；其次是不知 CCP 在自己岗位的哪一步。面试关联：能说出收货测温、烹饪中心温度、终洗温度三四个具体关键点，比背概念更加分。
Deep Dive
  • What: Hazard Analysis and Critical Control Points — the international preventive food-safety system: identify biological/chemical/physical hazards at each step, then set critical limits and monitor them at Critical Control Points (CCPs). Mandatory on cruise ships and aligned with the USPH Vessel Sanitation Program.
  • How: Front-line actions — verify receiving temperatures (chilled ≤5°C, frozen ≤-18°C, reject out-of-spec loads), store raw/RTE separately with FIFO, cook to core temperatures (poultry 74°C, ground meat 68°C, whole-muscle beef above 63°C), hot-hold at ≥60°C, label and date everything, and enter every temperature/sanitation reading into the HACCP log in real time; inspectors can spot-check weeks of continuous records.
  • Watch out: The most common failure is "back-filling the log" — records must be written as you work, with corrections struck through and signed; also know which CCPs sit on your own station. Interview tip: naming concrete CCPs (receiving temps, cooking core temps, final rinse temp) beats reciting the definition.

📍 应用场景：厨师/助理厨师/洗碗工每日 HACCP 日志填写；收货验温；USPH 与 VSP 迎检培训

备菜Mise en Place

深度解析
  • 【是什么】法语"各就各位"：开餐前把所有食材洗净、切配、称量、分装到位，锅具酱汁预先就绪。它是西厨组织纪律的基石，直接决定高峰期的出餐速度与出品一致性，也是厨师长评估新人执行力的第一窗口。
  • 【怎么做】按当日预订人数（covers）与预测单量制定备菜清单；蔬菜切配统一刀工规格（丁 dice、丝 julienne、碎 mince），按份分盒贴标冷藏；酱汁、高汤、腌料提前批量制备；台面布局遵循"常用就近"原则，砧板、刀具、抹布按颜色分区。一般午市前 2–3 小时、晚市前 3–4 小时完成主体备菜。
  • 【易错点】只求数量不求规格——大小不一的切块会在出餐时露馅；备好的料不贴标不盖膜，直接违反留样标签制度。检查要点：备菜盒是否全部带品名+日期标签、生熟是否分板。面试常问"你如何安排 busy service 前的 prep"，答案要有清单意识与时间轴。
Deep Dive
  • What: French for "everything in its place" — all ingredients washed, cut, weighed, portioned and staged before service, with pans and sauces ready. It is the backbone of galley discipline, driving both speed and consistency at peak, and the first thing a chef evaluates in a new hire.
  • How: Build a prep list from the day's covers and forecast; cut vegetables to uniform specs (dice, julienne, mince), portion into labeled, covered containers; batch sauces, stocks and marinades ahead; arrange the station "most-used closest", with color-coded boards and cloths. Main prep is generally done 2–3 hours before lunch and 3–4 hours before dinner.
  • Watch out: Quantity without consistency — uneven cuts show on the plate; unlabeled, uncovered prep containers violate the labeling rule. Check: every container has a product name + date label, raw and cooked kept on separate boards. In interviews, when asked "How do you organize prep before a busy service?", answer with a prep list and a timeline.

📍 应用场景：助理厨师/厨师每日开餐前备菜；部门考核与试用评估

食品留样与标签Food Labeling / FIFO

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船上所有半成品、解冻品、开封品必须"有名有姓有日期"：容器贴注品名、制备/开封日期（必要时加效期与班次），并按先进先出（First In, First Out）轮换取用。这是 USPH 检查的必查项，也是追溯食源性疾病的关键链条。
  • 【怎么做】使用船供防水标签或标签机，写品名、日期（部分船司要求班次/操作人缩写）；备用容器统一放标签笔；熟制冷藏一般 3–4 天内用完（按各船 SOP，常用"生产日+3 天"规则），解冻品 24–48 小时内使用不得复冻；取货永远从最旧批次拿，新货入库存放时压到旧货后面或下层。
  • 【易错点】"我认得出是什么"就不贴标——检查官不管你认不认得；撕掉旧标贴新标却不改日期；新货挡住旧货导致旧货过期。检查要点：随机开三盒都能看到清晰标签才算合格。面试关联："What does FIFO mean and why?" 是洗碗工/助理岗高频题。
Deep Dive
  • What: Every prepared, thawed or opened item on board must be "named and dated": containers carry product name plus prep/open date (some ships also require expiry and shift initials), and stock is rotated First In, First Out. A mandatory USPH checkpoint and the key traceability chain for any foodborne-illness investigation.
  • How: Use ship-supplied waterproof labels or a label printer with name, date (some lines add shift/preparer initials); keep one marker per station; use cooked chilled items within 3–4 days per ship SOP (commonly "day of production + 3"), use thawed items within 24–48 hours and never refreeze; always pull the oldest batch first and shelve new stock behind or below the old.
  • Watch out: "I can tell what it is" is not a label as far as inspectors are concerned; relabeling without updating the date; new stock blocking old stock until it expires. Check: open any three containers at random — all must show a clear label. Interview link: "What does FIFO mean and why?" is a frequent question for utility/steward roles.

📍 应用场景：冷厨/备菜间半成品管理；USPH 迎检抽查；库房与冷冻库轮换

生熟分离Cross-contamination Prevention

深度解析
  • 【是什么】防止生食材（尤其生肉、禽、海鲜）上的致病菌转移到即食食品的过程控制。交叉污染是船上食源性疾病爆发（gastro outbreak）的头号原因，属于 HACCP 的核心控制环节。
  • 【怎么做】执行颜色编码体系（国际通用：红=生肉、黄=生禽、蓝=生海鲜、绿=果蔬、白=熟食/乳制品）；生熟分刀分板分容器；生肉冷库存放于熟食下方，按"禽最高、肉其次、海鲜最低"的滴漏顺序分层；处理生食材后须洗手、换手套、消毒台面；生熟食材不同冷藏箱或同箱分层分隔。
  • 【易错点】砧板颜色对了但刀没换；处理生鸡后只换手套不洗手就去做沙拉；生肉汁滴到下层熟食（叠放顺序错误）。检查要点：颜色编码是否全站一致、抹布是否按色区分使用。面试常问"如何防止 cross-contamination"，答出颜色码+分层存放+洗手三件套即可拿满分。
Deep Dive
  • What: Process controls that stop pathogens from raw foods (especially raw meat, poultry and seafood) reaching ready-to-eat food. Cross-contamination is the leading cause of gastro outbreaks on board and a core HACCP control.
  • How: Follow the color-coded system (commonly red = raw meat, yellow = raw poultry, blue = raw seafood, green = produce, white = cooked/dairy); separate boards, knives and containers for raw and cooked; store raw meat below ready-to-eat items in the walk-in, stacking by drip risk — poultry on top, meat below, seafood lowest; wash hands, change gloves and sanitize surfaces after handling raw; use separate fridges or segregated shelving for raw and cooked.
  • Watch out: Right color board but same knife; changing gloves after raw chicken without washing hands before making salad; raw meat drips onto cooked food below (wrong stacking order). Check: color coding consistent across the whole galley, cloths color-matched to task. For the interview question "How do you prevent cross-contamination?", color codes + vertical storage + handwashing is a full-mark answer.

📍 应用场景：所有厨房岗位的砧板/刀具/冷藏存放规范；gastro 事件防控

出餐口The Pass / Expediter

深度解析
  • 【是什么】厨房与餐厅之间的交接台，是出品质量的最后一道关卡：所有菜品须经过主厨或传菜员（expediter）核对订单、检查摆盘与温度后才能放行。它同时是节奏控制器，决定整个餐厅的上菜顺序与速度。
  • 【怎么做】出餐前"三查"：一查单据（菜品与订单桌号/份数一致）、二查外观（摆盘干净、无指印油渍、份量符合照片标准）、三查温度与时效（热菜趁热走、冷菜不返温）；主厨喊单（calling the pass），各档口应答"Oui, chef / Yes chef"确认；服务生按叫号取餐，遇缺菜、改单、过敏单（allergy ticket）须在出餐口当面确认。
  • 【易错点】高峰期为赶速度跳过目检直接出餐——一份脏盘就可能引发客诉；过敏原订单（gluten-free、nut allergy）不与主厨口头确认就放行，是严重事故隐患。检查要点：过敏单是否有醒目标记并双确认。面试关联：体现团队协作与沟通意识，可用"pass 上人人报备"举例。
Deep Dive
  • What: The hand-off counter between galley and dining room — the final quality gate: every dish passes the head chef or expediter, who checks order, plating and temperature before release. It is also the pace-setter controlling the sequence and rhythm of service.
  • How: Run the three checks before firing: ticket (dish matches table/cover count), appearance (clean plate, no fingerprints or drips, portion matches the photo spec), and temperature/timing (hot food goes hot, cold food stays cold). The chef calls the pass and each station answers "Oui, chef / Yes chef"; waiters collect by call number, and any missing item, amendment or allergy ticket must be verbally confirmed at the pass.
  • Watch out: Skipping the visual check to gain seconds during peak — one dirty plate can trigger a guest complaint; releasing an allergy ticket (gluten-free, nut allergy) without verbal confirmation from the chef is a serious incident risk. Check: allergy tickets clearly flagged and double-confirmed. Interview link: a good example of teamwork and communication — "everyone calls back on the pass".

📍 应用场景：主厨/领班/传菜员高峰期出餐协调；过敏原订单管理

三星槽消毒3-Sink System

深度解析
  • 【是什么】人工洗碗的标准流程设施：第一槽洗涤（wash，含洗洁精的热水）、第二槽过清（rinse，干净清水）、第三槽消毒（sanitize，消毒液或热水）。USPH 检查必看，也是厨房助理/洗碗工入职第一课。
  • 【怎么做】三槽水温按各船 SOP 记录，常见规范：洗涤槽 ≥43°C（110°F）左右，消毒槽化学消毒（如含氯消毒液约 50–100 ppm，或季铵盐按厂商标定浓度）或热消毒 ≥77°C（170°F）浸泡至少 30 秒；每 2–4 小时或水脏即换槽；消毒浓度用试纸（test strip）验证并登记；洗后风干（air dry），不用抹布擦干以防二次污染。
  • 【易错点】三槽合一、洗涤槽水脏了继续用；消毒水浓度凭感觉倒、不用试纸测；洗完用口布擦干。检查要点：槽边是否挂有浓度试纸与记录表、水温计是否在位。面试常问"描述 3-sink 流程"，按 wash–rinse–sanitize 加换水频率作答。
Deep Dive
  • What: The standard manual dishwashing setup: sink 1 wash (hot water with detergent), sink 2 rinse (clean water), sink 3 sanitize (chemical solution or hot water). A must-see for USPH inspectors and the first lesson for galley utility/stewards.
  • How: Log sink temperatures per ship SOP — commonly wash at ≥43°C (110°F); sanitize chemically (chlorine roughly 50–100 ppm, or quaternary ammonium at the manufacturer's spec) or thermally at ≥77°C (170°F) for at least 30 seconds; change the water every 2–4 hours or sooner when soiled; verify sanitizer strength with a test strip and log it; air-dry after washing — never towel-dry, which recontaminates.
  • Watch out: Collapsing three sinks into one; reusing dirty wash water; pouring sanitizer "by feel" without a test strip; wiping dry with a side towel. Check: test strips and log sheet at the sink, thermometer in place. For the interview question "Describe the 3-sink process", answer wash–rinse–sanitize plus water-change frequency.

📍 应用场景：洗碗工/厨房助理人工洗涤区；USPH 日常自查与迎检

洗碗机终洗温度Final Rinse Temperature

深度解析
  • 【是什么】高温式（热消毒）洗碗机的最后一道喷淋工序温度指标：须达到约 82°C（180°F）才能在数秒内完成热消毒，是洗碗岗位最重要的数字，USPH 检查必查记录。低温式洗碗机则依靠化学消毒剂，水温要求低但药液浓度须验证。
  • 【怎么做】开班前预热机器，看机载温度表确认终洗达 82°C（180°F）再开始作业；每台机器的温度逐日记录在洗碗机日志（dish machine log）上，常见做法每小时抽记一次；压力不足或温度掉到 79°C 以下须停机报修；装筐讲究"面朝内、不叠压"，让水流能冲到每一件餐具的所有面。
  • 【易错点】机器没热透就开始洗；只看机器表不看水压表（pressure 应约 15–25 psi，视机型）；把大盆平放盖住下层餐具导致洗不净。检查要点：日志连续、温度达标、试纸/水温计可用。面试关联：洗碗工岗位面试必问此数字，82°C / 180°F 要背到肌肉记忆。
Deep Dive
  • What: The final spray-rinse specification of a hot-water sanitizing dish machine: about 82°C (180°F) is required to heat-sanitize in seconds — the single most important number for the wash-up crew and a mandatory USPH log item. Low-temp machines sanitize chemically at lower water temperatures but require verified chemical strength.
  • How: Preheat the machine at the start of shift and confirm the final rinse reads 82°C (180°F) before washing; record each machine's temperature daily on the dish machine log, commonly spot-checked hourly; if pressure drops or temperature falls below about 79°C, stop and call maintenance; rack properly — faces inward, no nesting — so water reaches every surface of every item.
  • Watch out: Starting before the machine is fully hot; checking temperature but ignoring the pressure gauge (roughly 15–25 psi depending on model); laying big pots flat over other ware and blocking the spray. Check: continuous logs, temperature on spec, working test strips/thermometer. Interview: this number is guaranteed for dishwasher roles — 82°C / 180°F should be muscle memory.

📍 应用场景：洗碗工（dishwasher/utility）岗位核心 SOP；USPH 检查重点

油污分离器Grease Trap

深度解析
  • 【是什么】安装在厨房排水管路上的油脂拦截设备，让废水中的油脂浮起、固体沉底、清水排出。邮轮对污水排放管制极严（MARPOL 附则 IV/V），油污分离器既是环保合规设备，也是防止厨房地漏堵塞返味的关键设施。
  • 【怎么做】按各船周期（常见每日刮除表面浮油、每周彻底清理，具体以轮机部排程为准）清理集油斗，把收集的废油（used cooking oil）倒入指定废油桶由环境部统一回收；清理后按表格登记日期与操作人；大块油脂与食物残渣先入残渣桶/粉碎机，不冲下水道；发现排水变慢立即报告，不等堵死。
  • 【易错点】把热油直接倒进地漏——冷凝后整段管路报废；清理后不登记，检查时无法追溯；戴普通手套伸手进热废水。检查要点：记录本与废油桶封条是否完整、水面浮油是否超标。面试关联：体现环保意识（environmental compliance），适合在职责问答中主动提及。
Deep Dive
  • What: A grease interception device on the galley drainage line — grease floats, solids settle, and clear water discharges out. Cruise ships face strict discharge rules under MARPOL Annexes IV/V, so the grease trap is both an environmental-compliance item and the key defense against blocked, smelly galley drains.
  • How: Skim floating grease and clean out the trap on the ship's schedule (commonly daily skimming, weekly full clean, per the Environmental Officer's roster); decant used cooking oil into designated waste-oil drums for centralized recovery; log date and initials after each clean; scrape food solids into the pulper/waste bin first — never rinse them down the drain; report slow drainage immediately instead of waiting for a blockage.
  • Watch out: Pouring hot oil straight into the drain — once it sets, the whole line is ruined; cleaning without logging, leaving no trace at inspection; reaching into hot wastewater with ordinary gloves. Check: complete log book, sealed waste-oil drums, grease layer on the water within limits. Interview link: mentioning environmental compliance shows strong awareness.

📍 应用场景：洗碗工/厨房助理日常清理；环境合规（MARPOL）检查

垃圾分拣Waste Segregation (MARPOL)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】依《国际防止船舶造成污染公约》（MARPOL）附则 V 对船上垃圾强制分类处置的制度。厨房垃圾主要分流为：食物垃圾（food waste，可粉碎或冷冻储存待岸上接收）、塑料（plastics，禁止任何形式入海）、纸类/金属/玻璃等可回收物，以及食用油（cooking oil）。
  • 【怎么做】每个配餐间与洗碗区设置分类桶并贴彩色图示标签；食物垃圾按距岸距离执行：距岸 3 海里内可经粉碎机（pulper）处理后排放（各船司政策更严），12 海里内可粉碎排放，超过 12 海里可排放已粉碎垃圾、25 海里内可排放未被粉碎的食品废弃物——具体执行以本船环保计划为准，拿不准就一律存柜待岸上接收；塑料必须单独打包存放在专门垃圾柜，抵港交岸上回收并留存收据（receipt）备查。
  • 【易错点】塑料袋混入食物垃圾——MARPOL 违规可致巨额罚款并影响全船绩效；图省事把食用油倒下水道。检查要点：分类标识是否清晰、垃圾柜无混装、处置记录完整。面试关联：环保问题是邮轮雇主价值观题，可答"segregate at source, log everything"。
Deep Dive
  • What: The mandatory shipboard waste-sorting regime under MARPOL Annex V. Galley waste mainly splits into: food waste (pulp or freeze and hold for shore reception), plastics (never discharged to sea in any form), recyclables (paper/metal/glass) and cooking oil.
  • How: Every pantry and wash-up area has labeled color-coded bins; food waste follows distance-from-shore rules — typically within 3 nm only via pulper per stricter line policies, within 12 nm comminuted discharge, comminuted beyond 12 nm and uncomminuted food waste beyond 25 nm — always deferring to your ship's environmental plan, and when in doubt, hold it for shore reception; plastics are bagged separately, stored in a dedicated waste room, handed ashore at port, with receipts kept for audit.
  • Watch out: Plastic bags mixed into food waste — a MARPOL violation can mean heavy fines and hits the whole crew's appraisal; pouring cooking oil down the drain "to save time". Check: clear bin signage, no mixed storage in the waste room, complete disposal records. Interview link: an employer-values question — answer "segregate at source, log everything".

📍 应用场景：全厨房岗位垃圾分拣；MARPOL 环保合规与港口国检查

个人防护装备PPE (Personal Protective Equipment)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】厨房岗位按作业风险配备的个人防护装备：常规作业的防滑鞋（non-slip safety shoes）、厨师服与围裙；高温作业的防烫手套（oven mitts/heat-resistant gloves）；化学品清洁（洗碗机加药、除垢、去油）所需的橡胶手套、护目镜（goggles）与防水围裙；油炸作业的长袖防护。船上由安全管理体系（SEMS/公司安全手册）统一规定。
  • 【怎么做】按任务领用对应 PPE：使用化学品前先读 MSDS/标签，穿戴手套+护目镜+围裙三件套；出烤炉、炸锅、蒸汽锅一律防烫手套并喊"Heads up / Behind you"；防滑鞋全程穿，湿滑区域慢走不跑；PPE 缺损立即向主管领新，不得"将就"。
  • 【易错点】配消毒液时不戴护目镜被溅伤——这是船上最常见的工伤之一；徒手去够炸篮；穿着普通帆布鞋进厨房。检查要点：岗位 PPE 清单是否张贴、破损装备是否更换。面试关联：安全意识题常以"你如何防止厨房烫伤/化学品伤害"出现，答 PPE+呼叫提醒+按标签操作。
Deep Dive
  • What: Task-based protective equipment for galley roles: non-slip safety shoes, chef jacket and apron for routine work; oven mitts or heat-resistant gloves for hot work; rubber gloves, goggles and a waterproof apron for chemical cleaning (dish machine dosing, descaling, degreasing); long sleeves for frying. Standards are set by the ship's safety management system (SEMS/company safety manual).
  • How: Match PPE to the task: read the MSDS/label before using any chemical and wear gloves + goggles + apron as a set; announce "Heads up / Behind you" and wear heat gloves whenever handling ovens, fryers or steam kettles; keep safety shoes on at all times and walk — never run — in wet areas; replace damaged PPE immediately through your supervisor, never make do.
  • Watch out: Mixing sanitizer without goggles — splash injuries are among the most common shipboard accidents; reaching into a fry basket bare-handed; canvas sneakers in the galley. Check: station PPE list posted, damaged gear replaced. Interview: safety questions often come as "How do you prevent burns/chemical injuries in the galley?" — answer with PPE, verbal call-outs and label-driven procedures.

📍 应用场景：全厨房岗位日常安全作业；化学品清洁与高温设备操作

肉类Meat

深度解析
  • 【是什么】西厨房核心蛋白线：牛肉（beef：西冷 sirloin、菲力 tenderloin、肋眼 ribeye）、猪肉（pork：里脊 loin、肋排 ribs）、羊肉（lamb：羊排 rack、腿肉 leg）、小牛肉（veal）等。术语掌握度直接决定能否听懂订单与口令，是面试与上岗的双重考点。
  • 【怎么做】牛排熟度（doneness）按中心温度：rare 约 49–52°C、medium-rare 约 52–55°C、medium 约 57–60°C、medium-well 约 63–66°C、well-done 71°C 以上，用探针温度计（thermometer probe）测中心；煎烤后静置（resting）约 5–10 分钟再切，让肉汁回流；按部位选用火候与刀法——菲力煎、肋排慢烤（low & slow）；生肉按颜色码分区处理并在熟食下方冷藏。
  • 【易错点】熟度全凭"手感"不测温——邮轮出品要求一致性，一份 overcooked 就要重做并计入成本；静置不够就切导致"血水"满盘；medium 与 medium-rare 口头混淆。面试常问五个熟度的英文与温度，medium-rare 52–55°C 是高频答案。
Deep Dive
  • What: The core protein line of a Western galley: beef (sirloin, tenderloin, ribeye), pork (loin, ribs), lamb (rack, leg), veal and more. Vocabulary mastery decides whether you can follow orders and call-outs — it is both an interview topic and a hands-on requirement.
  • How: Steak doneness by core temperature: rare about 49–52°C, medium-rare 52–55°C, medium 57–60°C, medium-well 63–66°C, well-done 71°C+, measured with a thermometer probe; rest steaks 5–10 minutes after cooking so juices redistribute before cutting; match technique to cut — sear the tenderloin, low-and-slow the ribs; handle raw meat under the color codes and store below ready-to-eat items.
  • Watch out: Judging doneness "by feel" alone — cruise production demands consistency, and one overcooked steak means a redo charged to cost; cutting before resting floods the plate; mixing up medium and medium-rare on call-outs. Interview: know all five doneness terms with temperatures; medium-rare at 52–55°C is the classic answer.

📍 应用场景：热厨（hot line）牛排房/烧烤档；点单与出餐口沟通

海鲜Seafood

深度解析
  • 【是什么】鱼类如三文鱼（salmon）、鳕鱼（cod）、鲈鱼（sea bass）、金枪鱼（tuna）、大比目鱼（halibut）；甲壳与贝类如虾（shrimp/prawn）、龙虾（lobster）、蟹（crab）、青口（mussels）、扇贝（scallops）、生蚝（oysters）。海鲜是船上高价值高风险食材：既贵，又是过敏与变质重灾区。
  • 【怎么做】鱼类中心温度约 60–63°C 即熟，判断口诀"flakes easily, opaque"（轻拨即分层、肉色不透明）；虾煮到卷曲变红即可（约 2–3 分钟），过火即老；双壳贝类（青口、蛤蜊）开口的煮熟、不开口的弃用；去腥用柠檬汁、白葡萄酒、香草（莳萝 dill 配三文鱼是经典组合）；海鲜必须 0–4°C 冰藏、垫冰存放，蓝区/蓝板单独处理。
  • 【易错点】把鱼煎到"全熟发白"才出锅——远洋客人对此零容忍；贝类死贝混入烹制引发食物中毒；解冻海鲜二次冷冻。检查要点：冰面是否覆盖鱼体、有无异味（新鲜海产是海风味不是腥臭味）。面试常问"如何判断鱼熟了"，答 flakes easily 即显专业。
Deep Dive
  • What: Fish such as salmon, cod, sea bass, tuna and halibut; crustaceans and shellfish such as shrimp/prawn, lobster, crab, mussels, scallops and oysters. Seafood is the galley's high-value, high-risk ingredient: expensive, allergy-sensitive and quick to spoil.
  • How: Fish is done at a core temperature of about 60–63°C — test "flakes easily, opaque"; shrimp curls and turns pink in 2–3 minutes, any longer and it toughens; bivalves (mussels, clams) that open when cooked are eaten, those that stay closed are discarded; lift aromas with lemon juice, white wine and herbs (dill with salmon is a classic); keep seafood at 0–4°C packed on ice and process it in the blue-zone/blue-board area only.
  • Watch out: Cooking fish until it is white and dry — guests have zero tolerance for overcooked fish; cooking dead bivalves (food-poisoning risk); refreezing thawed seafood. Check: fish fully bedded on ice, no off smell (fresh seafood smells briny, not fishy-foul). Interview: "How do you tell fish is done?" — "flakes easily" signals real kitchen experience.

📍 应用场景：海鲜档/冷厨处理与熟制；过敏原管理与冰鲜验收

主食谷物Staples & Grains

深度解析
  • 【是什么】菜单淀粉类主力：大米（长粒米 long-grain、意大利烩饭米 arborio/risotto rice、印度香米 basmati）、意面（spaghetti 直面、penne 笔管面、farfalle 蝴蝶面、tagliatelle 宽面）、土豆制品与藜麦（quinoa）、库斯库斯（couscous）等杂粮。量大、出餐快、看似简单，实则是连锁出错的重灾区。
  • 【怎么做】意面以 al dente（有咬劲）为准：比包装时间提前 1–2 分钟捞出，再入酱汁中收尾（finish in the sauce）吸收风味；煮面水加盐（约 1 升水 10 克盐），水面比例充足防止粘连；烩饭米先炒米（tostatura）再加热汤分次搅打（mantecatura 收尾加黄油帕玛森）；米饭按米种定水比，长粒米约 1:1.5–2。
  • 【易错点】意面一次煮一大锅泡在水中等单——面条吸水涨发报废；给 risotto 用长粒米；煮面水不加盐。检查要点：意面是否按份分煮或分批控时。面试关联："What does al dente mean?" 几乎必考——答"to the tooth, firm bite"。
Deep Dive
  • What: The starch backbone of the menu: rice (long-grain, risotto rice such as arborio, basmati), pasta (spaghetti, penne, farfalle, tagliatelle), potato preparations, plus grains like quinoa and couscous. High volume and fast-moving — simple on paper, but a common source of chain-wide errors.
  • How: Cook pasta al dente — pull it 1–2 minutes before the package time and finish it in the sauce so it absorbs flavor; salt the pasta water (about 10 g per liter) and keep the water-to-pasta ratio generous to prevent sticking; for risotto, toast the rice first (tostatura), add hot stock gradually, then beat in butter and Parmesan to finish (mantecatura); set rice water ratios by type — about 1:1.5–2 for long-grain.
  • Watch out: Boiling a huge batch of pasta and holding it in water waiting for orders — it swells into mush; using long-grain rice for risotto; unsalted pasta water. Check: pasta cooked to order or in timed batches. Interview: "What does al dente mean?" is nearly guaranteed — "to the tooth, firm bite".

📍 应用场景：热厨意面/主食档高峰出餐；自助餐厅大锅量米饭意面制备

乳制品Dairy

深度解析
  • 【是什么】厨房通用原料线：黄油（butter，烹饪基准为无盐 unsalted，便于控咸）、奶油（single/light cream、whipping cream、heavy cream——脂肪约 36% 以上，耐煮不易分离）、牛奶、酸奶油（sour cream）、奶酪（帕玛森 Parmesan、马苏里拉 mozzarella、切达 cheddar、布里 brie、菲达 feta）。乳制品既是风味担当也是危险温度带高风险食材。
  • 【怎么做】全程 5°C 以下冷藏、先进先出；奶油按用途选型——打发生物奶油需脂肪 30% 以上且 chilled 后打发，重奶油可直接入热酱汁；帕玛森现磨现用保香气；奶酪开封后蜡纸/油纸包裹（不用保鲜膜闷）存放；黄油用于酱汁与烘焙时注意区分盐度并统一换算。
  • 【易错点】打发奶油前奶油没冰透导致打不发；奶酪用保鲜膜紧贴软皮奶酪（如 brie）反而加速变质；heavy cream 与 whipping cream 混用导致热酱汁分离。检查要点：冷藏温度记录、开封日期标签齐全。面试关联：可结合 cappuccino/热酱汁场景谈奶油选择，体现原料知识。
Deep Dive
  • What: The universal dairy line: butter (unsalted is the cooking standard for salt control), creams (single/light cream, whipping cream, heavy cream — about 36%+ fat, holds up in hot sauces), milk, sour cream, and cheeses (Parmesan, mozzarella, cheddar, brie, feta). Dairy carries flavor but is also high-risk in the danger zone.
  • How: Keep everything below 5°C with strict FIFO; choose cream by use — whipping cream needs 30%+ fat and must be well chilled before whipping, heavy cream goes straight into hot sauces; grate Parmesan to order to keep aroma; wrap opened cheese in wax/parchment paper (not cling film, which suffocates rinds); note salt levels when using butter in sauces and baking, and convert consistently.
  • Watch out: Whipping cream that is not properly chilled will not whip; cling film pressed onto soft-rind cheeses like brie speeds spoilage; swapping heavy and whipping cream splits hot sauces. Check: fridge temperature logs, open-date labels on everything. Interview: discussing cream selection for a hot sauce or cappuccino shows real product knowledge.

📍 应用场景：冷厨/烘焙房/酱汁档原料使用；冷藏管理与打发作业
