蔬果Produce

深度解析
  • 【是什么】配菜与沙拉原料：芦笋（asparagus）、西兰花（broccoli）、土豆（potatoes）、胡萝卜（carrots）、番茄（tomatoes）、生菜（lettuce）、洋葱（onions）、柠檬（lemons）及各类时令水果。蔬果损耗占厨房成本大头，储存管理直接体现专业度。
  • 【怎么做】分区储存：果蔬冷库独立于生肉冷库，防交叉污染与串味；乙烯释放型水果（苹果、香蕉、瓜类）与乙烯敏感型叶菜、胡萝卜分开存放防催熟变质；叶菜 2–4°C 高湿存放、垫吸水纸；土豆洋葱避光常温阴凉库（冷藏反使土豆糖化）；每日巡检挑出腐损件，先进先出。
  • 【易错点】苹果和生菜同柜——几天内叶菜全黄；土豆进冷库发甜发黑（低温淀粉转糖）；洗过的叶菜不复冰存放加速腐烂。检查要点：库温、分区标识、有无霉变串放。面试关联：库房管理题可答"separate by ethylene, FIFO daily check"。
Deep Dive
  • What: Sides and salad raw materials: asparagus, broccoli, potatoes, carrots, tomatoes, lettuce, onions, lemons and seasonal fruit. Produce is a major slice of food cost — storage discipline is a direct read on professionalism.
  • How: Store by zone: a dedicated fruit-and-veg fridge separate from raw-meat storage, preventing both cross-contamination and odor transfer; keep ethylene-producing fruit (apples, bananas, melons) away from ethylene-sensitive greens and carrots to prevent premature ripening; hold leafy greens at 2–4°C and high humidity with absorbent paper; keep potatoes and onions in a dark, cool, ventilated dry store (refrigeration turns potato starch to sugar); inspect daily, cull spoilage, rotate FIFO.
  • Watch out: Apples sharing a shelf with lettuce — greens yellow within days; refrigerating potatoes, which turn sweet and darken; storing washed wet greens back in the fridge, which accelerates rot. Check: fridge temperatures, zone signage, no mold transfer. Interview: for storeroom questions, answer "separate by ethylene, daily FIFO checks".

📍 应用场景：冷厨/沙拉台备菜；果蔬冷库与干货库分区管理

干货香料Dry Goods & Spices

深度解析
  • 【是什么】常温库主力：面粉（flour）、米（rice）、豆类（legumes）、糖、油（橄榄油 olive oil、植物油）、罐头与香料——罗勒（basil）、迷迭香（rosemary）、百里香（thyme）、牛至（oregano）、黑胡椒（black pepper）、红椒粉（paprika）、孜然（cumin）等。干货占库房面积最大，管理好坏一眼可见。
  • 【怎么做】存放于阴凉（约 15–21°C）、干燥、通风的库房，离地离墙（离地约 15 cm 以上）防潮防虫；所有开封品密封容器转装并贴开封日期标签，按效期轮换；香料避光保存（光照加速精油挥发失香），整粒香料（胡椒粒、孜然粒）比粉状保香更久、现磨现用；面粉等大宗货先进先出并定期翻堆检查虫害。
  • 【易错点】开封的面粉袋原样敞口立在架子上；香料灶台边受热受光半年失效；新面粉压在旧面粉前面。检查要点：离地离墙是否到位、开封标签是否齐全、库房无虫迹。面试关联：库房（storeroom）岗位面试常问 dry storage 三要素——cool, dry, off the floor。
Deep Dive
  • What: The dry-store backbone: flour, rice, legumes, sugar, oils (olive and vegetable), canned goods and spices — basil, rosemary, thyme, oregano, black pepper, paprika, cumin and more. Dry goods take up the most storeroom space, so their condition is instantly visible to any inspector.
  • How: Store in a cool (about 15–21°C), dry, ventilated room, off the floor and away from walls (about 15 cm clearance) against moisture and pests; decant all opened items into sealed, dated containers and rotate by shelf life; keep spices out of light (light volatilizes their essential oils) and buy whole spices (peppercorns, cumin seeds) where possible — they hold aroma far longer than ground; rotate bulk flour FIFO and inspect regularly for pests.
  • Watch out: Open flour bags standing loose on the shelf; spice racks beside the stove losing all aroma within months from heat and light; new flour shelved in front of old. Check: proper off-floor clearance, date labels on every opened item, no pest traces. Interview: storeroom roles are often asked the three dry-storage rules — cool, dry, off the floor.

📍 应用场景：库房管理员（storekeeper）与备菜岗领料；虫害防控检查

开酥面团Laminated Dough

深度解析
  • 【是什么】可颂（croissant）、丹麦酥（Danish）、千层酥（puff pastry）的核心工艺：将黄油片包入面团后反复擀开折叠（folding），形成数十层"面层夹油层"结构；烘烤时黄油受热汽化、水蒸气把层与层推开，成就外酥内软的蜂窝层次。
  • 【怎么做】全程控温是命门：黄油与面团硬度必须一致（约 12–15°C 冷藏状态），黄油太硬会碎裂、太软会被面团吸收而失去层次；常用"三折"（letter fold）与"四折"（book fold）交替，每轮折叠后冷藏松弛 30–60 分钟再擀，防止面筋断裂回缩；最终擀厚约 3–4 mm 后切割成型，醒发温度约 24–27°C（可颂醒发不宜过热，黄油会融化渗出）。
  • 【易错点】为赶进度减少松弛时间——面团回缩、层次粘连成"死酥"；醒发温度过高黄油化掉、烤盘一滩油；擀制时撒粉过多导致成品干硬。检查要点：切面层次是否分明、烤后蜂窝是否均匀。面试关联：烘焙岗可谈"lamination 全靠温度纪律"，展示工艺理解。
Deep Dive
  • What: The core technique behind croissants, Danish pastries and puff pastry: a slab of butter is enclosed in dough, then rolled and folded repeatedly into dozens of alternating dough-butter layers; in the oven the butter vaporizes and the steam pushes the layers apart, creating the flaky, honeycombed crumb.
  • How: Temperature control is everything: butter and dough must be the same firmness (about 12–15°C, straight from the fridge) — butter too hard shatters, too soft gets absorbed and kills the layers; alternate letter folds and book folds, resting the dough 30–60 minutes chilled between rounds to relax the gluten; roll to about 3–4 mm, cut and shape, then proof at about 24–27°C (hotter proofing melts the butter, which leaks out).
  • Watch out: Cutting rest times to save time — the dough shrinks and the layers fuse into a "dead" paste; proofing too warm so the butter melts into a greasy tray; over-flouring the bench, giving dry, dense results. Check: clean layered cross-section, even honeycomb after baking. Interview: for bakery roles, frame lamination as "temperature discipline" to show craft understanding.

📍 应用场景：烘焙房（bakery/pastry）可颂与丹麦酥量产；早餐时段出品

海绵蛋糕体Sponge / Genoise

深度解析
  • 【是什么】经典蛋糕基底：Genoise（热那亚蛋糕体）靠全蛋加糖隔水加温打发充气获得体积，几乎不依赖泡打粉；另有分蛋法海绵（sponge，蛋白蛋黄分别打发）。是奶油蛋糕（layer cake）、瑞士卷、歌剧院蛋糕（opera）的共同地基。
  • 【怎么做】全蛋与糖隔水加热至约 40–45°C 边加热边打发，离火后继续高速打至浓稠发白、滴落痕迹缓褪（ribbon stage）；拌入过筛面粉用"翻拌"（fold in）手法——刮刀从底部捞起翻覆，轻柔快速，避免消泡；出炉后震模排出大泡、脱模倒扣冷却防止回缩；烤温常见 175–180°C，时间视模具 20–35 分钟，竹签插入无湿面糊即熟。
  • 【易错点】拌粉用"搅拌"代替"翻拌"，消泡塌成饼；蛋液未打到位（ribbon stage 未达成）就拌粉，体积不足；开炉门过早导致塌陷。检查要点：切面气孔细密均匀、按压回弹。面试关联：甜点岗常被问"蛋糕塌陷原因"，消泡与早开门是标准答案。
Deep Dive
  • What: The classic cake base: a Genoise gets its volume from whole eggs and sugar whipped warm over a bain-marie, with little or no baking powder; the sponge method whips whites and yolks separately. It underpins layer cakes, Swiss rolls and opera cake.
  • How: Whip whole eggs with sugar over a bain-marie to about 40–45°C, then off the heat continue to full ribbon stage — thick, pale, and the falling trail holds briefly; fold in sifted flour with a spatula, scooping from the bottom and turning over, gently and quickly to avoid deflating; tap the pan on release to expel big bubbles and unmold upside down to cool and prevent shrinkage; bake at about 175–180°C for 20–35 minutes depending on the mold, done when a skewer comes out clean.
  • Watch out: Stirring instead of folding — the batter collapses into a flat disk; adding flour before the ribbon stage is reached, leaving no volume; opening the oven door too early, which sinks the cake. Check: fine, even crumb and springy bounce-back. Interview: for pastry roles, "Why did my cake collapse?" is answered with deflation from rough mixing or early door-opening.

📍 应用场景：糕点房（pastry）蛋糕体量产；下午茶与生日蛋糕出品

蛋白霜Meringue

深度解析
  • 【是什么】蛋白与糖打发的泡沫体系，分三型：法式（French，生蛋白加糖直接打发，最轻最不稳定，需烤制）、意式（Italian，将约 118°C 热糖浆冲入打发蛋白，烫熟蛋白、最稳定，适合慕斯、装饰、 buttercream）、瑞式（Swiss，蛋白加糖隔水加热至约 55–60°C 糖溶解后再打发，介于两者之间）。
  • 【怎么做】器具绝对无油无水（一点蛋黄油即可报废整盆）；状态分软弯钩（soft peak，弯钩下垂）与硬挺（stiff peak，直立尖角），按用途停机；意式糖浆用糖温计控 118°C（软球阶段），细流冲入已打发的蛋白、避开打蛋头继续打至降温回温；成品即时使用或低温烘干（如 meringue cookies 约 90–100°C 慢烤）。
  • 【易错点】糖浆冲太快烫熟蛋白结块，或没避开打蛋头溅出结粒；法式蛋白霜直接挤花生食未烤足；打过头呈棉絮状失去光泽。检查要点：光泽度、弯钩形态、糖温读数。面试关联："三种 meringue 区别"是甜点岗经典考题，答出稳定性排序即可。
Deep Dive
  • What: A whipped egg-white-and-sugar foam in three styles: French (raw whites whipped with sugar — lightest and least stable, always baked), Italian (hot syrup at about 118°C streamed into whipped whites, "cooking" them — the most stable, ideal for mousses, décor and Italian buttercream), and Swiss (whites and sugar warmed to about 55–60°C until the sugar dissolves, then whipped — in between).
  • How: Bowls and whisks must be spotlessly grease- and water-free — a trace of yolk fat ruins the whole batch; stop at soft peak (a curling hook) or stiff peak (upright points) depending on use; for Italian, hold the syrup at 118°C on a sugar thermometer and stream it into already-whipped whites, avoiding the whisk, then whip until cooled back down; use immediately or dry-bake at low temperature (meringue cookies at about 90–100°C).
  • Watch out: Pouring syrup too fast — it cooks lumps into the whites — or splattering it onto the whisk into hard strings; serving uncooked French meringue without enough baking; over-whipping into a dry, cottony mass. Check: gloss, peak shape, syrup temperature reading. Interview: "Name the three meringues and rank stability" is a classic pastry question.

📍 应用场景：糕点房慕斯/裱饰/甜品台制作；烘焙岗技能考核

卡仕达Custard / Crème Anglaise

深度解析
  • 【是什么】英式奶油酱（crème anglaise）——蛋黄、糖与牛奶/奶油慢火加热而成的流动型蛋乳酱，也是所有烤制卡仕达（crème brûlée、蛋奶挞）的家族母体。它是甜点岗"温度手感"的试金石，成败全在 3°C 之间。
  • 【怎么做】蛋黄与糖打至发白，热牛奶细流冲入回温（tempering），全程小火/隔水并不断搅拌；用木勺挂背测试：加热至约 82–85°C 酱汁在勺背挂薄层（nappe），指划留痕即离火，立即过细筛入冰水浴降温，贴面盖保鲜膜防结皮，冷藏保存；绝对不超过约 85°C，蛋黄凝固整锅报废（curdled）。
  • 【易错点】大火求快——十秒之差从丝滑变蛋花；没做 tempering 直接把蛋黄倒进热奶烫熟成颗粒；降温不及时余温继续加热同样结块。检查要点：挂勺状态、有无颗粒、冷藏标签。面试关联："custard 结块怎么救"——立即过筛+冰浴，答出体现实战。
Deep Dive
  • What: Crème anglaise — a pourable egg-yolk custard of yolks, sugar and milk/cream cooked gently, and the family mother of all baked custards (crème brûlée, custard tarts). It is the pastry section's litmus test for temperature feel — success lives within about 3°C.
  • How: Whisk yolks and sugar until pale; temper with hot milk in a thin stream; cook over low heat or a bain-marie, stirring constantly; the coat-the-spoon test rules — at about 82–85°C the sauce coats the back of a spoon (nappe) and a finger drawn through leaves a clean line: off the heat at once, strain into an ice bath, press cling film onto the surface and chill. Never exceed about 85°C — the yolks set and the batch curdles.
  • Watch out: Rushing on high heat — seconds separate silky from scrambled; skipping tempering and dumping yolks straight into hot milk, making lumps; cooling too slowly, so residual heat still curdles it. Check: nappe coating, no grains, labeled and chilled. Interview: "How do you fix a curdled custard?" — strain at once and chill over ice; answering this shows hands-on experience.

📍 应用场景：糕点房甜品酱汁与烤制卡仕达制作；技能测试常考项

巧克力调温Chocolate Tempering

深度解析
  • 【是什么】通过精确的升温—降温—回温曲线，让可可脂形成稳定的β-5 晶型的工艺：调温正确的巧克力成品光亮、脆硬、脱模利落、不泛白霜（fat bloom）；调温失败则暗哑发软、沾手。手工巧克力、装饰片、蘸酱（dipping）全部依赖调温。
  • 【怎么做】黑巧克力经典曲线：融化至 45–50°C → 降至 28–29°C（此时析出不稳晶型）→ 回温至 31–32°C 操作温度；牛奶/白巧克力操作温度略低约 29–30°C。两种手法：大理石台面法（tabling，倒三分之二在台面上反复推刮降温再回拌）与 seeding 法（留三分之一的已调温籽晶块拌入熔融巧克力诱导结晶）。全程用巧克力专用温度计监控，湿度要低（水汽会让巧克力"结砂" seize）。
  • 【易错点】水滴入巧克力整锅报废；不测温凭手感——台面上看着光亮、第二天全泛白霜；回温过头超过 32°C 把好晶型又熔掉。检查要点：刀背蘸取 3–5 分钟内凝固光亮即调温成功。面试关联：巧克力岗/甜点岗必考，背出黑巧三段温度即可过关。
Deep Dive
  • What: A precise melt–cool–rewarm process that forces cocoa butter into its stable beta-5 crystal form: well-tempered chocolate sets glossy, snappy and clean-releasing, with no fat bloom; poorly tempered chocolate is dull, soft and sticky. All hand-made chocolates, décor pieces and dipping work depend on it.
  • How: Classic dark-chocolate curve: melt to 45–50°C → cool to 28–29°C (unstable crystals form) → rewarm to the working temperature of 31–32°C; milk and white chocolate work slightly lower at about 29–30°C. Two techniques: tabling — pour two-thirds onto a marble slab, work it back and forth to cool, then return it to the bowl — and seeding — stirring in reserved blocks of already-tempered chocolate to induce the right crystals. Monitor with a chocolate thermometer and keep humidity low (moisture causes seizing).
  • Watch out: One drop of water ruins the whole batch; working "by feel" without a thermometer — glossy today, bloomed tomorrow; rewarming above 32°C and melting away the good crystals. Check: dip a knife tip — if it sets glossy within 3–5 minutes, tempering is right. Interview: mandatory for chocolatier/pastry roles; knowing the dark-chocolate three-stage curve is the pass mark.

📍 应用场景：糕点房手工巧克力与装饰制作；巧克力喷泉/蘸制台

面包发酵Proofing & Fermentation

深度解析
  • 【是什么】酵母面团的双阶段过程：一次发酵（bulk fermentation，面团整体膨发，发展风味与面筋结构）与最后醒发（final proof，整形后入炉前充气，决定成品体积）。面包房出品质量的根源工序，船上大量面包依靠醒发柜（proofer）标准化。
  • 【怎么做】标准条件：温度约 24–27°C、相对湿度约 75–80%；一次发酵至约两倍大（或按配方给时长），排气、分割、滚圆、整形后入醒发柜做最后醒发；判断到位：手指蘸粉戳洞，洞缓慢回弹一半即"醒发刚好"，立即回弹为不足，塌陷不回为过发；也可做 float test（面团入水浮起即熟成）。低温慢发酵（overnight retarding 约 4–10°C）可增风味，是烘焙房排产的常用手段。
  • 【易错点】过发面团入炉塌顶、组织粗酸；醒发不足面包小、皮厚爆裂；醒发柜温度与湿度不记录导致批次不稳。检查要点：戳洞测试、醒发柜温湿表。面试关联："How do you know dough is fully proofed?"——dent test 缓慢回弹是标准答案。
Deep Dive
  • What: The two-stage life of yeast dough: bulk fermentation (the whole dough rises, building flavor and gluten structure) and final proof (shaped dough gas-ups before the oven, setting final volume). The root process of bakery quality — on board, most bread is standardized through a proofer.
  • How: Standard conditions: about 24–27°C and 75–80% relative humidity; bulk-ferment to roughly doubled (or per recipe), then punch down, divide, round, shape and final-proof in the proofer; the dent test decides — flour a finger, poke the dough: slow half-spring-back means perfectly proofed (bake now), instant spring-back means underproofed, collapse means overproofed; the float test (dough floats in water) confirms readiness. Overnight retarding at about 4–10°C builds flavor and helps scheduling.
  • Watch out: Overproofed loaves collapse on entry and bake sour and coarse; underproofed bread stays small with thick, cracked crust; unlogged proofer temperature and humidity make batches inconsistent. Check: dent test, proofer gauges. Interview: "How do you know dough is fully proofed?" — the slow-spring-back dent test is the expected answer.

📍 应用场景：烘焙房每日面包量产；醒发柜操作与排产

五大母酱Five Mother Sauces

深度解析
  • 【是什么】法式经典酱汁体系的五个根基（埃斯科菲耶定型）：白汁 béchamel（牛奶 + 白 roux）、天鹅绒酱 velouté（白高汤 + 浅色 roux）、西班牙酱 espagnole（褐高汤 + 褐 roux，浓缩衍生 demi-glace）、荷兰汁 hollandaise（蛋黄 + 澄清黄油乳化）、番茄酱 sauce tomate。掌握母酱即可按比例衍生数十种小酱（small sauces），是西厨岗位面试的理论压轴题。
  • 【怎么做】roux 基准：等量面粉与黄油炒制，白 roux（奶白色，用于 béchamel/velouté）、褐 roux（坚果褐色，用于 espagnole）；热液体冲入冷 roux 或反之，边加边打防结块，小火搅煮去生粉味（至少 20–30 分钟）；hollandaise 走乳化路线（见黄油乳化条目）；衍生示例：béchamel + 奶酪 = Mornay，velouté + 奶油 = sauce suprême，espagnole + 红酒 = marchand de vin。
  • 【易错点】五个母酱名称与原料张冠李戴（espagnole 配牛奶是常见错答）；roux 没煮透导致酱汁有生面粉味；记忆混乱体现在面试直接出局。检查要点：能默写五母酱的"液体 + 增稠剂"组合表。面试关联：必考题，建议连小酱衍生一起背。
Deep Dive
  • What: The five roots of classical French sauce work (codified by Escoffier): béchamel (milk + white roux), velouté (white stock + light roux), espagnole (brown stock + brown roux, reduced into demi-glace), hollandaise (yolks emulsified with clarified butter), and sauce tomate. Master the mothers and dozens of small sauces follow by proportion — the theoretical finale of most Western galley interviews.
  • How: Roux baseline: equal parts flour and butter cooked out — white roux (creamy, for béchamel/velouté) and brown roux (nut-brown, for espagnole); add hot liquid to cold roux (or the reverse), whisking as you go to prevent lumps, then simmer gently at least 20–30 minutes to cook out the floury taste; hollandaise follows the emulsion route (see the Emulsification entry); derivations: béchamel + cheese = Mornay, velouté + cream = sauce suprême, espagnole + red wine = marchand de vin.
  • Watch out: Mixing up which mother pairs with which liquid — pairing espagnole with milk is the classic wrong answer; undercooked roux leaves a raw-flour taste; fumbling this in an interview ends the conversation. Check: be able to write out the "liquid + thickener" table for all five from memory. Interview: a guaranteed question — learn the small-sauce derivations too.

📍 应用场景：酱汁档日常制备与衍生小酱；厨师岗位面试理论题

高汤Stocks

深度解析
  • 【是什么】厨房的"液体资产"：白高汤（white stock，鸡/小牛骨汆水去杂后冷水起煮）、褐高汤（brown stock，骨头与蔬菜先烤上色再煮）与鱼高汤（fish stock，鱼骨快煮）。汤底、酱汁、烩饭全部从高汤出发，是评估一家厨房水准的第一指标。
  • 【怎么做】白高汤冷水下料、缓慢升温（蛋白质杂质随水温析出便于撇净）；褐高汤骨头先 200°C 左右烤箱烤至深褐再煮；全程微沸（simmer, never boil）——沸腾会把脂肪与杂质打进汤里变浑浊，并不停撇沫（skim）；时长：鸡骨 3–4 小时、牛骨 6–8 小时、鱼骨仅 30–45 分钟（久煮发苦）；蔬菜料（mirepoix：洋葱、胡萝卜、西芹 2:1:1）按汤种选择加入时机，最后过细筛保持清澈（clarity），快速冷却后贴标冷藏。
  • 【易错点】大火猛煮追求速度——汤浑油浊，只能降级使用；鱼汤煮 2 小时苦涩报废；没有撇沫也没有过滤；熬好的汤在室温晾凉过夜（严重违规，违反冷却规范）。检查要点：清澈度、冷却流程、标签日期。面试关联："white stock 与 brown stock 区别"是基础题，答"骨头是否先烤上色"。
Deep Dive
  • What: The kitchen's "liquid assets": white stock (chicken/veal bones blanched, then started in cold water), brown stock (bones and vegetables roasted to color first) and fish stock (quick-cooked fish bones). Soups, sauces and risotto all begin here — the first thing that tells you how good a galley is.
  • How: White stock starts in cold water and heats slowly, so impurities surface and can be skimmed; for brown stock, roast bones at about 200°C until deep brown first; keep everything at a simmer, never a boil — boiling emulsifies fat and debris and clouds the stock, so skim constantly; timing: chicken bones 3–4 hours, beef bones 6–8 hours, fish bones only 30–45 minutes (longer turns bitter); add the mirepoix (onion, carrot, celery at 2:1:1) per stock type, strain finely at the end for clarity, chill fast and label.
  • Watch out: Hard boiling to save time — a cloudy, greasy stock demoted to second use; fish stock simmered for two hours into bitterness; no skimming or straining; leaving finished stock to cool at room temperature overnight (a serious violation of cooling rules). Check: clarity, cooling procedure, labeled dates. Interview: "white vs brown stock" is a basic question — "whether the bones are roasted first".

📍 应用场景：汤锅档/酱汁档每日熬制；母酱与烩饭的基底供应

油醋汁比例Vinaigrette

深度解析
  • 【是什么】沙拉汁的基本公式：经典法式比例 3:1——三份油对一份酸（红酒醋 red wine vinegar、雪莉醋 sherry vinegar、巴萨米克 balsamic 或柠檬汁），按口味可调至 2:1（更酸爽）或 4:1（更圆润）。冷厨与自助沙拉台每天都在批量出品，比例感是冷厨岗基本功。
  • 【怎么做】基准配方：油 3 : 醋 1 + 第戎芥末（Dijon，约每 100 ml 酱 5–8 g 作乳化剂）+ 盐、黑胡椒，可选蒜末、小葱、蜂蜜；摇瓶或搅打至乳白暂时乳化；酸度先用少量直接点在生菜上试口再调整；大规模出品按桶配并贴标注明品名与日期，冷藏存放（橄榄油低温凝固属正常，回温摇匀即复）。
  • 【易错点】比例靠感觉随缘——同一天两批沙拉味道不同；忘记加芥末导致彻底不乳化、油醋分层上桌；用"口味重"的红酒醋原液直接浇菜。检查要点：摇匀后 1 分钟内无明显分层为乳化合格；使用前再次摇匀（shake before serving）。面试关联："What's a classic vinaigrette ratio?"——"Three parts oil to one part vinegar" 一句话拿分。
Deep Dive
  • What: The fundamental dressing formula: the classic French ratio is 3:1 — three parts oil to one part acid (red wine vinegar, sherry vinegar, balsamic or lemon juice), adjustable to 2:1 for sharper or 4:1 for rounder. Pantry and buffet salad stations batch it daily; ratio feel is a core pantry skill.
  • How: Baseline: 3 parts oil : 1 part vinegar + Dijon mustard (about 5–8 g per 100 ml as emulsifier) + salt and black pepper, optionally garlic, chives or honey; shake or whisk to a temporary milky emulsion; taste by dotting a little on a lettuce leaf before adjusting acidity; for volume production, batch into containers labeled with name and date, and refrigerate (olive oil solidifying when chilled is normal — rewarm and shake).
  • Watch out: Eyeballing the ratio — two salads from the same day taste different; skipping the mustard leaves a totally unemulsified, oily layer on the plate; pouring harsh vinegar straight onto greens undiluted. Check: after shaking, no visible separation within a minute means a good emulsion; always shake before serving. Interview: "What's a classic vinaigrette ratio?" — "Three parts oil to one part vinegar" scores on the spot.

📍 应用场景：冷厨（pantry/garde manger）沙拉台与自助餐厅批量调汁

勾芡增稠Thickening

深度解析
  • 【是什么】酱汁、汤羹获得稠度的技术体系，主要三法：油面酱 roux（等量面粉与黄油炒制，分白/浅褐/褐三度）、玉米淀粉水 cornstarch slurry（淀粉与冷水 1:1 调开）、liaison（蛋黄与奶油混合的离火增稠）。法餐以 roux 为正统，快节奏出餐常用 slurry。
  • 【怎么做】roux：小火炒制并按目标色停手（白 roux 约 2–3 分钟、褐 roux 可达 20 分钟以上），热汤冲入搅打，之后至少微沸 20 分钟去生粉味；slurry：淀粉冷水调匀后细流冲入微沸汤中，边加边搅——必须煮沸才完全透亮增稠（淀粉糊化），且忌长时间反复沸腾（会回稀变水）；liaison：蛋黄与奶油调匀后离火加入，缓缓搅打，汤温不得超过约 85°C 否则结块；量上宁少勿多、可再补。
  • 【易错点】slurry 直接撒干粉进汤结成疙瘩；增稠后不煮透，酱汁有生粉味且发黏；liaison 离火不够直接结蛋花；一次加过量无法回头。检查要点：挂勺度（nappe）、透明度、有无颗粒。面试关联：可与五大母酱联动作答，展示体系理解。
Deep Dive
  • What: The technical toolkit for giving sauces and soups body, in three main forms: roux (equal parts flour and butter cooked, in white/light/brown stages), cornstarch slurry (starch mixed 1:1 with cold water) and liaison (yolks and cream, added off the heat). Classical French cuisine anchors on roux; fast-paced service leans on slurry.
  • How: Roux: cook gently and stop at the target color (white about 2–3 minutes, brown up to 20+), whisk hot stock in, then simmer at least 20 minutes to cook out the flour taste; slurry: stream the cold slurry into a simmering liquid while stirring — it must come back to a boil to gelatinize fully and turn glossy, and avoid prolonged re-boiling, which thins it back out; liaison: blend yolks with cream, add off the heat, whisk in slowly, keeping the sauce below about 85°C or it scrambles; dose conservatively — you can always add more.
  • Watch out: Dumping dry starch straight into soup — instant lumps; not cooking the sauce out, leaving a raw-starchy taste and gluey texture; adding liaison without enough off-heat time, scrambling the yolks; over-thickening with no way back. Check: nappe coating, gloss, no grains. Interview: link this to the five mother sauces to show systematic understanding.

📍 应用场景：酱汁档/汤锅档浓度控制；自助餐厅大锅酱汁现调

黄油乳化Emulsification

深度解析
  • 【是什么】让互不相溶的油与水稳定共存的技艺，黄油系代表作品：荷兰汁 hollandaise（蛋黄包裹澄清黄油）与 beurre blanc（白葡萄酒底乳化黄油）。乳化酱汁是热厨晋升考核的经典项目，也最能暴露基本功。
  • 【怎么做】荷兰汁：蛋黄 + 水/柠檬汁隔水打至 sabayon 稠厚（约 65–70°C），离火后澄清黄油（clarified butter）温热约 50–60°C、细流加入并全程持续搅打，盐与辣椒汁（cayenne）调味，保温在 60°C 左右水浴（过热继续变熟、过冷则凝固）；一旦破乳（sauce splits，油水分离呈油渣状）：另取一颗蛋黄置干净盆中，将破乳酱汁一小勺一小勺缓缓打入新蛋黄重新乳化（re-emulsify）。
  • 【易错点】黄油倒太快一次一大勺——瞬间破乳；黄油过热（超过约 65°C）烫熟蛋黄；保温直接坐火上持续加热必结块。检查要点：酱汁光泽如丝缎、挂勺不流油。面试关联："你的 hollandaise 破了怎么办"是热厨岗经典实操题，答"new yolk, whisk it in slowly"。
Deep Dive
  • What: The craft of stabilizing oil and water that naturally repel each other; the butter-based masterpieces are hollandaise (yolks enveloping clarified butter) and beurre blanc (butter emulsified into a white-wine base). Emulsion sauces are a classic promotion test on the hot line — and the fastest way to expose weak fundamentals.
  • How: Hollandaise: whisk yolks with water or lemon juice over a bain-marie to a thick sabayon (about 65–70°C); off the heat, stream in warm clarified butter at about 50–60°C in a thin thread, whisking constantly; season with salt and cayenne; hold in a water bath around 60°C — hotter cooks it, colder sets it. If the sauce splits (oily, grainy separation): start with a fresh yolk in a clean bowl and whisk the broken sauce into it a spoonful at a time to re-emulsify.
  • Watch out: Adding butter in big spoonfuls — instant split; butter too hot (above about 65°C) scrambling the yolks; holding the sauce directly over heat, which always curdles it. Check: silky sheen, coats the spoon without weeping oil. Interview: "Your hollandaise split — what do you do?" is a classic hot-line question — "new yolk, whisk it in slowly".

📍 应用场景：热厨早班早午餐班次荷兰汁量产；晋升实操考核

复合黄油Compound Butter / Beurre Blanc

深度解析
  • 【是什么】两种黄油技法：复合黄油（compound butter）——软化黄油拌入香草、蒜末、柠檬皮等后冷藏成型，切抹在热牛排、烤鱼上即化即香（如 café de Paris 黄油、香草黄油）；白黄油汁（beurre blanc）——法国卢瓦尔河谷经典乳化酱，白葡萄酒与葱头收汁后乳化大量冷黄油而成。
  • 【怎么做】复合黄油：黄油软化至可搅拌（约 20°C 不可融化），配料碎末拌匀，油纸卷成圆柱冷藏定型（可冷冻分切），切 2 cm 厚片趁热菜上桌；beurre blanc：shallot 碎 + 白葡萄酒 + 白酒醋收汁至约 1–2 汤匙，离火微温状态下分次（每 2–3 块）加入冷黄油块并用力搅打/旋锅乳化，总量约为收汁液的 4–6 倍黄油量，酱汁须温热（约 55–60°C）即用——高温久煮必破乳，不适合提前保温过久。
  • 【易错点】复合黄油配料带水（如未沥干的香草）导致冷藏变质与冻结后冰晶；beurre blanc 放在热汤锅上保温 20 分钟后必然油水分离；用加盐黄油做 beurre blanc 咸度失控。检查要点：复合黄油切面紧致无水痕；beurre blanc 光亮不泛油光。面试关联：可结合"你为牛排做过什么酱"讲 compound butter，故事性与专业性兼得。
Deep Dive
  • What: Two butter techniques: compound butter — softened butter blended with herbs, garlic, lemon zest and more, then chilled in a log and melted over hot steaks or fish (café de Paris butter, herb butter); and beurre blanc — the Loire Valley classic emulsion of butter whisked into a reduced white-wine base.
  • How: Compound butter: soften butter to spreadable (about 20°C, never melted), mix in finely chopped additions, roll in parchment into a log, chill to set (or freeze and slice), and cut 2 cm pats onto hot plates; beurre blanc: reduce minced shallots with white wine and a splash of vinegar to 1–2 tablespoons, then off direct heat add cold butter cubes a few at a time, whisking or swirling hard to emulsify — total butter roughly 4–6 times the reduction — and serve warm at about 55–60°C; prolonged heat always splits it, so it does not hold.
  • Watch out: Watery additions (un-drained herbs) cause spoilage and ice crystals in the frozen log; beurre blanc held over a hot well for 20 minutes separates into oil; salted butter makes the seasoning uncontrollable. Check: compound butter slices firm and water-free; beurre blanc glossy, not greasy. Interview: "What sauce have you made for steak?" — a compound-butter story lands both professionalism and personality.

📍 应用场景：热厨牛排/烤鱼出餐增值酱；冷厨香草黄油备制

酒吧部Bar / Beverage Department8 个术语▼

标准配方Standard Recipe / Spec Sheet

深度解析
  • 【是什么】酒单上每一杯酒的用量（如金酒 45ml）、指定品牌（如 Bombay Sapphire）、杯型（Highball / Martini / Rocks）与装饰物都以 spec sheet 形式固定，是出品一致性和酒水成本控制的核心文件。
  • 【怎么做】出酒前先对照 spec：用量以 jigger 量取，禁止"凭手感倒"。
  • 【怎么做】品牌缺货时须上报酒吧经理，不得擅自替换同类酒。
  • 【怎么做】装饰物（柠檬角、橄榄、樱桃）与杯型按 spec 逐项执行，出品前后核对一遍。
  • 【易错点】自由手倒（free pour）导致每杯差异大，酒水成本（通常要求控制在 22%–28%）失守，盘点时酒与销售单对不上。
Deep Dive
  • What: Every drink on the menu has a fixed spec — measured pours (e.g. 45 ml gin), specified brands, glassware and garnish — the backbone of consistency and beverage cost control.
  • How: Always jigger-measure to spec; never free pour.
  • How: If a listed brand is out of stock, report to the bar manager instead of substituting silently.
  • How: Execute garnish and glassware exactly as specced and double-check before serving.
  • Watch out: Free pouring creates inconsistent drinks and pushes liquor cost (typically 22%–28% target) off budget; inventory will not reconcile with sales.

📍 应用场景：按酒单出酒、新品培训与考核、月度酒水盘点、成本核算

六大基酒Six Base Spirits

深度解析
  • 【是什么】鸡尾酒体系的六大基酒：Vodka（中性，Moscow Mule / Bloody Mary）、Gin（杜松子草本，Gin & Tonic / Negroni）、Rum（甘蔗甜香，Mojito / Piña Colada）、Tequila（龙舌蓝草香，Margarita）、Whisky（谷物桶陈，Old Fashioned / Whisky Sour）、Brandy（果香蒸馏，Sidecar / B&B）。
  • 【怎么做】背下每支基酒的风味关键词、典型搭配与代表酒款，客人点"经典款"时能立即对应到正确基酒与杯型。
  • 【怎么做】向客人推荐时按风味描述（清爽/烟熏/花香）而非仅报品牌名。
  • 【易错点】把 Tequila 与 Mezcal 混为一谈，或用 Whisky 摇出 Mojito 等张冠李戴；开错基酒等于报废一杯成本。
Deep Dive
  • What: The six base spirits: Vodka (neutral — Moscow Mule, Bloody Mary), Gin (botanical — G&T, Negroni), Rum (sugarcane — Mojito, Piña Colada), Tequila (agave — Margarita), Whisky (oak-aged — Old Fashioned, Whisky Sour), Brandy (fruit-forward — Sidecar, B&B).
  • How: Memorize each spirit's flavor profile, classic pairings and signature cocktails so classic orders map instantly to the right base and glass.
  • How: Recommend by flavor descriptors (crisp, smoky, floral), not just brand names.
  • Watch out: Confusing Tequila with Mezcal, or building a Mojito on whisky — a mis-poured base spirit means a wasted pour and lost cost.

📍 应用场景：酒单设计、客人点酒推荐、新员工基酒培训、盘点分区（按基酒归类酒窖）

摇和与调和Shake vs Stir

深度解析
  • 【是什么】两种核心混合手法：含果汁、蛋清、奶油等原料的酒用摇和（Shake，波士顿摇酒壶，10–15 秒用力摇至金属壶身结霜）；纯酒精度高的酒（Martini、Manhattan、Negroni）用调和（Stir，吧勺在搅拌杯中旋转搅 20–30 秒）。
  • 【怎么做】摇和：装料加冰 → 双手扣紧壶身 → 剧烈摇 10–15 秒 → 双层滤冰入杯。
  • 【怎么做】调和：加冰搅拌杯 → 吧勺沿杯壁匀速搅 20–30 秒 → Julep 滤冰器滤入冰镇杯。
  • 【易错点】摇过度导致冰融化过多、酒体稀薄（over-dilution）；该 Stir 的 Martini 被摇晃后会浑浊且口感变差；滤网破损导致冰渣入杯。
Deep Dive
  • What: Two core mixing methods: Shake (Boston shaker, 10–15 seconds hard shake until frost forms) for drinks with juice, egg white or cream; Stir (bar spoon, 20–30 seconds in a mixing glass) for spirit-forward drinks like Martini, Manhattan, Negroni.
  • How: Shake — build with ice, seal both hands, shake hard 10–15 seconds, double-strain into the glass.
  • How: Stir — ice in mixing glass, rotate bar spoon steadily 20–30 seconds, strain with a Julep strainer into a chilled glass.
  • Watch out: Over-shaking over-dilutes and waters the drink down; a shaken Martini turns cloudy and flat; a worn strainer lets ice chips into the glass.

📍 应用场景：经典鸡尾酒出品、出品手法培训、酒品口感投诉排查、吧台效率提升

酒水申领单Bar Requisition

深度解析
  • 【是什么】酒吧向船上酒窖/仓库领取酒水和物料的正式单据，写明品名、规格、数量、申领部门与日期，是库存流转和成本归集的凭证。
  • 【怎么做】① 盘点后按销售预估填写申领单；② 酒吧经理签字 → 酒窖主管复核签字（双签核验）；③ 领货时逐件核对品名与数量、检查酒瓶完好与封口；④ 回吧台后将实际入库数量登记进库存台账。
  • 【易错点】单据只有一签即发货；领货不看保质期和瓶身破损；实际领到数量与单面不符却未在单上更正，导致月末盘点差异（variance）无法解释。
Deep Dive
  • What: The formal document bars use to draw stock from the ship's cellar/store — item, size, quantity, requesting department and date; the audit trail for inventory movement and cost posting.
  • How: Fill the requisition after stocktake based on sales forecast.
  • How: Obtain bar manager's signature and cellar keeper's countersign (two-signature check) before issue.
  • How: Verify each item's name, quantity and bottle integrity/seals on pickup, then post actual receipts into the stock ledger.
  • Watch out: Issuing stock on a single signature; ignoring expiry dates or damaged bottles; not correcting quantity discrepancies on the document — these create unexplained month-end variance.

📍 应用场景：每日/每周酒水补货、航次前备货、月末盘点对账、成本审计

酒吧开吧与备货Bar Setup / Mise en Place

深度解析
  • 【是什么】营业前 30–60 分钟按点位清单把一切备齐：冰（冰桶/冰铲就位）、杯具（按酒单杯型码放并检查水渍指纹）、装饰台（柠檬角、青柠、橄榄、樱桃、薄荷）、常备酒水（常用基酒上 work station、配酒如糖浆/苦精/Bitters 就位）、吧布与垃圾袋。
  • 【怎么做】① 按开吧清单（opening checklist）逐项打钩；② 杯具用抛光布过一遍，杯口朝下放置；③ 装饰物切好后覆盖保鲜膜并冷藏，标注切配时间；④ 开吧完成后由当班主管验收签字。
  • 【易错点】高峰期才发现冰桶是空的、Popular 杯型不够；装饰物提前几小时切好氧化变色；收吧时忘记关酒水申领需求导致次日缺货。
Deep Dive
  • What: Prepping everything 30–60 minutes before service per a station checklist: ice (buckets and scoops in place), glassware (staged by menu glass, polished), garnish tray (lemon/lime wedges, olives, cherries, mint), well bottles at the work station, mixes like syrups and bitters, bar towels and liners.
  • How: Run the opening checklist item by item.
  • How: Polish glassware and store rim-down; cut garnishes, cover and refrigerate with prep time labeled.
  • How: Have the duty supervisor inspect and sign off the setup before doors open.
  • Watch out: Discovering empty ice buckets or missing popular glassware mid-rush; garnish cut hours ahead turning brown; forgetting to file the requisition at closing, causing next-day stockouts.

📍 应用场景：每日开吧流程、新员工岗位培训、高峰期出酒效率、主管巡检

USPH 酒吧卫生USPH Bar Sanitation

深度解析
  • 【是什么】美国公共卫生服务（USPH）对邮轮酒吧的强制卫生规范，覆盖冰、杯具、装饰物、砧板与个人卫生，卫生检查不合格可导致停业整改。
  • 【怎么做】① 冰必须用冰铲/夹子取，冰铲不用时柄朝下存放，禁止徒手或用杯子舀冰；② 拿杯只能抓杯柄/杯底，手指不得触碰杯口和杯内壁；③ 生熟砧板分色使用（如红色生肉、绿色蔬果、白色熟食），刀具随板走；④ 装饰物冷藏保存，容器标注开盒日期与时间；⑤ 手部有伤口或患病须立即报医并调岗。
  • 【易错点】把冰铲插在冰里（柄部污染冰面）；用手抓橄榄装饰；生肉汁污染砧板后继续切柠檬角——这些是 USPH 检查中最常见的扣分项。
Deep Dive
  • What: Mandatory US Public Health Service rules for cruise bars covering ice, glassware, garnish, cutting boards and personal hygiene; a failed USPH inspection can shut the bar down for remediation.
  • How: Scoop ice only with a scoop/tongs stored handle-up — never bare hands or a glass.
  • How: Handle glassware by stem or base; fingers must never touch the rim or inside surface.
  • How: Use color-coded cutting boards (e.g. red raw meat, green produce, white cooked food), knife travels with its board.
  • How: Keep garnishes refrigerated with opened-on date/time labeled; report wounds or illness to medical and be reassigned immediately.
  • Watch out: Sticking the scoop into the ice (handle contaminates the surface), grabbing olives by hand, or cutting lemon wedges on a raw-meat-contaminated board — the most-cited USPH violations.

📍 应用场景：USPH 突击检查、每日开吧卫生自查、新员工卫生培训、装饰台与冰站管理

责任饮酒RSA (Responsible Service of Alcohol)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】依法依规地向客人售酒的责任制度：不向未满 21 岁（美国航线标准）者售酒，不向已醉酒者续杯，对醉酒客人承担照护与安全责任。
  • 【怎么做】① 识别醉酒征兆：言语含糊、站立不稳、情绪失控、反复点酒；② 发现后礼貌拒售："I'm sorry, I can't serve you another drink right now. Can I get you some water instead?"；③ 换上水或软饮并提供小食；④ 通知保安/酒吧经理并做书面记录（时间、地点、征兆、处理方式）；⑤ 疑似未成年客人查带照片证件（护照/登船证）。
  • 【易错点】碍于小费不好意思拒售；只记次数不看状态；拒售时语气冲突激化矛盾；未留下书面记录，事后纠纷无法举证。
Deep Dive
  • What: The duty to serve alcohol responsibly: no alcohol to guests under 21 (US itinerary standard), no refills to visibly intoxicated guests, and duty of care for their safety.
  • How: Spot intoxication signs: slurred speech, unsteady stance, mood swings, repeated ordering.
  • How: Refuse politely: "I'm sorry, I can't serve you another drink right now. Can I get you some water instead?"
  • How: Offer water or a soft drink with a snack; notify security/bar manager and log the incident (time, place, signs, action taken).
  • How: Check photo ID (passport/ship card) for anyone who may be underage.
  • Watch out: Skipping refusal to protect tips; counting drinks without observing condition; a confrontational tone when refusing; no written record to back you up if a dispute arises.

📍 应用场景：21 岁以下查验、醉酒客人处置、夜间高峰期监管、安全事件记录

酒水推销与 UpsellUpselling Drinks

深度解析
  • 【是什么】在客人点单时主动、得体地推荐更高价位或特色酒水，以提升客单价与人均酒水消费（revenue per guest），是酒吧部核心业绩指标。
  • 【怎么做】① 点单瞬间是最佳时机：客人说"来杯威士忌"时问 "Would you like to try our premium single malt? It's our bartender's pick tonight."；② 推荐双份升级："Shall we make it a double for just $4 more?"；③ 结合套餐/畅饮包（drink package）与当日特饮推荐；④ 首杯上桌 3–5 分钟后回访，顺势推荐第二杯或餐后酒。
  • 【易错点】一味推销引起反感；推荐与客人口味不符的高端酒（如给不喝甜的人推利口酒）；忘记报价格导致结账争议；对已订畅饮包的客人重复推销单点。
Deep Dive
  • What: Proactively and tactfully suggesting premium or signature drinks at the point of order to lift average spend (revenue per guest) — a core bar revenue metric.
  • How: Strike at order time: when the guest asks for a whisky, offer "Would you like to try our premium single malt? It's our bartender's pick tonight."
  • How: Offer the double upgrade: "Shall we make it a double for just $4 more?"
  • How: Tie suggestions to drink packages and the daily special; check back 3–5 minutes after the first round to suggest a second round or a digestif.
  • Watch out: Pushy upselling that annoys guests; mismatched premium suggestions (a sweet liqueur to a dry-palate guest); quoting no price and causing bill disputes; upselling à-la-carte to guests already on a drink package.

📍 应用场景：点单推荐、双份/高端酒升级、畅饮包推广、首轮回访二次销售

冷萃咖啡 Cold Brew粗研磨粉 1:8–1:10（粉水比）冷水浸泡 12–18 小时，出品兑水/奶按 1:1–1:2 稀释高玻璃杯加冰 350–450 ml / 12–16 oz全程冷藏萃取、过滤两遍；酸苦更低、口感顺滑回甘冷萃拿铁、氮气冷萃 Nitro（气泡绵密）、加汤力水做 Espresso Tonic 风味
阿芙佳朵 Affogato1 份热 espresso 浇在一球香草冰淇淋（约 60–70 g）上甜品杯/玻璃碗，150–180 ml现萃热咖啡即点即浇，上桌即刻享用，避免冰淇淋完全融化加 Frangelico/Amaretto 等坚果利口酒、撒可可脆片或碎坚果
爱尔兰咖啡 Irish Coffee1 份浓缩或浓热咖啡 120 ml + Irish Whiskey 40 ml + 红糖 1–2 茶匙，顶层轻打发冷奶油浮面耐热爱尔兰咖啡杯 240 ml / 8 oz糖先融于热咖啡与威士忌，奶油沿勺背缓缓浮面不搅拌，热饮冷奶油对照口感BAILEYS 版本、苏格兰威士忌版（Gaelic Coffee）、去酒精版（改糖浆风味糖）

配方为国际咖啡业通用标准（SCA 参考值），船上以本船咖啡操作手册与菜单为准。

娱乐部14 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

每日节目单Daily Program

深度解析
  • 【是什么】每日节目单（Daily Program，各船司品牌名如 Carnival 的 Fun Times、Princess 的 Princess Patter）是按航次、按天发布的全船活动总表，英文名 Daily Program / Cruise Compass（Royal Caribbean），是宾客安排一天的唯一权威参考，也是娱乐部的工作日程表。
  • 【怎么做】内容含每场活动的名称、时间、地点与适龄提示；纸质版每日夜间送客舱、电子版在船司 App（Royal Caribbean 的 Navigator、MSC 的 For Me 等）实时更新（时间地点变更会推送）；娱乐员工须在每日班前会上熟记当日全部场次——通常 10–20 场——做到宾客随口问「今天几点有 trivia」时秒答时间与地点；临时取消或换场地须第一时间口头告知现场宾客。
  • 【易错点】错误：只记自己主持的场次（宾客问别的活动答不上）、节目单改版后仍按旧时间引导、忘记向宾客说明部分活动需提前报名（如 Solstice 级的才艺课）。检查要点：每天核对当日版节目单与内部排班表一致性；面试常问「你怎么记住那么多场次」——答班前会背表+随身抄小卡。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Daily Program — branded as Carnival's Fun Times, Princess Patter, or Royal Caribbean's Cruise Compass — is the day-by-day master schedule of all shipboard activities, published per voyage. It's the guests' single authoritative plan for the day and the entertainment team's own working agenda.
  • How: It lists each activity's name, time, venue, and age suitability. Paper copies are delivered to cabins nightly; the electronic version updates in real time on the line's app (Royal Caribbean's Navigator, MSC's For Me), with push alerts for changes. Cruise Staff must memorize the full day at the morning briefing — typically 10–20 events — so they can instantly answer "what time is trivia today?" with time and venue. Any cancellation or venue change is announced to guests in person first.
  • Watch out: Only knowing your own events (guests ask about everything), guiding guests by yesterday's schedule after a revision, and forgetting to mention that some activities require pre-registration. Cross-check the day's printed program against the internal roster daily. Interview favorite: "How do you remember so many events?" — briefing memorization plus a pocket cheat card.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部（Cruise Staff / Entertainment）每日班前会、现场引导与宾客咨询。

甲板游戏Deck / Pool Games

深度解析
  • 【是什么】甲板游戏（Deck/Pool Games）是 Cruise Staff 在泳池甲板等露天区域组织的大众参与型竞技活动，如木桶赛（tub race）、 trivia 问答、洗澡水大赛（belly flop / pool trivia）、雪糕吃比赛等，英文名 Deck Games / Pool Games，是邮轮日间娱乐的核心与 Cruise Staff 的标志性日常工作。
  • 【怎么做】标准流程：提前 10–15 分钟到场布置道具与音响 → 手持无线麦克风用高能量开场（"Come on over, we're starting in two minutes!"）→ 讲清规则（不超过 60 秒）→ 分组组织、控节奏 → 颁发小奖品并感谢参与；一场约 20–45 分钟；同时执行安全职责：提醒湿滑甲板（wet deck）、游戏期间留意泳池安全，必要时配合救生员（lifeguard）暂停活动。
  • 【易错点】错误：规则没讲清导致现场混乱、只顾气氛忘了的安全（宾客在池边奔跑）、冷场时不会救场（要准备备用小游戏与互动话术）、音量过大干扰休息区宾客。检查要点：每场活动结束清点道具归位；面试常问「怎么让害羞的宾客参与」——答开放式邀请+示范参与，不点名施压。
Deep Dive
  • What: Deck/Pool Games are mass-participation competitions run by Cruise Staff on the pool deck and open decks — tub races, trivia, belly-flop contests, ice-cream eating races, and more. They are the heart of daytime entertainment and the signature daily work of a Cruise Staff member.
  • How: Standard flow: arrive 10–15 minutes early to set up props and sound; open with high energy on a wireless handheld mic ("Come on over — we're starting in two minutes!"); explain rules in under 60 seconds; organize teams and keep the pace up; award small prizes and thank participants. Each session runs 20–45 minutes. Safety duties run in parallel: warn about the wet deck, watch pool safety throughout, and pause games if the lifeguard calls it.
  • Watch out: Rushing the rules into on-deck chaos, losing safety focus while hyping the crowd, freezing when energy dips (keep backup mini-games and call-outs ready), and over-amplifying into quiet zones. Count props back in after every session. Interview favorite: "How do you get shy guests involved?" — open invitations and lead by joining in, never singling anyone out.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Cruise Staff 海上日的泳池甲板时段（通常 10:00–17:00 多场次连排）。

儿童俱乐部Kids Club / Youth Program

深度解析
  • 【是什么】儿童俱乐部（Kids Club / Youth Program）是按年龄分级的儿童托管与活动体系，各船司品牌不同：Royal Caribbean 的 Adventure Ocean、Carnival 的 Camp Ocean、Norwegian 的 Splash Academy、Disney 的 Oceaneer Club，英文名统称 Kids Club / Youth Program，由 Youth Staff 运营，与 Cruise Staff 同属娱乐部大类。
  • 【怎么做】常见年龄分组 3–5 / 6–8 / 9–12 岁及青少年组（13–17 岁，多为自由进出）；执行严格签到签退（sign-in/sign-out）制度：家长登记并取得接领凭证，离营须凭凭证与船卡接领，监护人不符不放人；师生比按年龄执行（幼龄组通常 1:8–1:15，以船司政策为准）；每日活动含手工、寻宝、科学实验与主题派对，营业时间通常 9:00–22:00 分段开放；开放后区域消毒频次每日多次。
  • 【易错点】错误：让家长以外的熟人接孩子（必须凭证件+登记名单）、单独带一名儿童离开活动区（两人规则，two-deep rule）、超员接收。检查要点：签到表随身可查、点名频次按场次执行；面试常问「家长晚来接孩子怎么办」——答按规程继续看护并逐级上报，绝不单独送回客舱。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Kids Club (Youth Program) is the age-banded childcare and activity system, branded per line: Royal Caribbean's Adventure Ocean, Carnival's Camp Ocean, Norwegian's Splash Academy, Disney's Oceaneer Club — collectively Kids Club / Youth Program, run by Youth Staff within the entertainment division.
  • How: Typical bands are 3–5, 6–8, and 9–12, plus a teen group (13–17, usually free access). Strict sign-in/sign-out applies: parents register and receive a pick-up credential; children leave only to an authorized adult presenting the credential and cruise card. Staff-to-child ratios follow age bands (roughly 1:8–1:15 for younger groups, per line policy). Daily programming spans crafts, scavenger hunts, science experiments, and theme parties; the club typically opens 9:00–22:00 in sessions, with surfaces sanitized several times a day.
  • Watch out: Releasing a child to a family friend not on the registration list, taking one child out of the area alone (two-deep rule), and accepting kids over capacity. Keep the sign-in sheet on hand and run roll calls per session. Interview favorite: "What if a parent is late for pick-up?" — keep supervising per procedure, escalate, and never escort the child back to the cabin alone.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Youth Staff 儿童托管运营、家庭宾客接待与亲子活动。

广播寻呼PA Announcement

深度解析
  • 【是什么】PA 广播（Public Address Announcement）是通过全船广播系统（PA system）向宾客或船员播送通知的方式，英文名 PA Announcement；全船广播是官方沟通渠道，须按标准格式播报，禁止随意插播个人化内容。
  • 【怎么做】标准三段式格式：称呼（"May I have your attention, please" 或 "Attention all guests"）+ 定位/事由 + 内容与行动指引；播报前写下稿子、语速放慢、关键信息（时间/地点）重复两遍；儿童寻呼走专用码——如 Disney 的 Mike Call（"Mike call for [code name]"），用代号保护儿童姓名与位置信息不外泄；紧急广播有专用词汇（如 Code Alpha 为医疗急呼，各船司代码不同），非授权人员不得启用紧急频道。
  • 【易错点】错误：广播里直接念儿童全名与所在地点（安全隐患）、口误读错时间地点、随意插播打断官方通知。检查要点：所有寻呼须先经广播台或值班主管核准；面试常问「为什么儿童寻呼要用代号」——答防止陌生人获知儿童身份与位置。
Deep Dive
  • What: A PA Announcement is a notice delivered ship-wide through the public address system. The PA is an official communication channel: announcements follow a set format, and personal or off-script inserts are prohibited.
  • How: Use the three-part format — greeting ("May I have your attention, please" / "Attention all guests"), location/purpose, then the content and the action required. Write the script beforehand, slow your pace, and repeat key details (time, venue) twice. Child page calls use dedicated codes — Disney's Mike Call, for example — so a child's name and location are never broadcast openly. Emergency channels carry their own code words (e.g., Code Alpha for a medical emergency on many lines) and may only be used by authorized personnel.
  • Watch out: Reading a child's full name and location over the PA (a safety risk), fluffing times or venues, and cutting into official announcements. Every page must be cleared by the bridge radio room or the duty officer first. Interview favorite: "Why do child pages use code names?" — so strangers can't learn a child's identity or whereabouts.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Cruise Staff 活动召集、寻人寻呼与儿童专用广播码使用。

紧急情况职责Emergency Duties (Cruise Staff)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】紧急情况职责（Emergency Duties）指娱乐部员工在应急状态下的第二角色——按 SOLAS 要求每名船员都在应急部署表（Muster List）中有明确岗位，Cruise Staff 平时是演员主持，紧急时是宾客安抚与疏散引导员，英文名 Emergency Duties / Crisis Role。
  • 【怎么做】警报响起（七短一长鸣笛为弃船警报通用信号）后：立即穿上救生衣与工作服、按 Muster List 到达指定站位；主要职能：用双语安抚宾客（"Stay calm, follow me, please"）、引导宾客沿疏散路线到集合站（Muster Station）、维持排队秩序防拥挤、支援集合站清点人数（headcount）与特殊需求宾客（老人、儿童、轮椅）协助；每周/每航次参加弃船（abandon ship）与消防（fire drill）演习并签到。
  • 【易错点】错误：听到警报先去收拾私人物品、在走廊逆人流方向跑、对宾客说 "Don't worry" 空话而不给行动指令（应给明确指令如 "Follow me to your muster station"）。检查要点：演习签到不得缺勤；面试必问「你的紧急职责是什么」——答出 Muster List 站位+安抚+引导三件套。
Deep Dive
  • What: Emergency Duties are the entertainment staff's second role in a crisis. Under SOLAS every crew member has an assigned post on the Muster List — Cruise Staff are hosts and performers by day, guest-calming and evacuation guides in an emergency.
  • How: When the alarm sounds (seven short blasts plus one long signal abandon ship), don your lifejacket immediately, and proceed to your assigned station per the Muster List. Core functions: reassure guests in both languages ("Stay calm — follow me, please"), guide them along evacuation routes to their muster stations, keep lines orderly to prevent crushes, support headcounts, and assist guests with special needs (elderly, children, wheelchair users). Attend abandon-ship and fire drills every week/voyage and sign in.
  • Watch out: Never detour to collect personal belongings when the alarm sounds, never run against the flow of guests, and never offer empty reassurance — give clear action commands ("Follow me to your muster station") instead of just "Don't worry." Drill attendance is mandatory and checked. The guaranteed interview question: "What are your emergency duties?" — answer with your Muster List station, guest calming, and guiding.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部全员每周安全演习与真实应急事件中的宾客管理职责。

无线手持麦克风Wireless Handheld Mic

深度解析
  • 【是什么】无线手持麦克风（Wireless Handheld Mic）是娱乐部使用频率最高的设备，通常为 UHF 无线系统，手持发射器与舞台接收器按频点配对，英文名 Wireless Handheld Microphone；它是 Cruise Staff 的「饭碗工具」，管理好坏直接影响每场活动。
  • 【怎么做】开场前检查三件事：电池电量（新电池或充满的镍氢电池，长活动备一组备用电池）、接收器 RF/AF 信号灯是否亮、频点是否与接收器匹配（frequency check）；试音说 "Check, one two"，严禁吹气或拍打麦克风（吹麦 pop 音大且不专业）；持麦距嘴约 5–10 cm；不开口时执行「开关手势」——拇指下滑关闭静音开关、食指上抬恢复，避免咳嗽杂音外泄；用完关闭电源并归位充电座，不可随手放桌（防跌落与误触）。
  • 【易错点】错误：开场才发现没电、两只话筒频点打架导致断频、拿着开着的话筒在台上咳嗽或抱怨（全场都听得见）、用完不关电池过夜漏电。检查要点：每场活动前例行 "mic check" 并列入流程；面试可提「我每次活动前都检查电池和频点」体现职业习惯。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Wireless Handheld Mic is the entertainment team's most-used piece of equipment — typically a UHF system with the handheld transmitter paired to a stage receiver by frequency. It's the Cruise Staff's bread-and-butter tool, and how it's managed makes or breaks every event.
  • How: Before each session run three checks: battery level (fresh or fully charged NiMH cells, plus a spare set for long events), whether the receiver's RF/AF indicator lights up, and that the mic's frequency matches the receiver (frequency check). Sound-test with "Check, one two" — never blow into or tap the mic (big pops, and it looks unprofessional). Hold it 5–10 cm from your mouth. When not speaking, use the on/off hand signal — thumb down to mute, index finger up to unmute — so coughs and side comments never leak out. Switch it off and return it to the charging dock after use; never leave it on a table.
  • Watch out: Discovering dead batteries at showtime, two mics fighting over the same frequency (dropouts), coughing or venting into a live mic (the whole room hears it), and leaving it powered on overnight. Build a "mic check" into your pre-event routine. In interviews, "I always check batteries and frequencies before every event" signals real professionalism.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Cruise Staff 与技术员（Production/AV Crew）所有活动现场主持与设备交接。

音响调音台PA Mixer / Sound Console

深度解析
  • 【是什么】调音台（Mixer / Sound Console）是活动音频的中枢设备，负责话筒增益、背景音乐、音量平衡与 EQ 调节，英文名 PA Mixer / Sound Console；Cruise Staff 通常只需掌握基本通道操作，专业演出由 Audio Technician 负责。
  • 【怎么做】活动前设定：主输出音量（master fader）调至全场可听清但不刺耳；每支话筒单独调增益（gain）——宁低勿高，防止过载失真；防啸叫（feedback）三招：话筒不过音箱正前方轴线、增益从低往高慢慢加、一旦啸叫立即压低对应通道 EQ 高频或将话筒移离音箱；背景音乐垫底音量以 1–2 米外正常交谈不被盖过为准（约 60–70 dB）；推子拉下再关机、开机顺序先开调音台后开功放（防开机冲击声）。
  • 【易错点】错误：把 master 开到最大再用通道推子硬压（失真）、话筒正对主音箱（必然啸叫）、活动音量对隔壁休息舱造成投诉。检查要点：每场活动前 15 分钟完成走场试音（sound check）；面试常问「现场啸叫了怎么办」——答降低增益/压高频/移麦离音箱，顺序讲清。
Deep Dive
  • What: The mixer (sound console) is the hub of event audio — handling mic gain, background music, volume balance, and EQ. Cruise Staff usually need basic channel operation only; professional shows are run by the Audio Technician.
  • How: Before the event, set the master fader so the room hears clearly without harshness; set each mic's gain individually — start low and add gradually to avoid clipping. Feedback control comes down to three habits: keep mics off the main speakers' front axis, raise gain slowly, and at the first squeal cut the high EQ on that channel or move the mic away from the speaker. Keep background music at a level where guests can still converse 1–2 meters away (roughly 60–70 dB). Pull faders down before power-down, and switch the console on before the amps to avoid startup thumps.
  • Watch out: Cranking the master and fighting distortion with channel faders, pointing a mic straight at the main speakers (instant feedback), and playing loud enough to generate complaints from neighboring cabins. Complete a sound check 15 minutes before every event. Interview favorite: "Feedback starts — what do you do?" — lower gain, cut highs, move the mic; explain the order.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Cruise Staff 日常活动音控、AV Technician 演出与舞厅活动调音。

舞台灯光控制Lighting Console

深度解析
  • 【是什么】灯光控制台（Lighting Console / Lighting Board）控制剧场与活动场地的灯光场景切换，通过预设灯光程序（lighting cues）一键调用，英文名 Lighting Console；Cruise Staff 一般操作预设 cue，专业演出由 Lighting Technician 执行。
  • 【怎么做】掌握常用 preset cues：开场用全场亮场（full house lights）方便宾客入座；游戏与互动环节切换彩色面光（color wash）或追光（follow spot）营造气氛；抽奖、颁奖、表演独唱用聚光灯（spotlight）聚焦；节目间用暗场（blackout）配合音乐进出；每个 cue 有编号，切换前先核对 cue 号与台上口令（如主持人说 "Lights" 即切下一 cue），淡入淡出用 fade 推杆控制 2–3 秒过渡，避免黑场尴尬。
  • 【易错点】错误：cue 顺序背错导致该亮时不亮、fade 拉太快宾客眼前一黑、忘了在 cue 表上标记已执行位置。检查要点：演出前与技术员过一遍 cue sheet 并彩排一次；面试可提「我会提前对 cue 表并记住每个环节的灯位」体现配合意识。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Lighting Console controls scene changes in the theater and event venues through pre-programmed lighting cues. Cruise Staff generally fire preset cues, while Lighting Technicians run full productions.
  • How: Master the standard presets: full house lights for openers so guests can find seats; a color wash or follow spot for games and interactive segments; a spotlight for raffles, awards, and solo numbers; and a blackout with music for transitions between acts. Every cue has a number — verify the cue against the host's call (many hosts say "Lights" to trigger the next one), and use the fade fader for a 2–3 second crossfade so you never leave the stage in awkward darkness.
  • Watch out: Misremembering cue order (lights fail to come up on time), riding the fade too fast so the room goes pitch black, and failing to mark your position on the cue sheet. Walk the cue sheet with the technician and rehearse once before the show. In interviews, "I go through the cue sheet beforehand and know every segment's lighting" shows real teamwork awareness.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部剧场演出、游艺晚会与颁奖活动中的灯光 cue 配合。

投影与屏幕Projector / LED Screen

深度解析
  • 【是什么】投影仪与大屏（Projector / LED Screen）是 trivia、卡拉 OK、电影放映与讲座类活动的显示设备，英文名 Projector / LED Screen / Display Screen；Cruise Staff 常兼任「放幻灯片的人」，设备故障会直接让一场活动停摆。
  • 【怎么做】活动前 15–30 分钟检查：信号源与输入切换（input source：HDMI/VGA/USB）是否正常、PPT 与视频各完整试播一遍（视频尤其要看声音是否出）、遥控器电池、屏幕比例与分辨率匹配（16:9）；自备一条转接线（HDMI 转 VGA/USB-C）以防宾客自带来料接口不符；放映中字号确保最后一排可读；活动结束切换回默认画面（欢迎屏）并关闭设备省电、防长时间静帧烧屏。
  • 【易错点】错误：只试了 PPT 没试视频（现场黑屏无声）、忘了关投影导致整夜静帧、宾客自带 U 盘格式读不出（提前让宾客发文件）。检查要点：把「试播完整一遍」列入活动前 checklist；面试可提「我总是把视频从头到尾播一遍再开场」。
Deep Dive
  • What: The projector and LED screen are the display backbone for trivia, karaoke, movie nights, and enrichment talks. Cruise Staff often double as the slides operator, and a display failure can stall an entire event.
  • How: Check 15–30 minutes before the event: input-source switching (HDMI/VGA/USB) works, play the full deck and every video end to end (audio included — that's where it fails), check remote batteries, and confirm aspect ratio and resolution match (16:9). Carry a spare adapter cable (HDMI to VGA/USB-C) for guest-supplied devices with the wrong port. During the show, keep text large enough to read from the back row. Afterward, return to the default welcome screen, power down to save energy, and avoid leaving a static image up all night (burn-in).
  • Watch out: Testing the deck but not the videos (black screen, no sound at showtime), leaving the projector on a static frame overnight, and a guest's USB drive failing to read — have files sent in advance. Put "play everything through once" on your pre-event checklist. In interviews, "I always play videos start to finish before doors open" is a strong line.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 trivia/卡拉 OK/讲座等视听活动的设备准备与运行保障。

活动道具管理Props Inventory

深度解析
  • 【是什么】活动道具（Props）指娱乐活动使用的可移动物料：宾果球与宾果机、答题板与笔、游戏棒、骰子、奖品（prizes）、比赛用桶与救生圈道具等，英文名 Props / Props Inventory；道具管理是娱乐部库房（stores）纪律，直接影响活动能否按时开场。
  • 【怎么做】实行领用归还登记（sign-out/sign-in log）：姓名、日期、道具清单、数量逐项登记；每场活动前 10 分钟按清单清点，结束后当场清点归位——少一件当场找，不过夜；奖品单独上锁管理、按场次发放并留发放记录；发现损耗（如宾果球破损、答题板写花）当日上报娱乐部主管（Cruise Director / Activities Manager）补充，防止影响下一场次。
  • 【易错点】错误：借了不登记（别人无法核对）、活动结束不数就收箱（下一场开场才发现缺件）、奖品随手放办公室被「借走」。检查要点：库房每月盘点一次，账物一致；面试常问「道具少了怎么办」——答当场清点回溯+按登记表排查+当日上报。
Deep Dive
  • What: Props are the movable materials behind every activity — bingo balls and the bingo machine, answer boards and pens, game sticks, dice, prizes, race tubs, and novelty floatables. Props discipline lives in the entertainment stores, and it decides whether an event starts on time.
  • How: Run a sign-out/sign-in log: name, date, item list, and quantities item by item. Count against the list 10 minutes before each event and again immediately after, returning everything on the spot — a missing item gets chased down the same day, never overnight. Prizes are kept locked, issued per session, and logged. Report any breakage (cracked bingo balls, scribbled answer boards) to the Cruise Director / Activities Manager the same day so the next session is never short.
  • Watch out: Borrowing without logging (nobody can reconcile later), boxing everything up uncounted (missing pieces surface at the next event), and leaving prizes loose in the office to "walk away." Stores are inventoried monthly and books must match stock. Interview favorite: "Props go missing — what do you do?" — retrace with the log, count on the spot, and report the same day.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部库房管理、每日多场次活动的道具周转与奖品发放。

宾客邀请参与Inclusive Invitation

深度解析
  • 【是什么】宾客邀请参与（Inclusive Invitation）是 Cruise Staff 引导宾客参加活动的话术与分寸技巧：面向全场开放邀请、不点名施压、尊重宾客「旁观自由」，英文名 Inclusive Invitation / Open Invitation；这是 Cruise Staff 面试中的高频软技能题。
  • 【怎么做】实用做法：面向全场喊话（"Who wants to help me judge this contest?"）让人自己举手，而不是盯着一个人「就你了」；对独处或在旁观看的宾客先寒暄破冰（问航程感受）再顺势邀请，给台阶式选项（"you can judge instead of playing"）；被婉拒时微笑说 "No worries — enjoy the show!" 立即转向他人，不追着劝；活动中给参与者持续正面反馈（鼓掌、欢呼），营造「参与很安全」的氛围。
  • 【易错点】错误：当众点名造成尴尬甚至投诉、被拒绝后反复纠缠、只照顾活跃宾客冷落了边缘宾客。检查要点：每场活动结束后留意参与人数变化；面试必问「宾客不愿参加怎么办」——答尊重选择+保持友好+给替代参与方式（当评委/观众）。
Deep Dive
  • What: Inclusive Invitation is the art and etiquette of drawing guests into activities: open calls to the whole crowd, never putting anyone on the spot, and respecting guests' freedom to just watch. It's a high-frequency soft-skill question in Cruise Staff interviews.
  • How: Address the whole room ("Who wants to help me judge this contest?") and let people volunteer, instead of singling someone out with "You! Come up!" For guests sitting alone or watching, break the ice with small talk about the voyage first, then invite with a graceful low-stakes option ("You could be our judge instead of playing"). If declined, smile with "No worries — enjoy the show!", move on immediately, and never press. During the event, keep cheering and applauding participants so joining feels safe.
  • Watch out: Public call-outs that embarrass guests (and generate complaints), pestering after a refusal, and focusing only on the outgoing crowd while ignoring wallflowers. Track participation numbers after each event. The guaranteed interview question: "A guest doesn't want to join" — respect the choice, stay warm, and offer an alternative role like judge or audience MVP.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Cruise Staff 所有互动活动现场、社交破冰与面试软技能考核。

儿童看护边界Youth Staff Boundaries

深度解析
  • 【是什么】儿童看护边界（Youth Staff Boundaries）是娱乐部与儿童相关工作中的强制保护规范：任何员工不得单独带走或独自看护儿童，所有儿童接触必须在有见证（visible to others）的场景下进行，英文名 Youth Staff Boundaries / Child Safeguarding；这是涉及未成年人保护的合规红线。
  • 【怎么做】核心规则：儿童进出儿童俱乐部（Kids Club）必须走签到签退（sign-in/sign-out）流程，由登记在册的家长/监护人凭船卡与接领凭证接领；员工不得单独将儿童带离活动区域或送回客舱（须两人同行 two-deep rule）；发现走失儿童（lost child）立即按规程广播专用寻呼（如 Mike Call）并同步通知 Youth Staff 与安保部（Security），同时自己在原地看护并保持公共可见位置；不得与儿童私下单独合影、交换联系方式。
  • 【易错点】错误：好心单独送迷路孩子回舱（严重违规）、把儿童交给自称亲戚的陌生人（必须核对登记名单）、发现走失儿童先自己四处找而不同步上报。检查要点：全员每年须完成儿童保护（child safeguarding）培训；面试常问「孩子跟你说想让你单独带他去找妈妈怎么办」——答保持公共区域可见+两人规则+通知同事，绝不单独行动。
Deep Dive
  • What: Youth Staff Boundaries are the mandatory safeguarding rules for any work involving children: no staff member may take a child aside or supervise one alone, and all child contact must happen in sight of others. Also called Child Safeguarding — a strict compliance red line.
  • How: Children enter and leave the Kids Club only through the sign-in/sign-out procedure, released solely to registered parents or guardians presenting the cruise card and pick-up credential. Staff never escort a child out of the activity area or back to a cabin alone (two-deep rule). If you find a lost child, immediately trigger the coded page (e.g., Mike Call), notify Youth Staff and Security, and stay with the child in a public, visible spot. No private one-on-one photos or contact exchange with children.
  • Watch out: Kindly walking a lost child back to their cabin alone is a serious violation; never release a child to a self-declared relative without checking the registration list; don't wander off searching while failing to report a lost child. Everyone completes annual child-safeguarding training. Interview favorite: "A child asks you to take him to find his mom" — stay visible, apply the two-deep rule, call a colleague; never act alone.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Youth Staff 儿童托管、全员的走失儿童处置与未成年人保护合规。

醉酒宾客劝离活动区De-escalation

深度解析
  • 【是什么】劝离与降温（De-escalation）是娱乐员工面对醉酒、情绪激动宾客时使用的平息技巧：目标是让宾客平静离开活动区，而不是对抗，英文名 De-escalation Techniques；娱乐员工只有安抚与引导职责，没有强制权力。
  • 【怎么做】实操要点：语气平静、语速放慢、不对抗不评判（"Let me get you a water first"）；把沟通移到人群侧面单独进行，保住宾客面子；提供替代动作（休息区坐坐、送回客舱休息）引导离场；出现以下任一信号立即通知安保（Security）接手：言语威胁、推搡动作、拒绝一切沟通或影响他人安全；期间用对讲机报告位置与宾客特征，自己始终站在可退出的位置，不做肢体接触。
  • 【易错点】错误：当众大声制止激化情绪、自己上手拉拽宾客（越权且危险）、忘了叫安保单独硬撑。检查要点：事后必须写事件报告（incident report）；面试必问「喝醉的宾客扰乱活动怎么办」——答先单独安抚引导、无效立即交 Security、绝不强制。
Deep Dive
  • What: De-escalation is the set of calming techniques Cruise Staff use with intoxicated or agitated guests — the goal is a peaceful exit from the event area, not confrontation. Entertainment staff may calm and guide, but hold no enforcement power.
  • How: Keep your tone low and slow, never judgmental or combative ("Let me get you some water first"). Move the conversation to the side of the room, one-on-one, protecting the guest's dignity. Offer an alternative action — a seat in the lounge, an escort back to the cabin. Call Security immediately at any of these signals: verbal threats, physical pushing, refusal of all communication, or risk to others. Meanwhile, radio in your location and a description of the guest, always keep an exit path behind you, and never make physical contact.
  • Watch out: Loudly confronting the guest in front of the crowd escalates things; physically pulling a guest is overstepping and dangerous; toughing it out alone instead of calling Security is a classic failure. File an incident report afterward, always. The guaranteed interview question: "A drunk guest disrupts your event" — calm and guide privately, hand over to Security if unresolved, never use force.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部夜场派对、酒吧区活动与泳池甲板高峰时段的现场秩序管理。

活动投诉安抚Complaint Handling

深度解析
  • 【是什么】活动投诉安抚（Complaint Handling）是娱乐员工处理针对活动本身的投诉的流程：音乐太吵、座位被占、活动取消、奖品不公等，英文名 Complaint Handling；娱乐部流程与前台 LEARN 模型一致但权限更聚焦现场可改项。
  • 【怎么做】四步法「倾听—致歉—解决—感谢」：先让宾客说完（不打断、记录要点）；真诚致歉不辩解；能当场改的立即改——音量调低、协助换座位、解释下一场次时间、补发小奖品；无法当场解决的（退款、跨部门协调、影响行程的变更）转交 Cruise Staff Manager / Cruise Director 跟进，并告知宾客谁会在多久内答复（如 24 小时内）；事后在班次日志记录投诉内容与处理结果。
  • 【易错点】错误：与宾客争辩「活动就是这样安排的」、口头答应补偿却没记录（ manager 无从跟进）、忘记向宾客关闭循环（以为转交了就完事）。检查要点：当场能改的绝不上交——一线解决速度就是满意度；面试常问「宾客抱怨音响太吵怎么办」——答立即调低+道歉+留下自己姓名以示负责。
Deep Dive
  • What: Complaint Handling is the process for complaints about the activities themselves — music too loud, seats taken, events cancelled, prize disputes. It mirrors the front desk's LEARN model, but entertainment staff focus on what can be fixed on the spot.
  • How: Run the four steps — Listen, Apologize, Resolve, Thank. Let the guest finish without interrupting while noting the details; apologize sincerely without defending; fix whatever is fixable immediately — lower the volume, help relocate seats, explain the next session's time, hand out a consolation prize. Anything beyond the spot-fix (refunds, cross-department coordination, itinerary-related changes) goes to the Cruise Staff Manager / Cruise Director, and you tell the guest who will respond and within what time (e.g., 24 hours). Log every complaint and outcome in the shift report.
  • Watch out: Arguing "that's how the schedule works" never helps; promising compensation verbally without logging it leaves the manager nothing to follow up on; and handing it off without closing the loop with the guest feels like abandonment. Fix on the spot whenever possible — one-line resolution speed is guest satisfaction. Interview favorite: "Guests say the music is too loud" — turn it down immediately, apologize, and give your name to show ownership.

📍 应用场景：娱乐部 Cruise Staff 活动现场、剧场与酒吧区活动后的宾客反馈处理。

免税商业部11 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

免税销售Duty-Free Sales

深度解析
  • 【是什么】邮轮零售的核心业务：船舶在国际水域（公海）航行时，商品免征目的地关税与增值税，以低于岸上专柜的价格销售。受海关法与船旗国法规双重约束，是免税店员工（Retail Sales Associate）的立身之本。
  • 【怎么做】每日开航前核对当日「可售时段表」——只有确认船舶已驶出领海（Territorial Waters）才开门营业；进入领海前（通常靠港前数小时，以船司通告为准）按指令关店、贴封条（seal）并在日志登记封条编号；对烟酒等受限品类逐一核对宾客护照、航程目的地与海关配额，POS 系统会按航段自动限制超限交易。
  • 【易错点】新人最常见错误是在船舶尚未完全驶出领海时提前开店，或靠港前忘记贴封条——两者都是海关违规，可导致巨额罚款甚至 ports of call 禁售处罚。面试常问「When can you open the shop?」，标准答案要点：only in international waters, per the captain's announcement and company policy。
Deep Dive
  • What: The core business of cruise retail — goods sold in international waters are exempt from destination duties and VAT. It is governed by both customs law and flag-state regulations.
  • How: Verify the daily "trading window" before opening — the shop may trade only once the vessel is fully outside territorial waters; before entering territorial waters, close the shop, apply seals, and log the seal numbers. Check each guest's passport, itinerary and customs allowance for restricted categories such as tobacco and liquor.
  • Watch out: Opening early or failing to seal the shop before arrival is a customs violation with heavy fines. Interview classic: "When can you open the shop?" — answer: only in international waters, per the captain's announcement and company policy.

📍 应用场景：免税店员工每日开店/关店操作、靠港前后封店流程、面试问答

POS 收银Point of Sale (POS) System

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船上零售的统一交易系统，支持宾客船卡记账（cruise card / room charge）、现金与外币三种支付方式，每笔交易自动关联航段与海关额度，是免税店与宾客资金往来的唯一合规通道。
  • 【怎么做】扫描或录入商品后先请宾客出示船卡核对姓名与房号（防止盗刷）；现金交易当面点清并唱收唱付（"Your total is US$86, receiving US$100"）；每班结束做对账（reconciliation）：清点现金、核对 POS 系统小计、填写班次报告（shift report），差异当日上报经理，不得隔班处理或私下补差。
  • 【易错点】不核对船卡持有人与刷卡人就入账，是盗刷投诉的高发原因；对账差异隐瞒不报属于诚信红线，邮轮公司几乎零容忍。面试关联：「How do you handle a cash discrepancy?」——答：report immediately, never adjust it yourself。
Deep Dive
  • What: The unified transaction system for onboard retail, supporting cruise card charge, cash and foreign currencies; every sale is linked to the voyage segment and customs allowance.
  • How: Verify the guest's cruise card name and stateroom number before charging; count cash in front of the guest and announce amounts; at end of shift perform reconciliation — count cash, match POS totals, complete the shift report, and report any variance the same day.
  • Watch out: Charging without verifying the cardholder is the top cause of billing complaints; hiding a cash variance is an integrity red line. Interview: "How do you handle a cash discrepancy?" — report immediately, never adjust it yourself.

📍 应用场景：免税店收银岗日常交易、班末对账、财务审计

防损与盘点Loss Prevention / Inventory Audit

深度解析
  • 【是什么】保障库存与现金安全的管理体系：高价值商品（珠宝、腕表）专柜锁存、领用登记，每航次（per voyage/rotation）进行盘点（inventory count），把损耗率（shrinkage）控制在极低水平（如 0.5% 以内，以船司为准）。
  • 【怎么做】贵重品实行「交接签名制」：从保险柜/库房领出时双方当面清点签字，归还时同样操作；盘点时按货位逐件扫码或点数，与系统库存比对，差异逐条写进差异报告（discrepancy report）并注明可能原因（漏扫、错发、丢失）；接班时先巡场检查柜台锁具与展示品数量。
  • 【易错点】口头交接不签字、盘点差异「先自行补货抹平」——前者出问题无人可查，后者直接构成隐瞒，性质比差异本身严重得多。面试常问：「What would you do if an item is missing after count?」——答：report to the manager and document, never replace it quietly。
Deep Dive
  • What: The system protecting stock and cash: high-value items (jewelry, watches) kept locked with sign-out logs, and an inventory count every voyage with shrinkage kept extremely low (e.g. within 0.5%, per company policy).
  • How: Use two-signature handover for valuables; count item by item against system stock during inventory, record every variance on a discrepancy report with likely causes; on taking over a shift, first check locks and display quantities.
  • Watch out: Verbal handovers without signatures leave no accountability; quietly replacing missing stock to hide a variance is far more serious than the variance itself. Interview: "What would you do if an item is missing after count?" — report and document, never replace it quietly.

📍 应用场景：贵重品柜台交接、航次盘点、防损审计

促销活动Port Day Promotion

深度解析
  • 【是什么】随「港口日/海上日」客流节奏设计的销售策略：靠港日宾客上岸、店内客流低，以折扣与活动拉客流；海上日（sea day）客流高峰则全力冲销售并延长营业时间。免税店销售按此节奏排班与备货。
  • 【怎么做】港口日：执行当日促销方案（如买赠、满减、单品折扣），主动在廊桥口与中庭发传单/举牌引流，跟踪转化率；海上日：提前补满货架与试用品，高峰时段分点位接待（香水区/腕表区分工），用限量款与套装提高客单价；促销开始前确认 POS 中价格已更新，避免按旧价收款。
  • 【易错点】促销价未在 POS 同步就开卖，导致收银差错与客诉；或港口日「守株待兔」不主动引流——邮轮零售很看重 floor presence（主动招呼）。面试关联：「How do you drive sales on a sea day?」——答：greet proactively, upsell sets and bestsellers, know your products。
Deep Dive
  • What: Sales strategy built around port/sea day rhythm: on port days low footfall is offset by discounts and events; on sea days the team maximizes sales with extended hours.
  • How: On port days run the promotion plan, hand out flyers at the gangway and atrium, track conversion; on sea days restock shelves and testers early, split stations (fragrance/watch zones), push limited editions and sets to raise average transaction value; confirm promo prices are updated in POS before selling.
  • Watch out: Selling before POS prices are updated causes cash errors and complaints; standing idle instead of actively engaging guests hurts sales. Interview: "How do you drive sales on a sea day?" — greet proactively, upsell sets and bestsellers, know your products.

📍 应用场景：港口日引流、海上日冲量、促销排班与备货

限购额度Purchase Limits / Customs Allowance

深度解析
  • 【是什么】各国海关对入境旅客携带免税烟酒的数量上限（duty-free allowance），是船上烟酒销售的硬性合规约束。常见量级：酒精 1–2 升、香烟 200 支（1 条）上下，随目的地国与航段不同而变化，具体以各国规定为准。
  • 【怎么做】收银时按宾客的 disembarkation country 调出对应额度表；核对护照确认目的地，POS 自动累计同一宾客在本航段的受限品类购买量，超限即停售并礼貌解释（"This exceeds the customs allowance for your destination"）；对转机或联程旅客提示目的国可能另有更严限额。
  • 【易错点】只看「想买多少」不看「能带多少」，替宾客凑单超限；或把不同航段额度混为一谈。额度用尽还坚持售卖属违规，责任在销售员而不在宾客。面试关联：「What do you check before selling liquor?」——答：passport, destination and remaining customs allowance。
Deep Dive
  • What: Each country's customs cap on duty-free tobacco and liquor brought in by arriving passengers — a hard compliance limit on onboard sales. Typical figures: 1–2 liters of alcohol, around 200 cigarettes, varying by destination and voyage segment.
  • How: Look up the allowance for the guest's disembarkation country at checkout; verify the passport and destination; the POS accumulates restricted-category purchases per guest and blocks excess sales — explain politely when it does; remind transit passengers of possibly stricter limits at their final destination.
  • Watch out: Never oversell beyond the allowance to close a sale — liability sits with the seller. Interview: "What do you check before selling liquor?" — passport, destination and remaining customs allowance.

📍 应用场景：烟酒收银核对、多航段额度累计、海关检查配合

奢侈腕表Luxury Watches

深度解析
  • 【是什么】免税店利润最高的品类之一，销售员需掌握品牌梯度、价带与机芯常识，用专业知识建立信任促成大单。
  • 【怎么做】熟记定位梯度：Rolex（劳力士，运动豪华标杆）、Omega（欧米茄，Swatch 集团高端线）、Longines（浪琴，中高端入门首选）；能报出入门价带（Longines 约 US$1,000–2,500 起、Omega 约 US$4,000 起，以店内当日价为准）；会讲 automatic（机械，靠佩戴摆陀上链、需 3–5 年保养）与 quartz（石英，电池驱动、走时更准）差异，并按宾客预算与佩戴场景推荐（正装/潜水/日常）。
  • 【易错点】只报价格不讲价值（机芯、防水、保值性），推高不可及的款式吓跑顾客；或承诺店内无法兑现的售后服务——售后一律以品牌授权维修点为准。面试关联：「How do you sell a US$5,000 watch?」——答：ask about occasion and budget first, explain movement and value, never pressure。
Deep Dive
  • What: One of the highest-margin duty-free categories; associates must know brand hierarchy, price bands and movement basics to build trust and close high-ticket sales.
  • How: Know the hierarchy — Rolex (sport-luxury benchmark), Omega (Swatch Group's high-end line), Longines (entry point for the mid-to-high segment); quote entry price bands (Longines from about US$1,000–2,500, Omega from about US$4,000, per current store pricing); explain automatic vs quartz movements and match watches to budget and occasion (dress, dive, daily wear).
  • Watch out: Don't quote price without value (movement, water resistance, resale); never promise after-sales service the store can't deliver — repairs go to authorized brand service centers. Interview: "How do you sell a US$5,000 watch?" — ask about occasion and budget first, explain movement and value, never pressure.

📍 应用场景：腕表专柜销售、高客单成交、品牌知识考核

香水美妆Fragrance & Beauty

深度解析
  • 【是什么】免税店客流最大、复购最高的品类；核心技能是香调分类与柜台试用规范，把「逛」变成「买」。
  • 【怎么做】熟记四大香调家族：花香调（floral）、木质调（woody）、东方调（oriental）、清新调（fresh），先问宾客喜好（日常还是晚宴、偏甜还是偏清爽）再推荐；试用流程：试纸（blotter）先试，喷后扇风等前调散去再闻；皮肤试用喷于手腕内侧、不要揉搓（揉搓会破坏前调分子）；一次不超过 3 款，避免嗅觉疲劳（olfactory fatigue）导致判断失准。
  • 【易错点】连喷七八款让宾客「鼻子失灵」，最后什么都选不出来；或把男士香水推荐给明确要送礼女士的宾客而不先确认对象。面试关联：「How do you recommend a fragrance?」——答：ask about preference and occasion first, then guide with the four scent families。
Deep Dive
  • What: The highest-traffic, most repeat-purchase duty-free category; key skills are scent-family knowledge and proper tester etiquette that turn browsers into buyers.
  • How: Master the four families — floral, woody, oriental, fresh — ask about preference and occasion before recommending; use a blotter first and wait for top notes to settle; for skin testing, spray the inner wrist and never rub (rubbing crushes the top notes); limit to 3 fragrances per session to avoid olfactory fatigue.
  • Watch out: Spraying seven or eight scents leaves the guest unable to choose anything; don't recommend men's fragrance without confirming who the gift is for. Interview: "How do you recommend a fragrance?" — ask about preference and occasion first, then guide with the four scent families.

📍 应用场景：香水柜台接待、礼品推荐、试用服务规范

烈酒烟草Spirits & Tobacco

深度解析
  • 【是什么】免税店传统高价值品类，专业知识门槛高：干邑等级、威士忌产区、雪茄保存条件都是销售员的必备话术。
  • 【怎么做】干邑按陈酿年限排序：VS（Very Special，最浅 2 年）、VSOP（Very Superior Old Pale，4 年以上）、XO（Extra Old，10 年以上），价格与口感层次随之递增；威士忌按产区讲差异（苏格兰 Scotch、波本 Bourbon、日本 Japanese whisky）；雪茄保存于保湿盒（humidor），相对湿度约 65–72%、温度 18–21°C——过干易裂、过湿易霉，开柜先看湿度计。
  • 【易错点】把 VS 和 XO 的差别只说成「贵一点」，讲不出陈酿逻辑；雪茄柜湿度超标不报修，整批货发霉损失巨大。另须牢记：所有烟酒销售都受海关限购额度约束（见 kb-5-5）。面试关联：「Explain the difference between VS and XO.」
Deep Dive
  • What: A traditional high-value duty-free category demanding real expertise: cognac grades, whisky origins and cigar storage conditions are core sales knowledge.
  • How: Rank cognacs by age — VS (Very Special, minimum 2 years), VSOP (Very Superior Old Pale, 4+ years), XO (Extra Old, 10+ years) — price and complexity rising accordingly; explain whisky by origin (Scotch, Bourbon, Japanese); store cigars in a humidor at about 65–72% relative humidity and 18–21°C — too dry they crack, too damp they mold; check the hygrometer when opening the case.
  • Watch out: Reducing VS vs XO to "just more expensive" loses credibility; an unchecked humidor can ruin an entire stock. Remember all tobacco and liquor sales are bound by customs allowances. Interview: "Explain the difference between VS and XO."

📍 应用场景：烟酒专柜销售、雪茄柜养护、藏家级宾客接待

珠宝Fine Jewelry

深度解析
  • 【是什么】免税店单笔金额最高的品类，销售员必须能读懂纯度标记与钻石 4C 分级，向宾客做专业讲解而非「只会报折扣」。
  • 【怎么做】贵金属：18K 金含金量 75%，戒圈内壁刻 "750" 即其欧洲标法，14K 为 585；钻石按 4C 分级——克拉（Carat）重量、颜色（Color）D–Z（D 最白）、净度（Clarity）FL–I3、切工（Cut）Excellent–Poor；推介时按宾客预算在 4C 之间权衡（如预算有限优先保切工），并出示证书（GIA 等）。
  • 【易错点】只吹克拉数忽略切工——切工差钻石不闪，宾客戴上后悔必退货差评；对「是不是真的」之类问题答不上证书与标记依据。面试关联：「How do you explain the 4Cs?」——答：walk through each C with the guest's budget as the anchor。
Deep Dive
  • What: The highest single-ticket duty-free category; associates must read purity marks and the diamond 4Cs, and explain them professionally rather than just quoting discounts.
  • How: Precious metals — 18K gold is 75% pure, stamped "750" inside the band (European standard), 14K is 585; diamonds are graded by the 4Cs — Carat weight, Color D–Z (D whitest), Clarity FL–I3, Cut Excellent–Poor; trade off across the 4Cs within the guest's budget (e.g. prioritize cut on a limited budget) and show certificates (GIA etc.).
  • Watch out: Selling on carat weight alone ignores cut — a poorly cut diamond lacks brilliance and invites returns and bad reviews; be ready to back "is it real?" with hallmarks and certificates. Interview: "How do you explain the 4Cs?" — walk through each C with the guest's budget as the anchor.

📍 应用场景：珠宝专柜销售、钻石讲解、证书验真

皮具箱包Leather Goods

深度解析
  • 【是什么】免税店与奢侈品零售的主力品类，考核点在材质识别、保养建议与工艺讲解——这三项是判断销售员「专业度」的直接标尺。
  • 【怎么做】材质：区分头层皮（full-grain，天然纹理最耐用）、荔枝纹（pebbled，耐磨抗刮）与涂层帆布配皮（coated canvas with leather trim）；保养建议：避免雨淋暴晒、不用酒精湿巾擦拭（会破坏皮面涂层）、建议配防刮底垫与收纳袋（dust bag）；讲解品牌五金（hardware，如镀层、刻字）与缝线工艺（均匀细密、无跳线）作为档次鉴别点。
  • 【易错点】拿酒精湿巾当场清洁皮具演示——直接损伤商品还要照价赔偿；把所有皮都叫「真皮」而讲不出层级。面试关联：「How do you care for full-grain leather?」——答：avoid sun and rain, no alcohol wipes, store in a dust bag。
Deep Dive
  • What: A mainstay of duty-free and luxury retail; assessment focuses on material identification, care advice and craftsmanship storytelling.
  • How: Distinguish full-grain leather (natural grain, most durable), pebbled leather (scratch-resistant) and coated canvas with leather trim; care advice — avoid rain and direct sun, never use alcohol wipes (they strip the finish), recommend base shapers and dust bags; point out hardware (plating, engraving) and stitching (even, tight, no skipped stitches) as quality markers.
  • Watch out: Cleaning leather with an alcohol wipe in front of the guest damages the product and you pay for it; calling every leather "genuine" without grades sounds amateur. Interview: "How do you care for full-grain leather?" — avoid sun and rain, no alcohol wipes, store in a dust bag.

📍 应用场景：箱包专柜销售、售后保养咨询、材质知识考核

手表保养与电池Watch Service

深度解析
  • 【是什么】腕表专柜的售后窗口：换电池（battery replacement）、表带调节（bracelet sizing）、防水检测等基础服务咨询。服务边界清晰——店内只做基础服务，不拆机。
  • 【怎么做】换电池：确认表款型号与电池规格，操作在防静电环境完成，更换后做防水检测（pressure test）并告知结果；表带调节：按腕围增减链节，多余链节连同保卡归还宾客；机械表须提示佩戴动力（不戴时走时停摆属正常）与定期保养（每 3–5 年机芯保养，以品牌要求为准）；一切大修明确指引品牌授权维修点。
  • 【易错点】guest 让你「顺便看看机芯」就真的拆开——超出店内服务范围，损坏即责任事故；换电池后不做防水检测就交付，进水投诉无据可依。面试关联：「A guest asks for movement repair in-store. What do you do?」——答：explain in-store service limits, refer to the authorized service center。
Deep Dive
  • What: The after-sales window of the watch counter: battery replacement, bracelet sizing and water-resistance checks as basic in-store services — with clear limits, no movement opening.
  • How: For batteries, confirm the model and cell spec, work in an anti-static environment, run a pressure test afterwards and report the result; for sizing, add/remove links to wrist size and return spare links with the warranty card; remind automatic owners that stopping when unworn is normal and movements need servicing every 3–5 years per brand requirements; refer all major repairs to the authorized service center.
  • Watch out: Opening the case back "just to look" exceeds in-store scope — damage becomes your liability; skipping the water-resistance test after a battery change leaves you defenseless against leak complaints. Interview: "A guest asks for movement repair in-store. What do you do?" — explain the service limits and refer to the authorized service center.

📍 应用场景：腕表售后接待、换电池/调表带服务、维修转介

安保与医务23 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

国际海上人命安全公约SOLAS (International Convention for the Safety of Life at Sea)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】IMO（国际海事组织）1974 年通过的核心公约，规定消防、救生设备、弃船演练、集合站（muster station）制度与分舱稳性要求，是船上一切安全操作的法律基础；1912 年泰坦尼克号海难是其直接催生背景。
  • 【怎么做】新船员上船后的首要学习对象：熟记本船 Safety Plan 中自己的职责卡（Muster List）条目、集合站位置与两条最短逃生路线；参加每周弃船/消防演练并做到「听得到、找得到、穿得上」；客舱服务岗位还须会演示救生衣穿戴并向宾客讲解。
  • 【易错点】把 SOLAS 演练当「走过场」不按要求穿戴——SOLAS 规定乘客登船前或离港后 24 小时内必须完成旅客安全简报，演练缺勤会被记录并影响续约；面试必问：「What is SOLAS?」——答：the IMO convention for safety of life at sea, covering fire and life-saving drills and muster procedures。
Deep Dive
  • What: The IMO's cornerstone convention (adopted 1974), governing fire safety, life-saving appliances, abandon ship drills, muster station procedures and subdivision — the legal basis for all onboard safety operations; born from the 1912 Titanic disaster.
  • How: First thing every new crew member studies: know your Muster List duties, muster station and two shortest escape routes; attend weekly abandon ship and fire drills fully dressed and equipped; cabin stewards must also demonstrate lifejacket donning to guests.
  • Watch out: Treating drills as a formality gets recorded and affects contract renewal; passengers must receive a safety briefing before departure or within 24 hours of sailing. Interview must-know: "What is SOLAS?" — the IMO convention for safety of life at sea, covering fire and life-saving drills and muster procedures.

📍 应用场景：全员安全培训、每周演练、面试安全题

国际船舶和港口设施保安规则ISPS Code (International Ship and Port Facility Security Code)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】IMO 2002 年通过的保安规则（9/11 事件后出台），规定船舶保安计划（Ship Security Plan, SSP）、门禁管控、限制区域（restricted areas）与三级保安等级，是安保团队（Security Guard）日常工作框架。
  • 【怎么做】按 SSP 执行日常职责：舷梯口（gangway）值勤查验登船凭证与证件、控制船员通道门禁、按轮巡表巡视限制区域并填写保安日志（security log）；发现可疑人员或未授权闯入立即上报值班保安官（Duty Security Officer）与船舶保安员（SSO, Ship Security Officer）。
  • 【易错点】值舷梯时只扫证件不核对照片与人脸——冒用船卡/证件是常见渗透手法；日志事后补填、漏填，审计时属于不合格记录。面试关联：「What is the ISPS Code?」——答：the IMO security code covering ship security plans, access control, restricted areas and security levels 1–3。
Deep Dive
  • What: The IMO security code adopted in 2002 (post-9/11), covering the Ship Security Plan (SSP), access control, restricted areas and three security levels — the daily operating framework for the security team.
  • How: Follow the SSP in daily duties: man the gangway checking boarding passes and IDs, control crew access doors, patrol restricted areas per the roster and complete the security log; report suspicious persons or unauthorized entry to the Duty Security Officer and the Ship Security Officer (SSO).
  • Watch out: Checking IDs without matching faces at the gangway is the classic penetration trick; backfilled or missing log entries fail any audit. Interview: "What is the ISPS Code?" — the IMO security code covering ship security plans, access control, restricted areas and security levels 1–3.

📍 应用场景：舷梯口值勤、门禁管控、保安日志与审计

弃船信号Abandon Ship Signal

深度解析
  • 【是什么】全船通用弃船警报：七短一长声（seven short blasts followed by one prolonged blast），由汽笛/警铃发出，意味着「立即弃船」，是 SOLAS 统一规定的信号体系之一。
  • 【怎么做】听到信号后立即行动：穿好救生衣（系紧四扣并打好腰跨结）、带上保暖装备，按最近安全路线直奔本人集合站，向部门负责人报到点名；途中不回舱室收拾行李、不乘普通客梯逆行。与消防信号（连续警铃 + 三遍 "Fire, fire, fire"）和通用紧急警报区分记忆。
  • 【易错点】听到信号先回舱拿手机护照——弃船信号下时间就是生命，演练中回舱即被判不合格；混淆「七短一长」与火灾警报。面试必背：「What is the abandon ship signal?」——答：seven short blasts and one prolonged blast, then proceed to your muster station with a lifejacket。
Deep Dive
  • What: The ship-wide abandon ship alarm: seven short blasts followed by one prolonged blast, sounded on the ship's whistle/alarms, meaning "abandon vessel" — standardized under SOLAS.
  • How: On hearing it, don your lifejacket (all buckles fastened, waist straps tied), bring warm clothing if at hand, proceed directly to your muster station by the nearest safe route and report for roll call; never return to your cabin for belongings or go against guest flow on passenger stairs.
  • Watch out: Going back for your phone or passport — during drills this is an automatic fail; distinguish it from the fire alarm (continuous bells plus "Fire, fire, fire" three times). Interview must-know: "What is the abandon ship signal?" — seven short and one prolonged blast, then proceed to your muster station with a lifejacket.

📍 应用场景：弃船演练、真实紧急撤离、面试必考题

弃船演练Abandon Ship Drill

深度解析
  • 【是什么】SOLAS 第三章规定的强制演练：客船须每周举行一次弃船演练（含消防演练），全体船员必须参加并记录航海日志。
  • 【怎么做】标准流程：警报响起（演练用七短一长）→ 穿救生衣 → 到集合站报到点名 → 演示救生衣正确穿戴 → 核对 Muster List 上本人的应急职责（如引导宾客、携带救援装备、协助登艇）→ 按指令参加救生艇筏准备与登乘演示；演练后参加讲评，缺陷项限期整改。
  • 【易错点】迟到、不穿救生衣或让同事「代报到」——点名与登录记录是 PSC（港口国监督）检查重点，代报到属造假；救生衣穿戴漏打裆部扣带是演示环节最常见的扣分点。面试关联：「How often are abandon ship drills held?」——答：at least weekly under SOLAS, and all crew must attend。
Deep Dive
  • What: A mandatory SOLAS Chapter III drill: passenger ships must hold an abandon ship drill at least weekly, with all crew attending and entries logged in the logbook.
  • How: Standard flow — alarm sounds (seven short, one prolonged) → don lifejacket → report at muster station for roll call → demonstrate correct lifejacket donning → confirm your Muster List duties (guest guiding, carrying rescue gear, assisting embarkation) → take part in lifeboat/liferaft preparation and embarkation demonstrations → attend the debrief and close out deficiencies.
  • Watch out: Being late, skipping the lifejacket, or having a colleague answer roll call for you — roll-call records are a key PSC inspection item and substitution is falsification; missing the crotch strap is the most common donning deduction. Interview: "How often are abandon ship drills held?" — at least weekly under SOLAS, and all crew must attend.

📍 应用场景：每周演练、PSC 检查、新船员入职培训

人员落水Man Overboard (MOB)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】邮轮最高级别紧急事件之一：人员坠入海中，需要全船协同的搜寻救援程序（MOB maneuver），任何岗位员工都可能是第一发现人。
  • 【怎么做】
    • 发现后立即：
    • 朝落水者方向大声呼喊 "MAN OVERBOARD, PORT/STARBOARD SIDE!" 并扔下救生圈（lifebuoy，带自亮浮灯）
    • 死盯落水者不丢视线，同时通知最近的工作人员接力报告驾驶台
    • 记住落水舷侧与大概时间，供驾驶台记录 MOB 位置并减速回转搜寻
    • 服从广播指令，非救援岗位人员不得擅自跳海施救
  • 【易错点】只顾喊不扔救生圈、或跑去打电话层层转报浪费黄金时间；视线跟丢后搜寻难度剧增。面试必问：「What do you do first if you see someone fall overboard?」——答：shout "man overboard", throw a lifebuoy, keep the person in sight and alert the bridge。
Deep Dive
  • What: One of the highest-priority emergencies at sea — a person in the water requiring a coordinated search-and-rescue maneuver; any crew member may be the first witness.
