  • How: On sighting: shout "MAN OVERBOARD, PORT/STARBOARD SIDE!" toward the bridge and throw a lifebuoy with the self-igniting light; keep the person in sight without breaking eye contact while relaying the alarm through the nearest crew member; note the side and approximate time for the bridge's MOB position and turn; follow broadcast instructions — never jump in to rescue unless assigned.
  • Watch out: Shouting without throwing the lifebuoy, or running off to phone it in, wastes critical seconds; losing visual contact makes the search far harder. Interview must-know: "What do you do first if you see someone fall overboard?" — shout "man overboard", throw a lifebuoy, keep the person in sight and alert the bridge.

📍 应用场景：全岗位应急第一响应、驾驶台 MOB 程序、面试必考

消防信号Fire Alarm

深度解析
  • 【是什么】火灾紧急信号：连续警铃 + 广播三遍 "Fire, fire, fire"，报告格式为地点 + 火情类型，如 "Fire, fire, fire. Fire in the galley on Deck 3."。船上是「岸上的城市」，消防是每个船员的基本职责（STCW 基本安全培训内容）。
  • 【怎么做】发现火情：立即喊 "Fire, fire, fire" + 具体位置报告附近同事与总机/驾驶台，尝试用最近灭火器扑灭初起小火（回忆灭火四要素：remove heat, fuel, oxygen or chain reaction）；听到警报后非消防队成员按职责到指定位置待命或引导宾客；灭火器选型——油类/电气用 CO2 或干粉，普通可燃物用水基。
  • 【易错点】报告时只喊 "Fire!" 不说位置，延误响应；用错误灭火器（对油火用水）导致火势扩大；电气火未断电就扑救。面试必背：「What is the fire alarm signal and how do you report a fire?」——答：continuous bells plus "Fire, fire, fire" three times with location and type of fire。
Deep Dive
  • What: The fire emergency signal: continuous bells plus the announcement "Fire, fire, fire" repeated three times, format = location + type of fire, e.g. "Fire, fire, fire. Fire in the galley on Deck 3." Firefighting is every crew member's basic duty under STCW basic safety training.
  • How: On discovering a fire, shout "Fire, fire, fire" with the exact location and alert nearby crew and the bridge/switchboard; attack small incipient fires with the nearest suitable extinguisher; non-fire-team members proceed to assigned stations or guide guests; select extinguishers correctly — CO2 or dry powder for oil and electrical fires, water-based for ordinary combustibles.
  • Watch out: Reporting "Fire!" without a location delays response; using water on an oil fire spreads it; never fight an electrical fire before isolating power. Interview must-know: "What is the fire alarm signal and how do you report a fire?" — continuous bells plus "Fire, fire, fire" three times with location and type of fire.

📍 应用场景：消防演练与真实火情响应、灭火器实操、面试必考

保安等级Security Levels 1–3

深度解析
  • 【是什么】ISPS Code 定义的三级船舶保安等级：Level 1（常态，正常预防措施）、Level 2（加强戒备，针对特定风险）、Level 3（紧急防御，针对可能发生的袭击，须政府/主管机关指令）。等级由船长与船舶保安员（SSO）宣布并执行对应措施。
  • 【怎么做】Level 1：常规舷梯值勤、门禁与证件查验；Level 2：加密轮巡、增派舷梯人手、限制部分通道与舷窗开启、100% 复查行李；Level 3：关闭大部分出入通道、只留单一受控入口、全员身份核验、配合政府指令——安保全员取消轮休就位。
  • 【易错点】以为升级是安保部「内部的事」——Level 2/3 时所有部门都可能被抽调配合（如封舱、清点、引导）；擅自向宾客评论「升级原因」造成恐慌属违规。面试关联：「What happens at Security Level 2?」——答：enhanced screening and patrols, restricted access, per the Ship Security Plan。
Deep Dive
  • What: The three ISPS security levels — Level 1 (normal, routine preventive measures), Level 2 (heightened vigilance for a specific risk), Level 3 (defensive emergency against a probable attack, set by government/authority direction). The Master and Ship Security Officer announce and enforce level changes.
  • How: Level 1 — routine gangway duty, access and ID checks; Level 2 — intensified patrols, extra gangway staffing, some access points closed, 100% baggage re-screening; Level 3 — most access points shut to a single controlled entry, 100% identity verification, compliance with authority instructions, all security staff on duty.
  • Watch out: Assuming level changes concern Security only — at Levels 2/3 all departments may be tasked (cabin lockdowns, headcounts, guest guidance); speculating to guests about "why" causes panic and violates the SSP. Interview: "What happens at Security Level 2?" — enhanced screening and patrols, restricted access, per the Ship Security Plan.

📍 应用场景：安保值勤、高风险港口戒备、全船应急配合

金属探测门Walk-Through Metal Detector (WTMD)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】登离船口（gangway）核心筛查设备：宾客与船员穿过门框，设备对金属物报警；与手持金属探测器（HHMD, Hand-Held Metal Detector）配合构成人工安检闭环。
  • 【怎么做】按现场环境分档校准灵敏度（平衡防误报与防漏报）；宾客报警时礼貌引导至二次检查区："Sir/Madam, the detector was activated — may I check you, please?"；用 HHMD 从头到脚按「头→躯干→腿部→脚踝」扫查报警部位，男女分区由同性安检员执行；遇宾客拒绝搜身或为假肢/医疗器械，转交主管按流程处理，不自行决断。
  • 【易错点】报警后挥手放行「看着不像坏人」——漏检责任在安检员；搜身未由同性执行或动作粗暴引发投诉。面试关联：「What do you do when the WTMD alarms?」——答：politely ask for a secondary check with the hand-held detector, same-gender officer, escalate refusals to the supervisor。
Deep Dive
  • What: The core screening unit at the gangway: guests and crew pass through the archway, which alarms on metal; paired with the Hand-Held Metal Detector (HHMD) it forms the manual screening loop.
  • How: Calibrate sensitivity in steps to balance false alarms against misses; on an alarm, guide the guest politely — "Sir/Madam, the detector was activated — may I check you, please?"; scan the alarmed areas head to toe with the HHMD, conducted by a same-gender officer in separated lines; refer refusals, prosthetics or medical devices to the supervisor per procedure rather than deciding alone.
  • Watch out: Waving through an alarm because someone "doesn't look suspicious" is a missed-screening failure; rough or cross-gender searches trigger complaints. Interview: "What do you do when the WTMD alarms?" — politely request a secondary HHMD check, same-gender officer, escalate refusals to the supervisor.

📍 应用场景：登离船安检、二次人工检查、宾客沟通

X 光行李机X-ray Screening

深度解析
  • 【是什么】行李与货物登船前的透视筛查设备，安检员通过判读屏幕图像发现违禁品（刀具、武器、超量酒类等），是 ISPS 门禁管控的技术手段之一。
  • 【怎么做】掌握颜色逻辑：有机物（organic，如食物、液体）呈橙色系、金属（metal，高密度）呈蓝色系、混合/无法判读的重叠区域标为排除区（exclusion zone）；图像不清晰、存在排除区或形态可疑时必须开箱手检（manual search），请宾客在场："May I open your bag for a check, please?"；发现违禁品登记暂扣并出具凭证，不与宾客争执，按公司政策处理。
  • 【易错点】「看着像充电宝」就凭猜测放行——排除区不手检是安检大忌；屏幕图像堆积不做图像增强（zoom/enhance）就下结论。面试关联：「When must you hand-search a bag?」——答：when the image is unclear or contains an exclusion zone — never clear it by guesswork。
Deep Dive
  • What: The X-ray machine screening baggage and cargo before boarding, letting operators detect prohibited items (blades, weapons, excess liquor) — a technical pillar of ISPS access control.
  • How: Read the colors — organics (food, liquids) show orange, metals show blue in high density, and overlapping unreadable areas appear as exclusion zones; whenever the image is unclear, contains an exclusion zone or looks suspicious, open the bag for a manual search with the guest present ("May I open your bag for a check, please?"); log and hold prohibited items with a receipt, handling them per company policy without arguing.
  • Watch out: Clearing "what looks like a power bank" by guesswork is a cardinal screening error; always zoom/enhance before concluding. Interview: "When must you hand-search a bag?" — when the image is unclear or contains an exclusion zone — never clear it by guesswork.

📍 应用场景：登船行李安检、违禁品处置、X 光判读考核

CCTV 监控CCTV Patrol

深度解析
  • 【是什么】安保部的「电子眼」体系：覆盖舷梯口、客舱走廊、收银台、电梯厅与限制区域的闭路电视网络，由监控室（CCTV room）值守员按轮巡表逐路巡视，是事前预警与事后取证的依据。
  • 【怎么做】按轮巡表逐路切换画面，重点盯防现金区与限制区域出入口；发现异常（尾随、徘徊、遗弃包裹）即时用无线电通知现场安保员处置；发生事件时标记时段、导出备份并填写调阅记录（audit trail）——谁能看、看了什么、何时看，全程留痕，录像仅限授权人员调阅。
  • 【易错点】把监控画面截图外发或向同事「分享八卦」——严重违反保密与数据保护规定，通常直接解职；发现异常只记录不上报。面试关联：「How do you handle CCTV footage of an incident?」——答：flag the timeframe, back it up, and log every access per the audit trail — footage is confidential。
Deep Dive
  • What: The security team's electronic-eyes network covering gangways, cabin corridors, cash points, elevator lobbies and restricted areas; operators in the CCTV room patrol cameras per the rotation list — for early warning and post-incident evidence.
  • How: Switch through cameras per the roster, watching cash areas and restricted-area entrances closely; on anomalies (tailgating, loitering, abandoned packages) radio the nearest officer immediately; for incidents, flag the timeframe, export a backup and complete the audit trail — who viewed what and when — with footage accessible only to authorized personnel.
  • Watch out: Screenshotting footage to share as gossip breaches confidentiality and data-protection rules — usually grounds for dismissal; spotting an anomaly without reporting defeats the system. Interview: "How do you handle CCTV footage of an incident?" — flag the timeframe, back it up, log every access per the audit trail — footage is confidential.

📍 应用场景：监控室值守、事件取证、数据保密合规

门禁与限制区域Access Control / Restricted Areas

深度解析
  • 【是什么】ISPS 保安体系的空间落地：船员工卡（crew card）按部门授权开启对应门锁，机舱、驾驶室、锚链舱、厨房后区等划为限制区域（restricted areas），非授权人员禁入。
  • 【怎么做】工卡权限随岗位变动即时调整（升职/调部门/离船即更新）；进入非本部门限制区域须登记并经值班安保官授权；日常巡查核对「门应关而未关」「门禁异常报警」并立即上报；带访客（供应商、检查官）进入限制区域全程陪同并登记进出时间。
  • 【易错点】帮同事「刷开门」或把自己的工卡借人——门禁记录指向你本人，出事即担责；看到防火门被楔住不处理。面试关联：「What are restricted areas?」——答：areas such as the engine room and bridge, access-controlled by ISPS — entry only with authorization and logging。
Deep Dive
  • What: The spatial layer of the ISPS regime: crew cards carry department-based access rights, and spaces like the engine room, bridge, chain locker and galley back areas are restricted areas off limits to unauthorized personnel.
  • How: Card privileges are updated immediately on transfer or promotion; entering a restricted area outside your department requires logging and Duty Security Officer approval; on rounds, check for doors left open or access-control alarms and report at once; escort and log all visitors (suppliers, inspectors) entering restricted areas.
  • Watch out: Holding a door open for a colleague or lending your card — the access log points at you, and you carry the liability; ignoring a fire door wedged open is another classic violation. Interview: "What are restricted areas?" — ISPS-controlled spaces such as the engine room and bridge; entry only with authorization and logging.

📍 应用场景：工卡权限管理、限制区域巡查、访客陪同

酒精检测Breathalyzer Test

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船司与 MLC（2006 海事劳工公约）框架下的酒精管控措施：对值班船员随机抽检（常见于开航前、港口日），确保值班人员血液酒精浓度不超标，保障航行与操作安全。
  • 【怎么做】抽检通常在集合点或岗位进行：使用校准合格的呼气检测仪，按操作说明采集呼气样本，当场宣读结果并请受检人签字确认；结果超限立即报告部门主管并停止当班，按公司纪律程序处理；检测结果书面记录存档。
  • 【易错点】凭「看起来没醉」放行同事而不测——判断必须以仪器为准，目测不构成免责；检测仪超校准期仍使用，结果无效还误导处置。面试关联：「Can a crew member be on duty after drinking?」——答：no — random breathalyzer checks apply, and anyone over the limit is stood down immediately。
Deep Dive
  • What: Alcohol control under company policy and the MLC 2006 framework: random breath tests for crew on watch (commonly before departure and on port days) to keep blood-alcohol levels within limits.
  • How: Test at muster points or workplaces using a calibrated breathalyzer, collect the sample per instructions, read the result aloud and have the person sign; anyone over the limit is reported to the head of department and stood down immediately, then handled under the disciplinary procedure; all results are recorded and filed.
  • Watch out: Waiving a test because a colleague "looks fine" — judgment rests on the device, not eyeballing; using a unit past its calibration date invalidates the result. Interview: "Can a crew member be on duty after drinking?" — no — random breathalyzer checks apply, and anyone over the limit is stood down immediately.

📍 应用场景：开航前抽检、值班酒精管控、纪律程序

常备 OTC 药品Over-the-counter (OTC) Medications

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船上医务室常备的非处方药：退烧止痛类（antipyretics/analgesics，如对乙酰氨基酚 paracetamol）、抗过敏类（antihistamines）、肠胃类（gastrointestinal，如抗酸剂 antacids、止泻药 anti-diarrheals）等，按船旗国要求配额存放。
  • 【怎么做】发放流程：宾客或船员到医务室描述症状 → 由护士/船医评估后发放（非医务人员不得给药，行李中自用药品除外）→ 逐条记录领用人、药名、剂量与时间；每航次清点补充，按效期先进先出（FEFO）。
  • 【易错点】船员之间「好心」分享自己的止痛药——给别人用药超出你的法律权限，过敏或相互作用出事即担责；发放不记录导致药品账物不符。面试关联：「Can you give a guest a painkiller yourself?」——答：no — only the medical team may dispense medication; I would escort them to the medical center。
Deep Dive
  • What: The medical center's stocked non-prescription drugs: antipyretics/analgesics (e.g. paracetamol), antihistamines and gastrointestinal remedies (antacids, anti-diarrheals), held in quantities required by the flag state.
  • How: Guests or crew describe symptoms at the medical center, where a nurse or doctor assesses and dispenses (non-medical staff must never give medication); every issue is logged — recipient, drug, dose and time; stock is replenished each voyage on a first-expiry-first-out (FEFO) basis.
  • Watch out: "Kindly" sharing your own painkillers with a crewmate exceeds your legal authority — an allergy or interaction makes you liable; unlogged dispensing breaks the medication audit trail. Interview: "Can you give a guest a painkiller yourself?" — no — only the medical team may dispense; escort them to the medical center.

📍 应用场景：医务室发药、全员用药红线、药品台账

处方药管理Controlled Drugs

深度解析
  • 【是什么】管制/处方药品（controlled drugs，如强效镇痛剂、镇静剂）的船上管理制度：因存在滥用与流入黑市风险，实行最严格的保管与记录要求。
  • 【怎么做】「双人双锁」保管（two-person, dual-key custody）：药品柜两把钥匙由两名授权人分别持有，取药必须两人在场；出入药逐笔登记入登记簿（register）——日期、药名、批号、数量、领用人、用途——并双人签核；定期盘点核对账物一致，差异立即上报船医与保安部。
  • 【易错点】图省事单人开柜取药——登记簿与门禁记录立刻暴露；登记簿用铅笔或涂改不留痕。面试关联：「How are controlled drugs stored onboard?」——答：under dual-key custody with a signed register for every transaction and regular reconciliation。
Deep Dive
  • What: The strict onboard regime for controlled drugs (strong analgesics, sedatives): because of misuse and diversion risks, storage and record-keeping requirements are the tightest of any stock.
  • How: Two-person, dual-key custody — the cabinet has two keys held by two authorized persons and any access needs both present; every transaction is entered in the register (date, drug, batch, quantity, recipient, purpose) with dual signatures; regular counts reconcile book against stock, with any variance reported to the doctor and Security immediately.
  • Watch out: Opening the cabinet alone "to save time" is exposed instantly by the register and access logs; pencil entries or untraceable corrections fail every audit. Interview: "How are controlled drugs stored onboard?" — dual-key custody with a signed register for every transaction and regular reconciliation.

📍 应用场景：管制药品保管、双签登记、定期盘点

船上药房Ship's Pharmacy

深度解析
  • 【是什么】医务室内的药品存储与管理单元：配备清单由船旗国要求与航线长短决定（依据客船医疗指南如 IMO Medical Guide 的基本药品与器械目录），是海上无岸基医疗支持时的生命线。
  • 【怎么做】日常管理：药品按类别分区存放（处方/OTC/急救）、标签清晰、效期动态监控；靠港时按消耗提交补货单（requisition）由供应商或代理（agent）补给；执行 FEFO（先到期先出），过期药品单独封存并按港口危险品/药品规定处置，绝不随生活垃圾丢弃。
  • 【易错点】新药入库压在旧药后面导致旧药过期浪费；过期药与普通垃圾混弃——多数港口对药品处置有专门规定。面试关联：「How is the ship's pharmacy restocked?」——答：stock is reviewed per voyage, replenished in port via the agent, with FEFO rotation and disposal per port regulations。
Deep Dive
  • What: The medical center's drug storage and management unit; its stock list is set by flag-state requirements and voyage length, following ship medical guide essentials — the lifeline when shore-based care is unavailable.
  • How: Store drugs by category (prescription/OTC/emergency) with clear labels and live expiry tracking; submit replenishment requisitions in port through suppliers or the agent; rotate stock first-expiry-first-out (FEFO); quarantine expired drugs and dispose of them under port pharmaceutical regulations — never with general garbage.
  • Watch out: Shelving new stock in front of old leads to expired waste; mixing expired drugs into regular garbage breaches most port rules. Interview: "How is the ship's pharmacy restocked?" — reviewed per voyage, replenished in port via the agent, FEFO rotation, disposal per port regulations.

📍 应用场景：药房日常管理、靠港补货、过期药处置

医疗记录Medical Log / Patient Confidentiality

深度解析
  • 【是什么】医务室的核心文书体系：每次就诊、用药、处置逐条记录（medical log），同时患者信息严格保密（patient confidentiality）——两者共同构成船上医疗的合规底线。
  • 【怎么做】记录要素：时间、主诉（complaints）、生命体征、处置与用药、随访安排，签名完整可追溯；病历妥善保存（航程与法定年限要求）；信息仅医疗团队与经授权人员可查阅，其他部门问询（包括部门经理、甚至「就问问是不是酒精相关」）一律以「患者隐私」为由不透露。
  • 【易错点】把「某船员昨晚去了医务室」当谈资传播——船上圈子小，泄密传播极快且属违纪；记录事后补写或无签名，医疗纠纷时无凭据。面试关联：「A manager asks why a crewmate saw the doctor. What do you say?」——答：patient information is confidential; only the medical team and authorized persons may access it。
Deep Dive
  • What: The medical center's core documentation: every consultation, medication and treatment entered in the medical log, paired with strict patient confidentiality — together the compliance baseline of onboard medicine.
  • How: Record time, presenting complaint, vital signs, treatment and medication, and follow-up, fully signed and traceable; keep records for the required retention period; information is accessible only to the medical team and authorized persons — inquiries from other departments, even managers, are declined on confidentiality grounds.
  • Watch out: Gossiping that "so-and-so went to the medical center" spreads fast in a small shipboard community and is a disciplinary breach; backdated or unsigned records leave no defense in a medical dispute. Interview: "A manager asks why a crewmate saw the doctor. What do you say?" — patient information is confidential; only the medical team and authorized persons may access it.

📍 应用场景：医务室文书、患者隐私保护、跨部门问询应对

转运送医Medevac (Medical Evacuation)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】重病重伤超出船上医疗能力时启动的医疗转运程序：轻症安排就近港口下船送医，重症协调直升机救援（helicopter evacuation）或海上拖带/接驳；由船医评估决策，配合岸上搜救机构（SAR）与海关检疫流程。
  • 【怎么做】船医评估并向船长建议 → 船长决定并与岸上公司医务协调、联系最近港口或 SAR → 保障部门准备文件（护照、病历摘要、保险信息）→ 安保与医护护送至舷梯/直升机吊运区并维持现场秩序 → 全程记录时间线供保险与调查使用。
  • 【易错点】非医疗岗位对宾客断言「没事/很严重」——病情判断与转运信息统一由船医发布；直升机吊运区周围闲人围观未及时清场。面试关联：「Who decides on a medevac?」——答：the ship's doctor advises and the Master decides, coordinated with shore-side medical support and SAR。
Deep Dive
  • What: The medical evacuation procedure when illness or injury exceeds onboard capability: minor cases are landed at the nearest port for treatment; critical cases may involve helicopter evacuation or rendezvous transfers — assessed by the ship's doctor, coordinated with shore-side search-and-rescue and customs/quarantine.
  • How: The doctor assesses and advises the Master → the Master decides, liaising with company medical support and the nearest port or SAR → guest services prepare documents (passport, medical summary, insurance) → security and medical staff escort the patient to the gangway or hoist area and control the scene → a full timeline is recorded for insurance and investigation.
  • Watch out: Non-medical staff must never characterize a guest's condition as "fine" or "serious" — the doctor owns all medical messaging; clear bystanders from the helicopter hoist area promptly. Interview: "Who decides on a medevac?" — the ship's doctor advises and the Master decides, coordinated with shore-side medical support and SAR.

📍 应用场景：重症转运决策、直升机吊运配合、岸上协调

心肺复苏CPR (Cardiopulmonary Resuscitation) / AED

深度解析
  • 【是什么】对心搏骤停者的基础生命支持（BLS, Basic Life Support）：胸外按压维持循环 + 人工呼吸，配合 AED（自动体外除颤器）尽早除颤，是 STCW 基本安全培训与各船司急救认证的核心内容。
  • 【怎么做】确认环境安全、拍肩呼唤判断反应 → 呼救并取 AED → 检查呼吸（不超过 10 秒）→ 无呼吸即开始按压：成人按压深度 5–6 cm、频率 100–120 次/分钟、按压与吹气比 30:2，每次按压后让胸廓完全回弹，尽量减少中断；AED 到场立即开机，按语音提示贴电极片（右锁骨下 + 左腋下），分析心律与放电时任何人不得接触患者，电击后立即继续按压。
  • 【易错点】按压深度不足（最常见的「无效按压」）或频率过快；AED 分析心律时仍在接触患者导致放电失败；害怕按断肋骨而力度保守——有效按压优先。面试必问：「What are the CPR compression rate and depth?」——答：100–120 per minute, 5–6 cm deep, 30:2 with rescue breaths。
Deep Dive
  • What: Basic Life Support (BLS) for cardiac arrest — chest compressions to maintain circulation plus rescue breaths, with early defibrillation using an AED; core to STCW basic safety training and every line's first-aid certification.
  • How: Confirm scene safety, check response with a shoulder tap → call for help and send for the AED → check breathing (max 10 seconds) → if absent, start compressions: adults at 5–6 cm depth, 100–120 per minute, 30:2 with breaths, full chest recoil after each compression, minimal interruptions; when the AED arrives, switch it on, follow the voice prompts, place pads (below the right collarbone and left armpit), touch no one during rhythm analysis or shock delivery, and resume compressions immediately after.
  • Watch out: Shallow compressions (the most common "ineffective CPR") or rushing the rate; touching the patient during AED analysis defeats the shock; don't press lightly out of fear of breaking ribs — effective compressions come first. Interview must-know: "What are the CPR compression rate and depth?" — 100–120 per minute, 5–6 cm deep, 30:2 with rescue breaths.

📍 应用场景：急救演练与真实骤停响应、AED 操作、STCW 培训考核

海姆立克法Choking / Heimlich Maneuver (Abdominal Thrusts)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】气道异物梗阻的急救手法（abdominal thrusts）：餐饮食pest与客房岗位最可能遇到的紧急情况——宾客就餐时噎住，黄金处置时间以分钟计。
  • 【怎么做】先判断：能咳嗽说话就鼓励用力咳嗽，不干预；不能发声、双手掐喉（universal choking sign）即施救：从背后环抱，拳头置于脐上两横指、另一手包住，快速向内向上冲击，反复直至异物排出；婴儿（<1 岁）禁用腹部冲击——改为 5 次拍背 + 5 次胸部按压交替；任何阶段患者失去反应立即呼救并转 CPR。
  • 【易错点】患者还能咳嗽就急着拍背冲击，反而使异物更深；冲击位置过高（打到胃底以上/胸骨）致伤；对婴儿用腹部冲击是严重错误。面试必问：「How do you help a choking adult?」——答：encourage coughing first; if unable to breathe, give abdominal thrusts above the navel until the object is out or they collapse, then CPR。
Deep Dive
  • What: First aid for a foreign-body airway obstruction (abdominal thrusts) — the emergency F&B and housekeeping staff are most likely to face when a guest chokes during a meal, where minutes decide the outcome.
  • How: Assess first — if the guest can cough or speak, encourage forceful coughing and don't intervene; if silent with hands clutching the throat (the universal choking sign), stand behind, place a fist two fingers above the navel, grasp it with the other hand and thrust sharply inward and upward until the object clears; for infants under 1 year, abdominal thrusts are forbidden — alternate 5 back blows with 5 chest thrusts; if the patient goes unresponsive at any point, call for help and start CPR.
  • Watch out: Slapping the back while the guest can still cough can drive the object deeper; thrusts placed too high cause injury; abdominal thrusts on an infant are a serious error. Interview must-know: "How do you help a choking adult?" — encourage coughing first; if unable to breathe, give abdominal thrusts above the navel until the object is out or they collapse, then CPR.

📍 应用场景：餐厅就餐噎食急救、客房与全岗位应急、急救认证考核

止血包扎Bleeding Control

深度解析
  • 【是什么】外伤出血的现场急救：按「直接压迫 → 加压包扎 → 止血带」的升级顺序控制出血，是 First Aid 认证的基础模块；大出血几分钟内即可危及生命。
  • 【怎么做】戴手套做好自我防护；首选直接压迫：干净敷料按住伤口持续加压，敷料浸透不更换而是叠加；无效则用加压包扎（pressure bandage）绷紧固定；四肢大出血仍无法控制时才使用止血带（tourniquet）：绑扎于伤口近心端 5–7 cm 处，拧紧至出血停止，必须记录绑扎时间并写在患者显眼处（额头/止血带上），每间隔按培训要求复核。
  • 【易错点】浸透的敷料揭开重盖——破坏已形成的凝血；随意使用止血带或松松绑上「意思一下」；忘记记录时间导致后续处置失据。面试关联：「When would you use a tourniquet?」——答：only for life-threatening limb bleeding that direct pressure cannot control, with the time noted prominently。
Deep Dive
  • What: First aid for traumatic bleeding, escalating from direct pressure to a pressure bandage to a tourniquet; a foundational first-aid module, since major bleeding can be fatal within minutes.
  • How: Glove up first; apply direct pressure with a clean dressing held firmly — if it soaks through, add layers rather than removing; escalate to a pressure bandage if ineffective; use a tourniquet only for life-threatening limb bleeding that pressure cannot control: place it 5–7 cm above the wound, tighten until bleeding stops, and record the application time prominently on the patient or the tourniquet.
  • Watch out: Peeling off soaked dressings destroys forming clots; applying a tourniquet casually or too loose is worse than none; forgetting the time undermines all later treatment. Interview: "When would you use a tourniquet?" — only for life-threatening limb bleeding that direct pressure cannot control, with the time noted prominently.

📍 应用场景：外伤出血现场处置、止血带操作、急救认证

烧烫伤Burns & Scalds

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船上高频意外伤害——厨房热油、蒸汽、热水壶、甲板暴晒都可能造成烧烫伤（burns/scalds），厨房与客房员工的急救必修课。
  • 【怎么做】立即用流动冷水（cool running water）冲淋伤处至少 20 分钟降温；冲淋同时小心去除手表、戒指等束缚物（肿胀前取下）；用干净敷料松散覆盖创面；严重烧伤（面积大、面部、呼吸道）立即呼救船医并持续降温；化学烧伤先按 MSDS 冲洗流程处理。
  • 【易错点】涂牙膏、黄油、酱油等「偏方」——妨碍散热且增加感染，送医时还要清创；挑破水泡；冰块直敷造成冻伤。面试关联：「A guest scalds a hand with coffee. What do you do?」——答：cool with running water for at least 20 minutes, remove rings and watches, cover loosely, and call the medical center。
Deep Dive
  • What: One of the most frequent shipboard injuries — hot oil, steam, kettles and sun exposure all cause burns and scalds; required knowledge for galley and housekeeping staff.
  • How: Cool the burn under cool running water for at least 20 minutes; while cooling, gently remove rings, watches and other constrictions before swelling sets in; cover the wound loosely with a clean dressing; for major burns (large area, face, airway) call the medical center immediately and keep cooling; chemical burns follow the MSDS flushing procedure.
  • Watch out: Toothpaste, butter or soy sauce trap heat, raise infection risk and complicate debridement; never burst blisters; direct ice causes frostbite. Interview: "A guest scalds a hand with coffee. What do you do?" — cool with running water for at least 20 minutes, remove rings and watches, cover loosely, and call the medical center.

📍 应用场景：厨房烫伤、宾客事故处置、急救培训演练

骨折固定Fracture & Splinting

深度解析
  • 【是什么】疑似骨折（楼梯摔落、运动受伤、加床跌倒）的现场处理原则：固定（splinting）优先于复位，目标是防止二次损伤并安全送医。
  • 【怎么做】就地固定：用夹板或临时器材（卷起的毛巾、硬纸板、枕头）固定伤肢，固定范围超过上下两个关节；不尝试复位或拉直畸形；固定后检查末梢循环（末梢颜色、温度、感觉、脉搏），过紧即放松重绑；脊柱疑似伤员绝不移动，保持原位等船医；完成后尽快送医务室或启动 Medevac。
  • 【易错点】热心「帮伤者把歪了的手掰直」——造成血管神经二次损伤；夹板绑过紧不查末梢循环；随意搬动疑似脊柱伤员。面试关联：「A guest falls and the arm looks deformed. What do you do?」——答：don't realign it, splint in position checking circulation, and call the medical team。
Deep Dive
  • What: The field response to suspected fractures (stair falls, sports injuries, falls from beds): immobilize, never reduce — the goal is preventing secondary injury and safe transport.
  • How: Splint in place using a splint or improvised materials (rolled towels, cardboard, pillows), immobilizing the joints above and below; never attempt to realign or straighten deformity; after splinting, check circulation (color, temperature, sensation, pulse) and loosen if too tight; never move a suspected spinal injury — hold position and wait for the medical team; then transfer to the medical center or start a medevac.
  • Watch out: "Helpfully" straightening a deformed arm injures nerves and vessels; a splint tied too tight with no circulation check; moving a suspected spinal patient. Interview: "A guest falls and the arm looks deformed. What do you do?" — don't realign it, splint in position checking circulation, and call the medical team.

📍 应用场景：跌倒骨折处置、夹板固定、脊柱伤保护

中暑与失温Heat Stroke & Hypothermia

深度解析
  • 【是什么】两种体温调节急症，邮轮场景对应「两极」：热带航线甲板暴晒的中暑（heat stroke），与寒冷海域落水后的失温（hypothermia）——MOB 救援后的必处理项。
  • 【怎么做】中暑：移至阴凉处、脱减衣物、喷水扇风或冰敷颈、腋、腹股沟大血管处快速降温，清醒者小口补含盐饮品；意识不清不喂水，按急症呼救。失温：移入室内、换除湿衣，用干燥毯子逐步复温，先裹躯干后裹四肢，可给清醒者温热饮品；避免急剧加温（热水袋直贴皮肤）与搓揉肢体（加重冷血回流心律失常）。
  • 【易错点】把中暑当「晒晕了歇会就好」——heat stroke 可致命，降温就是急救；对失温者立即泡热水或猛搓手脚——外周冷血回流可诱发心搏骤停。面试关联：「A crewmate is pulled from cold water — first aid?」——答：remove wet clothing, rewarm gradually with dry blankets, core first, and get medical help。
Deep Dive
  • What: Two thermoregulation emergencies mapped to cruise reality: heat stroke from tropical-deck sun exposure, and hypothermia after falling into cold water — the mandatory follow-up to any MOB rescue.
  • How: Heat stroke — move to shade, loosen clothing, spray with water and fan or ice the neck, armpits and groin over the great vessels, give small sips of salty fluids only if fully alert; if unresponsive, treat as an emergency and call for help. Hypothermia — move indoors, remove wet clothing, rewarm gradually with dry blankets, torso first then limbs, warm drinks for the conscious; avoid sudden heat (hot-water bottles on skin) and rubbing limbs, which drives cold blood back to the core and can trigger arrhythmia.
  • Watch out: Writing off heat stroke as "just overheated" is dangerous — it can be fatal and rapid cooling is the treatment; dunking a hypothermic person in hot water or vigorously rubbing their hands and feet can trigger cardiac arrest. Interview: "A crewmate is pulled from cold water — first aid?" — remove wet clothing, rewarm gradually with dry blankets, core first, and get medical help.

📍 应用场景：热带航线防暑、MOB 救援后复温、全岗位急救

娱乐场6 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

荷官学校与执照Dealer School & License

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船司（或合作赌场运营方）设立的内部培训机构，结业即取得发牌资格认证；这是娱乐场岗位的上岗门槛，也是薪资等级的起点。
  • 【怎么做】培训通常 4–8 周，内容包括各游戏规则（Blackjack、Roulette、Baccarat 等）、发牌手法、筹码计算速度（一般要求 20–30 秒内完成一摞筹码点算）与仪表规范；结业需通过实操考核，误差率超标即重训。转岗到其他船司或跳槽到岸上赌场常需重新认证，各辖区执照互不通用。
  • 【易错点】新人误以为会玩扑克就能当荷官——考核的核心是「零差错 + 速度 + 手在镜头前可见（hand above the table）」；面试常问「你为什么想做荷官 / 能否接受每周 60–70 小时轮班」，应结合培训经历作答而非泛谈兴趣。
Deep Dive
  • What: The casino operator's in-house training program; graduating from dealer school with certification is the entry requirement for any table-game position and the starting point of the pay grade.
  • How: Training usually runs 4–8 weeks covering game rules (Blackjack, Roulette, Baccarat), dealing technique, chip arithmetic (you must count a stack within roughly 20–30 seconds) and grooming standards. Graduation requires passing a live audited exam; excessive error rates mean retraining. Certification is not portable — moving to another line or a land-based casino often means re-certification.
  • Watch out: New hires assume "good at poker = dealer." The exam tests zero-error speed with hands always visible above the table. In interviews, expect "Why do you want to be a dealer? Can you handle 60–70-hour weeks?" — answer with concrete school experience, not general enthusiasm.

📍 应用场景：娱乐场岗位入职培训与考核环节；面试问答（Cruise Staff / Dealer 面试）

洗牌与切牌Shuffle & Cut

深度解析
  • 【是什么】牌靴（shoe）换牌或新局开始前对牌组执行的防作弊标准动作：洗牌（shuffle）打乱牌序，切牌（cut）插入切牌卡（cut card）确认牌组未被标记或预置。
  • 【怎么做】流程固定且全程在监控（eye in the sky）下进行：换靴时当众拆封新牌（usually two decks per game）、按口令完成多次洗牌动作，请一位宾客或由荷官执行切牌，切牌卡位置决定发牌起点；每个动作有口令与节奏要求，监管审计会回放录像抽查动作是否合规、有无多余动作。
  • 【易错点】新人发牌过快或省略「请宾客切牌」环节，会被审计判定程序违规；另一常见错误是手部离开台面或牌桌可视区，即使无辜也会被回查。检查要点：口令到位、切牌卡可见、动作与录像可逐帧复核。
Deep Dive
  • What: The standard anti-cheating routine performed before a new shoe or round: shuffling randomizes the deck order, and the cut (inserting a cut card) confirms the deck has not been marked or stacked.
  • How: The sequence is fixed and always under the "eye in the sky": open the sealed new deck in full view (usually two decks per game), complete the prescribed shuffle calls, invite a guest or the dealer to cut, and let the cut card position determine the deal start. Auditors replay CCTV to verify every movement against the standard calls.
  • Watch out: New dealers who skip the guest cut or deal too fast are flagged for procedure violations; hands drifting below table level — even innocently — trigger a review. Key checks: calls delivered, cut card visible, every move frame-verifiable on tape.

📍 应用场景：赌台开局与换靴环节；娱乐场内部审计与监控复核

赌台限额Table Limits

深度解析
  • 【是什么】每张赌台公示的最小/最大下注区间（如 US$5–1,000，具体以船司为准），是赌场风险控制与分舱定价的基础：低限额台面向休闲客，高限额台服务 VIP。
  • 【怎么做】开局前须核对台面限额牌（limit sign）与系统设置一致；开航后运营方可能按客流动态调整限额，调整须经上级批准并同步更新标识。宾客提出超限下注时，荷官应礼貌拒绝并引导至对应限额的台，不得口头破例。
  • 【易错点】新人最常见的错误是接受「就这一手加一点」的口头请求——任何超限下注都属违规，且录像可查；另一疏漏是换限额后忘记更新/擦净台面标识，导致宾客投诉「与公示不符」。面试关联：常被问「客人坚持超额下注你怎么办」，标准答案是重申限额并请楼层经理（pit boss）介入。
Deep Dive
  • What: The posted minimum/maximum bet range at each table (e.g., US$5–1,000, varies by line), the foundation of casino risk control and tiered pricing: low-limit tables serve casual players, high-limit rooms serve VIPs.
  • How: Verify the limit sign matches the system setting before opening; limits may be adjusted by management during the voyage with approval and updated signage. Politely refuse over-limit bets and direct guests to the appropriate table — never make verbal exceptions.
  • Watch out: Accepting "just this once, a little more" requests is the classic rookie mistake — any over-limit bet is a violation caught on tape. Also, forgetting to update signage after a limit change causes "the sign said otherwise" complaints. Interview staple: "What if a guest insists on exceeding the limit?" — restate the limit and call the pit boss.

📍 应用场景：赌台日常运营与限额调整环节；宾客沟通与楼层管理（pit boss）协同

VIP 与会员积分Casino Points / Comps

深度解析
  • 【是什么】宾客凭会员卡下注累积积分（points），可兑换免费舱房、餐饮、饮品或船票抵扣（comps，complimentary 的缩写）；积分体系同时是赌场营销与客人分层管理的核心数据。
  • 【怎么做】宾客开卡后，荷官须确保卡在下注时被系统记录（rating the player），按平均下注额与时长计算理论值（theo）；收银处凭积分办理兑换，兑换规则（门槛、有效期、可抵扣项目）以当期宣传口径为准。VIP 台位的邀请、预留与楼层经理审批流程也挂在这套体系上。
  • 【易错点】漏刷或忘录会员卡是高频投诉来源——宾客下注整晚积分未入账，只能事后调录像补偿；其次是不清楚兑换门槛就随意承诺「能换免费房」，引发纠纷。检查要点：每局开始前确认卡已插入读卡器、口头确认积分状态，任何兑换承诺以收银系统显示为准。
Deep Dive
  • What: Guests earn points while playing with their loyalty card, redeemable for complimentary cabins, dining, drinks or cruise-fare credits (comps, short for complimentary). The points system also powers casino marketing and player-tier management.
  • How: Dealers must make sure the card is rated during play — the system calculates theoretical value from average bet and time at the table. Redemptions are processed at the cashier per current published rules (thresholds, expiry, eligible items). VIP table invitations and reservations also run through this system with pit-boss approval.
  • Watch out: Forgetting to rate the card is a top complaint source — an unrated night can only be fixed by tape review. Never promise "a free cabin" without checking the redemption threshold. Key habits: confirm the card is inserted before each round, verbally confirm point status, and quote only what the cashier system shows.

📍 应用场景：会员开卡与积分录入（rating）环节；收银兑换与 VIP 宾客服务

领海禁赌Casino Closed in Territorial Waters

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船舶进入一国领海（距海岸线 12 海里以内）及停靠港口期间，娱乐场必须停止营业（not in operation within territorial waters / while alongside）——中国等多数沿海国家法律明确要求；这是娱乐场运营的硬性红线，违反属重大合规事故，可能导致罚款、扣船或吊销运营许可。
  • 【怎么做】营业时间由船长室发布指令，娱乐场经理据此排班：通常在进入领海前完成清台、结账与闭场广播，锚定/靠港期间关闭赌台、封存筹码并配合检查；驶离领海（12 海里外）后经确认方可重新开场。公海上「领海边界」常由驾驶台按 GPS 与航线通报，部门须以官方通报而非自行估算为准。
  • 【易错点】新人易犯的错误包括：临近领海仍接待宾客下注（哪怕「最后一局」）、对宾客抱怨解释不清法规依据、以及忘记关闭积分投注终端。检查要点：营业开关以船长室指令为准、闭场前完成结账与人数清点、对宾客统一口径说明「进入领海/靠港期间赌场依法关闭」。
Deep Dive
  • What: When the ship enters a nation's territorial waters (within 12 nautical miles of the coast) or is alongside in port, the casino must close — a legal requirement in China and most coastal states. It is a hard compliance line: violations count as serious incidents and can bring fines, detention or loss of operating permission.
  • How: Operating hours follow the Captain's standing orders; the casino manager schedules around them. Before entering territorial waters, close out tables, settle balances and announce closure; while anchored or alongside, keep tables closed and chips secured until cleared to reopen beyond 12 nm. The bridge reports the boundary by GPS and track — rely on official announcements, not personal estimates.
  • Watch out: Never let guests place bets near the boundary (not even "one last hand"); be ready to explain the law clearly to frustrated guests; and don't forget to shut down point-rating terminals. Key checks: open/close strictly on the Captain's orders, settle and count before closing, and give guests one consistent explanation: "The casino is closed by law within territorial waters and while in port."

📍 应用场景：进出领海与靠港/锚泊时段的娱乐场闭场与重开流程；对宾客的法规解释

反洗钱报告AML Reporting

深度解析
  • 【是什么】娱乐场是洗钱高风险场所，达到辖区申报阈值的现金交易必须提交申报文件并留存记录（如单笔或累计 US$10,000 以上，具体以辖区规定为准）；这是赌场收银与财务的法定义务，也是监管审计的重点。
  • 【怎么做】宾客以现金购买筹码或兑现超过阈值时，收银须识别身份、填写申报表（CTR/Currency Transaction Report 类文件）、登记护照信息并存档备查；「结构性拆分」（structuring，故意拆成多笔规避申报）本身即违法，员工发现疑似拆分也须上报。
  • 【易错点】最严重的红线是提示或协助宾客规避申报（如暗示「分开几次换就不会被登记」）——在任何辖区都属于刑事层面的违规，员工会被立即解雇并可能被追责；其次是漏记累计交易，只盯单笔金额。检查要点：累计口径统计当日多笔现金交易、申报文件双人复核、绝不向宾客透露申报策略细节。
Deep Dive
  • What: Casinos are high-risk venues for money laundering. Cash transactions at or above the jurisdiction's reporting threshold (e.g., single or aggregated transactions of US$10,000+, per local rules) must be reported and documented — a legal duty of the cashier and finance team and a key audit focus.
  • How: When a guest buys in or cashes out above the threshold, identify the guest, complete the report form (CTR-type documentation), log passport details and file the record. Structuring — deliberately splitting transactions to stay under the threshold — is itself illegal, and suspected structuring must also be escalated.
  • Watch out: The gravest red line is tipping off or helping a guest evade reporting (e.g., hinting "split it into smaller amounts") — a criminal-level violation in any jurisdiction, an instant-dismissal offense. Also track aggregated daily cash transactions, not just single amounts. Key checks: aggregate same-day cash deals, dual-review every report, and never reveal reporting strategy to guests.

📍 应用场景：娱乐场收银大额现金交易环节；合规审计与员工红线培训

岸上观光部5 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

接驳艇运营Tender Operations

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船舶无法直接靠泊码头（锚泊/anchoring）时，用救生艇或专用接驳艇（tender boats）往返岸边运送宾客的整套运营体系；这是岸上观光部最复杂、安全要求最高的现场作业之一。
  • 【怎么做】按接驳券编号（tender ticket numbers）分批次放行，避免码头拥堵；单艇严格限载（按救生艇认证人数，通常每人计一，儿童含在内）；行动不便宾客、轮椅使用者优先安排并安排专人协助；每航次发船前后清点人数，岸侧与船侧各设引导岗，恶劣海况（浪高超限）立即停航并广播说明。
  • 【易错点】新人常犯：不看批次号就放行宾客（导致编号系统失效）、忘报轮椅宾客需求（岸侧无准备）、最后一班未与岸上收尾组对齐人数。检查要点：票据与批次一致、单艇人数记录留档、紧急停航时先用双语广播安抚宾客再调整行程。
Deep Dive
  • What: The full operation of ferrying guests ashore and back by lifeboats or dedicated tender boats when the ship cannot dock alongside (at anchor). It is one of the Shore Excursions team's most complex, safety-critical operations.
  • How: Release guests in batches by tender ticket number to avoid pier congestion; enforce strict per-boat load limits (per the lifeboat certification count, every guest including children counts); prioritize guests with reduced mobility and assign escorts; count guests before and after every run with guides posted on both sides. Stop operations immediately when sea conditions exceed limits and announce the reason.
  • Watch out: Common rookie errors: boarding guests without checking their batch number (breaking the system), failing to pre-report wheelchair guests to the shore side, and not reconciling headcount with the shore team on the last run. Key checks: tickets match batches, per-boat counts logged, and any suspension is announced bilingually before schedule changes.

📍 应用场景：锚泊港口的宾客上下船环节；接驳艇安全与批次管理

返船时间All Aboard Time

深度解析
  • 【是什么】每个港口日宾客必须回船的最后期限，通常为开航前 30–60 分钟（以每日通告 Daily Programme 为准）；这是岸上观光部与全船广播、前厅、安保联动的关键时间节点。
  • 【怎么做】部门须在岸上游、广播、电梯屏与港口信息台多渠道反复提示；开航前通常按「1 小时 / 30 分钟 / 15 分钟 / 最后登船」节奏倒计时广播；全部宾客回船后与安保完成人数核对，方可通知驾驶台准备开航。
  • 【易错点】最需要反复向宾客讲清的风险：迟到者可能被甩港（left behind），后续行程自理、费用自担（自行前往下一港口汇合），船司不承担任何费用；员工不得口头承诺「船会等」——是否等候由船长依全船情况决定。面试关联：常问「客人快到点没回船你怎么处理」，要点是立即上报值班经理并按应急流程联系，而非自行等待。
Deep Dive
  • What: The deadline by which all guests must be back on board each port day, usually 30–60 minutes before sailing (per the Daily Programme). It is a critical coordination point between Shore Excursions, PA announcements, Guest Services and Security.
  • How: Repeat the time through shore tours, PA calls, elevator screens and the port information desk; run countdown announcements (typically 1 hour / 30 / 15 / final boarding); after the last guest boards, reconcile headcount with Security before the bridge is cleared to sail.
  • Watch out: Make the risk unmistakable: latecomers may be left behind and must travel to the next port at their own expense — the line covers nothing. Never promise "the ship will wait"; that call belongs to the Captain. Interview staple: "A guest is missing near all-aboard" — escalate to the duty manager immediately and follow the emergency contact procedure instead of waiting on your own.

📍 应用场景：港口日返船提醒与倒计时广播环节；甩港风险沟通与应急上报

团体游与自由行Guided Tours vs Independent Exploration

深度解析
  • 【是什么】宾客在港口日的两种主要游玩方式：团体游（guided tours）由船司审核的岸上供应商承接，含交通、导游与保障，出问题有售后渠道；自由行（independent exploration）由宾客自行安排，时间灵活但风险自担。
  • 【怎么做】员工应向宾客客观说明两者差异：团体游有统一的返船保障（迟到团体会等待或协调）、行程变更时有船方介入；自由行则须自行掌握返船时间与当地应急电话。销售团体游时如实介绍行程与限制（如步行强度、含餐情况），不做夸大承诺。
  • 【易错点】员工的两条红线：一是不得向宾客推介或承诺特定岸上商家（包括「这家店东西好」之类的话），岸上购物纠纷极易追责到推荐员工；二是不能只报喜不报忧——必须讲清自由行的返船风险。检查要点：推荐口径与官方 tour brochure 一致、自由行宾客被明确告知 all aboard time 与当地紧急联系方式。
Deep Dive
  • What: The two ways guests spend a port day: guided tours are run by vetted shore-side vendors with transport, guides and recourse if things go wrong; independent exploration is self-arranged — flexible in timing but entirely at the guest's own risk.
  • How: Explain the trade-offs objectively: tours carry an all-aboard guarantee (the ship or operator coordinates for late tour groups) and ship-side support for itinerary changes; independent travelers must manage their own all-aboard time and local emergency numbers. Sell tours honestly — itinerary, walking level and meal inclusions as published, with no inflated promises.
  • Watch out: Two red lines for staff: never recommend or endorse specific shore-side merchants (even "that shop is great" — shopping disputes trace back to whoever recommended it), and never gloss over the risks of going independent. Key checks: sales pitch matches the official tour brochure, and independent guests are clearly told the all-aboard time and local emergency contacts.

📍 应用场景：港口日前夕的行程咨询与岸上游销售环节；宾客沟通合规口径

岸上安全提示Port Safety Briefing

深度解析
  • 【是什么】每次靠港前向宾客发布的安全信息简报，内容与安保部门的港口评估一致，涵盖目的地的治安、交通、食品饮水与应急安排，是船司对宾客的法定告知义务之一。
  • 【怎么做】标准内容包括：贵重物品保管建议（护照留存或贴身携带的取舍）、当地交通风险（如无牌照出租车、摩托艇租赁隐患）、食品与饮水注意事项、港口紧急联系方式（含船上应急电话与当地报警电话）、回船路线与 all aboard time。发布渠道为船上广播、Daily Programme 与岸上观光台当面提示。
  • 【易错点】新人常把简报念成走过场，漏掉最关键的三件事：当地应急电话、回船路线、水上活动的安全警示；另一问题是口径与安保简报不一致（如治安描述互相矛盾），会造成宾客困惑。检查要点：每日按安保部门当期简报更新内容、双语播报、在码头设点重复提示。
Deep Dive
  • What: The safety briefing delivered before every port call, aligned with the Security department's port assessment. It covers local safety, transport, food and water, and emergency arrangements — part of the line's duty to inform guests.
  • How: Standard content: valuables guidance (carry-on vs. in-safe decisions), transport risks (unlicensed taxis, jet-ski rentals), food and water precautions, emergency contacts (ship's emergency number and local police), the route back to the pier and the all-aboard time. Channels: PA announcements, the Daily Programme and face-to-face reminders at the excursions desk.
  • Watch out: Rookies tend to read the script without the three essentials: local emergency numbers, the way back to the ship, and water-activity warnings. Conflicting wording with the Security briefing (e.g., differing crime descriptions) confuses guests. Key checks: update daily from Security's current briefing, deliver bilingually, and repeat the message at the gangway.

📍 应用场景：靠港前广播与码头现场提示环节；与安保部门简报的一致性核对

供应商质量抽查Vendor Audit

深度解析
  • 【是什么】岸上观光部对合作供应商（车辆、导游、餐厅、活动运营商）进行的定期质量检查，通过随团观察与评分形成审计报告，是供应商分级与续约决策的依据。
  • 【怎么做】专员随团全程观察：车况与准点率、导游讲解质量与安全提示执行、餐食卫生与菜单兑现、活动安全装备与应急预案；按评分表逐项打分并记录宾客现场反馈，报告提交总部采购/体验团队；连续不达标的供应商将暂停合作或限期整改。
  • 【易错点】常见问题：评分流于形式（只打分不记录具体事例，报告无说服力）；对宾客投诉的岸上问题没有当日闭环（应记录车号/导游名/时间点以便追溯）。检查要点：抽查覆盖不同航次与时段而非只看「表演团」、证据留痕（照片、票据、宾客评价编号）、整改项有复检日期。
Deep Dive
  • What: Periodic quality checks the Shore Excursions team runs on vendors (coaches, guides, restaurants, activity operators), based on riding along with tours and scoring against a checklist — the basis for vendor tiering and contract renewal decisions.
  • How: An auditor shadows the full tour: vehicle condition and punctuality, guide quality and safety briefings, food hygiene and menu delivery, safety equipment and emergency readiness for activities. Score every item, capture live guest feedback, and submit the report to the head-office sourcing/experience team; vendors that repeatedly fail are suspended or put on corrective timelines.
  • Watch out: Scores without documented incidents carry no weight — always note specific examples. Shore-side complaints must be closed the same day (log coach number, guide name, time). Key checks: audit different voyages and time slots, not just the "showcase" tour; keep evidence (photos, receipts, guest comment references); and give every corrective item a re-audit date.

📍 应用场景：随团质检与供应商评审环节；岸上游采购与续约决策支持

财务部5 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

宾客账单审计Folio Audit

深度解析
  • 【是什么】对宾客船上账户（folio，即挂账账单）的每晚对账工作：核对餐饮、酒吧、免税店、Spa、岸上游等全部挂账交易与原始凭证是否一致；离船结算（disembarkation settlement）前必须把争议清零，否则会影响离船速度与宾客体验。
  • 【怎么做】夜审（night auditor）逐日导出交易清单与签单凭证比对，重点核查：重复入账（double posting）、挂错房号（wrong room charge）、无签名或无凭证签单（unsigned/unsupported charges）、折扣与促销价是否正确入账；发现差异当日追溯责任部门并调账，账务调整须留痕审批。
  • 【易错点】高频漏洞：只看总额不做明细抽样（漏掉同房号下的错挂）、忽视免单/补偿类交易（comp adjustments）缺少审批单；离船日上午集中出现争议时会拖慢整个下船流程。面试关联：常问「客人对账单有异议怎么办」——先致歉、当场调出凭证逐笔核对、能当场更正的立即更正并出具调整单。
Deep Dive
  • What: The nightly reconciliation of each guest's onboard account (folio) against source documents for every posted charge — dining, bars, shops, spa, shore excursions. All disputes must be cleared before disembarkation settlement, or they slow the whole gangway and hurt guest scores.
  • How: The night auditor compares the daily transaction listing with signed receipts, focusing on double postings, charges to the wrong stateroom, unsigned or unsupported checks, and mis-posted discounts or promotions. Discrepancies are traced to the responsible outlet and adjusted the same day, with approvals on file.
  • Watch out: Typical gaps: checking totals without sampling line items (missing mis-posted charges under the same cabin number) and comp adjustments lacking approval slips. Piled-up disputes on disembarkation morning stall the entire process. Interview staple: "A guest disputes their folio" — apologize, pull the vouchers, review line by line, correct on the spot and issue an adjustment slip.

📍 应用场景：夜审与离船结算环节；各营收部门挂账凭证管理

现金与外汇管理Cash & FX Handling

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船上现金收付与外汇兑换（FX）的保管与控制制度：双人保管（dual custody）、保险柜分级存放、兑换按当日牌价执行并全程留痕；船舶是封闭流动环境，现金风控比岸上更严格。
  • 【怎么做】收银点每日多次缴款入库（cash lift），双人开封、双人清点并签收；保险柜按面额与用途分级存放，钥匙与密码分人持有；外汇兑换按当日公布的牌价（以船司财务口径为准）操作并出具回执；港口日与岸上银行交割现金时须双人押运、全程交接签单。
  • 【易错点】单人操作现金（哪怕「就一分钟」）即属违规，录像与审计均可查；兑换牌价未更新（用了前一日汇率）会造成系统性差额；交接单漏签是审计常见缺陷项。检查要点：每一次开封/缴款都有双人签名、牌价按日更新并公示、押运交接记录完整可追溯。
Deep Dive
  • What: The custody and control regime for onboard cash and foreign exchange: dual custody, tiered safe storage, and FX exchanged at the day's published rate with a full paper trail. On a sealed, moving vessel, cash controls are stricter than ashore.
  • How: Cashier stations make multiple cash lifts daily; every safe opening is two-person with joint counts and signatures. Safes are tiered by denomination and purpose, with keys and codes held by different people. FX is converted at the daily posted rate (per the line's finance standard) with a receipt issued; port-day bank deliveries move cash under two-person escort with signed handovers.
  • Watch out: Handling cash alone — even "just for a minute" — is a violation, visible on CCTV and in audits. Stale FX rates (using yesterday's) create systematic variances, and missing signatures on handovers are a classic audit finding. Key checks: dual signatures on every open/lift, rates updated and posted daily, escort logs complete and traceable.

📍 应用场景：收银缴款与保险柜管理环节；港口日岸上银行交割

每日营业报表Daily Revenue Report

深度解析
  • 【是什么】汇总全船各营收点（餐饮、酒吧、免税店、Spa、岸上观光、娱乐场等）当日收入的报表，与 POS/收银系统对账后报送船司总部，是总部评估航次业绩与调配资源的核心数据。
  • 【怎么做】各部门在营业结束后按截止时间上报日结数据；财务核对 POS 系统总额、现金/刷卡/挂账三种支付方式的分项与实物（现金抽点）一致后汇总成报；差异须当日查明并在报表中注明原因，不允许「带差异上报」。
  • 【易错点】常见错误：部门迟报导致报表拼不齐、支付方式分类错误（挂账被记成现金）、只对系统数不对现金实物（短溢discrepancy被发现时已隔夜）。检查要点：上报截止时间刚性执行、三项支付渠道逐项勾稽、现金抽点比例按制度执行并留痕。
Deep Dive
  • What: The daily consolidation of revenue from every outlet onboard — dining, bars, shops, spa, shore excursions, casino — reconciled against POS/cashier systems and reported to head office. It is the core dataset head office uses to judge voyage performance and allocate resources.
  • How: Each outlet submits end-of-day figures by the deadline; finance reconciles POS totals across cash, card and room-charge payment types, spot-counts cash, and consolidates the report. Variances must be explained the same day in the report — submitting with unexplained gaps is not allowed.
  • Watch out: Usual failures: late outlet submissions that break consolidation, misclassified payment types (room charges booked as cash), and reconciling system figures without physical cash counts (over/shorts discovered a day late). Key checks: hard submission deadlines, three payment channels cross-checked line by line, cash spot-check rates per policy with records kept.

📍 应用场景：每日日结与总部上报环节；各营收部门日结协作

反洗钱合规AML Compliance

深度解析
  • 【是什么】全船范围的反洗钱（AML，Anti-Money Laundering）合规体系：大额现金交易按辖区阈值申报（如 US$10,000，以辖区规定为准），船上收银与娱乐场收银共用同一合规口径，由合规官/财务负责人统筹。
  • 【怎么做】建立可疑交易识别机制：短期多笔接近阈值、频繁现金兑付、无消费逻辑的大额充值等均须记录并上报；申报文件按辖区要求留存备查（通常 5 年以上）；全员接受 AML 培训，收银与赌场员工每年复训；辖区更替时（进入不同水域）适用阈值与规则以最新合规通告为准。
  • 【易错点】与 kb-7-6 同源的红线：员工绝不可提示宾客规避申报或协助拆分交易，一经查实立即解雇并可能承担法律责任；次常见问题是各收银点口径不一（赌场申报了、前台漏了同一位客人的大额现金）。检查要点：全船收银点共享可疑客人提示（profile alerts）、培训记录完整、阈值更新及时传达到岗。
Deep Dive
  • What: The shipwide Anti-Money Laundering framework: cash transactions at or above the jurisdiction's threshold (e.g., US$10,000, per local rules) must be reported, with ship cashiers and casino cashiers applying one consistent compliance standard under the compliance officer / head of finance.
  • How: Maintain suspicious-activity detection: multiple near-threshold transactions in a short window, frequent cash-outs, or illogical large deposits are logged and escalated. Reports are retained per jurisdiction (typically 5+ years); all staff take AML training, with annual refreshers for cash and casino staff. When waters change, thresholds and rules follow the latest compliance bulletin.
  • Watch out: The same red line as the casino side: never tip off a guest or help structure transactions — proven cases mean immediate dismissal and potential legal liability. Another common gap: inconsistent standards across cashier points (casino files the report, front desk misses the same guest's large cash). Key checks: shipwide suspect alerts shared across stations, training records complete, threshold updates reach every position.

📍 应用场景：全船收银点大额现金申报环节；AML 培训与跨部门口径统一

MLC 工资支付规范MLC 2006 Wage Rules

深度解析
  • 【是什么】《海事劳工公约》（MLC 2006）对船员工资的强制要求：按约定周期（通常每月）足额支付、工资单清晰列明各项构成（基薪、加班、津贴、扣款）、不得无依据克扣；财务部与 HR 共同执行，港口国监督检查（PSC）会核查。
  • 【怎么做】工资单（payslip）须逐项列明收入与扣款并可供船员随时索取；扣款仅限合同或法规允许的项目（如税款、经同意的汇款安排）；工资争议可通过船上申诉机制（onboard complaint procedure）逐级反映，公约要求处理过程有记录、不因申诉而报复。
  • 【易错点】高频疑问是「小费/提成怎么算」——不同船司、不同岗位（如餐厅 vs 客房）口径不同，必须以本人合同条款为准，HR 口径与工资单应一致；新人另需注意：工资发放日与币种在签约时即锁定，中途不应被单方变更。检查要点：工资单项目完整、扣款均有依据文件、申诉渠道在船员餐厅/公告栏公示。
Deep Dive
  • What: The Maritime Labour Convention 2006 sets mandatory wage rules for seafarers: payment in full at agreed intervals (usually monthly), payslips itemizing every component (base pay, overtime, allowances, deductions), and no unauthorized deductions. Finance and HR run this jointly, and Port State Control inspects it.
  • How: Payslips must itemize earnings and deductions and be available to crew on request; deductions are limited to items allowed by the contract or law (taxes, agreed remittances). Wage disputes go through the onboard complaint procedure, escalating step by step — handled with records and no retaliation, as the Convention requires.
  • Watch out: The most common question is "how are tips/commissions paid?" — it differs by line and position (dining vs. housekeeping), so always defer to the individual contract; HR's verbal answers must match the payslip. Note that pay date and currency are fixed at signing and cannot be changed unilaterally. Key checks: complete payslip line items, documented basis for every deduction, complaint procedure posted in crew messes.

📍 应用场景：工资发放与工资单管理环节；工资争议申诉与港口国监督检查应对

人力资源部5 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

登船与离船手续Sign On / Sign Off

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船员合同开始与结束时的标准行政流程：登船（sign on）核验资质并完成船上注册，离船（sign off）结清事务并归档，全程受 MLC 与船旗国要求约束，记录可供 PSC 与船司审计调阅。
  • 【怎么做】登船日核验： employment contract、体检证书（medical certificate）、护照、海员证及职位所需证书；随后办理船员卡（crew card）、开门权限、集合站（muster station）分配与安全须知确认；离船日反向办理：结清个人账目、归还装备与制服、完成离职面谈（debrief）与档案归档，机票与后续安排通常由 HR/中介协调。
  • 【易错点】新人高频失误：证书有效期临近合同结束仍未预警续办（导致下个合同无法登船）；离船时个人账单未清零被扣留办理；遗漏归还钥匙/门禁卡产生赔偿争议。面试关联：常问「登船第一天做什么」，标准答案包含向部门主管报到、确认集合站与安全培训日程——安全永远排在岗位培训之前。
Deep Dive
  • What: The standard administrative process at the start and end of a crew contract: sign on verifies credentials and registers you onboard; sign off closes out affairs and files the record. Governed by MLC and flag-state requirements, with records open to PSC and head-office audits.
  • How: On sign-on day, verify the employment contract, medical certificate, passport, seaman's book and position-specific certificates; then issue the crew card, door access, muster-station assignment and safety briefing confirmation. Sign-off reverses the process: settle your folio, return equipment and uniforms, complete the debrief and file records; flights and onward travel are usually coordinated by HR/the agency.
  • Watch out: Common mistakes: letting a certificate expire mid-contract or too close to it (blocking the next sign-on), a folio not settled at sign-off, and forgotten keys/access cards that turn into disputes. Interview staple: "What do you do on your first day?" — report to your head of department, confirm your muster station and safety training schedule. Safety always comes before job training.

📍 应用场景：船员合同首尾日的行政流程；证书有效期管理与离船结账

MLC 2006 船员权益MLC 2006 (Maritime Labour Convention)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】国际劳工组织的《海事劳工公约》（2006 年通过，俗称「海员权利法案」），规范船员的最低工作与生活标准：工时与休息、工资、餐饮住宿、医疗、申诉机制等；邮轮几乎都悬挂已批准 MLC 的船旗国，船上必须公示并接受检查。
  • 【怎么做】核心量化标准：每 24 小时至少 10 小时休息、每 7 天至少 77 小时（休息时间最多可分为两段，其中一段不少于 6 小时）；客舱、膳食与医疗标准由公约附件规定；船员可通过申诉机制（complaint procedure）逐级投诉——先向直属主管，未解决再向部门总监/船长，直至船旗国或港口国当局，且不得因投诉受到报复。
  • 【易错点】误解一：「MLC 只管水手不管酒店部门」——公约适用于船上全体船员（含娱乐、餐饮员工）；误解二：把休息时间规定当成「理想目标」，实际是 PSC 检查项，工时记录（rest hours records）造假属严重违规。面试关联：了解自己合同期的工时与休息安排是常见加分项。检查要点：休息时间记录如实填写并可追溯、申诉流程张贴于船员区域。
Deep Dive
  • What: The ILO's Maritime Labour Convention 2006 — the "seafarers' bill of rights" — sets minimum standards for hours of work and rest, wages, accommodation and catering, medical care and complaint procedures. Nearly all cruise ships fly flag states that have ratified MLC, so the ship must post and uphold it, subject to inspection.
  • How: Key numbers: at least 10 hours of rest in any 24-hour period and at least 77 hours in any 7-day period (rest may be split into no more than two periods, one at least 6 hours). Accommodation, catering and medical standards follow the Convention annexes. Crew complaints escalate through the onboard complaint procedure: head of department first, then the master, up to the flag state or port authorities — with no retaliation for complaining.
  • Watch out: Myth 1: "MLC only covers deck and engine crews" — it applies to all crew onboard, including entertainment and F&B. Myth 2: treating rest-hour rules as aspirations — they are PSC inspection items, and falsifying rest-hour records is a serious violation. In interviews, knowing your contract's work/rest arrangements earns credit. Key checks: rest-hour records truthful and traceable, complaint procedure posted in crew areas.

📍 应用场景：船员工时记录、住宿餐饮标准核查与申诉渠道；港口国监督检查应对

培训与证书跟踪Training Records / STCW Renewals

深度解析
  • 【是什么】对全体船员强制性证书（STCW 基本安全培训、消防、急救等）与船司内部培训记录的统一管理系统；证书过期者不得在岗，这是船旗国与港口国的硬性检查项。
  • 【怎么做】STCW 证书有效期通常为 5 年，HR 须建立台账并在到期前数月（通常 3–6 个月）发出更新提醒，协调岸上培训点或船上补训；登船时逐人核验证书原件并扫描存档；在职培训（安全演练、岗位复训、反骚扰培训等）同样留痕，与证书台账合并管理。
  • 【易错点】常见漏洞：只盯自己部门的证书、跨部门调岗后新岗位所需的专项证书未补齐；以复印件代替原件核验；培训签到表代签（审计中一旦发现即整体存疑）。检查要点：到期预警机制运行、调岗触发证书复核、所有培训记录含日期/内容/讲师/参训人四方信息。
Deep Dive
  • What: The unified tracking system for all mandatory certificates (STCW Basic Safety Training, fire-fighting, medical first aid, etc.) plus in-house training records. Working on an expired certificate is prohibited — a hard flag-state and port-state inspection item.
  • How: STCW certificates are typically valid 5 years; HR maintains a register and sends renewal reminders 3–6 months ahead, arranging shore-based courses or onboard refreshers. Originals are verified and scanned at sign-on. In-service training (drills, refreshers, anti-harassment training) is logged in the same system.
  • Watch out: Common gaps: tracking only your own department's certificates; transfers to a new position without the role-specific certificates; verifying copies instead of originals; and training sign-in sheets signed by others — once found, the whole record set becomes suspect. Key checks: expiry alerts working, transfers trigger certificate re-checks, and every training record carries date, content, trainer and attendees.

📍 应用场景：登船资质核验、证书到期预警与在职培训记录管理

纪律程序Disciplinary Procedure

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船司对员工违规行为的标准化处理流程，从面谈到遣返分级递进，受 MLC 与船旗国劳动要求约束；程序合规比结论本身更重要——程序瑕疵会让处罚在仲裁或检查中被推翻。
  • 【怎么做】标准链条：面谈（interview，听取员工陈述）→ 口头警告（verbal warning，记录在案）→ 书面警告（written warning）→ 最终警告/停职 → 严重违纪者终止合同并安排遣返（repatriation）；每一步都须书面记录、给员工申辩机会，并由 HR 与部门负责人共同签署；涉及安全或法律问题的（如斗殴、盗窃）可直接进入快速通道。
  • 【易错点】主管最常见的错误：跳过面谈直接处罚、口头警告不留记录（后续无法递进）、在愤怒状态下当场宣布遣返决定。船员角度：收到警告时应确认自己收到书面副本并有权附上申辩意见。面试关联：被问「你如何处理团队违规」时，要点是「程序先于情绪」。检查要点：每一步有书面记录与签名、员工申辩意见被如实记录、处罚与既往案例尺度一致。
Deep Dive
  • What: The cruise line's standardized process for handling crew misconduct, escalating from interview to repatriation, governed by MLC and flag-state labor requirements. Procedural fairness matters more than the outcome — a flawed procedure can overturn a sanction in arbitration or inspection.
  • How: Standard chain: interview (hear the employee's side) → verbal warning on record → written warning → final warning/suspension → termination and repatriation for serious cases. Every step needs written records, a chance for the employee to respond, and joint sign-off by HR and the department head. Safety or legal matters (fighting, theft) can go straight to the fast track.
  • Watch out: Typical supervisor errors: skipping the interview, verbal warnings left unrecorded (blocking later escalation), and announcing repatriation in the heat of the moment. From the crew side: always confirm you received a written copy and that your written response is attached. Interview angle: when asked how you handle team violations, the key phrase is "procedure before emotion." Key checks: records and signatures at every step, employee statements captured faithfully, sanctions consistent with precedent.

📍 应用场景：员工违纪处理与文书留痕环节；主管管理与船员自我保护

船员活动Crew Events / Crew Gym

深度解析
  • 【是什么】HR 福利职能为船员组织的活动与设施体系：体育联赛、电影夜、节日聚会、船员酒吧（crew bar）活动、船员健身房等；合同期 6–9 个月、每天工作 10 小以上的高压环境下，这是留任率与团队士气的直接抓手。
  • 【怎么做】船员健身房按班次错峰开放并公示时段；活动安排须避开主干班次（与部门排班协调发布）；福利专员（crew welfare officer / crew activities coordinator）负责日程、场地与物资；大型活动（节日聚会、体育决赛）常需跨部门支持并控制在公共区域不影响宾客体验。
  • 【易错点】常见问题：活动时间与夜班/晚班船员冲突（等于变相排除一半人）、船员健身房器材损坏报修不及时、活动只在英语主导的氛围中开展让非英语母语船员边缘化。检查要点：活动排期覆盖不同班次、设施报修有闭环、活动通知双语发布。
Deep Dive
  • What: The welfare program HR runs for crew: sports leagues, movie nights, holiday parties, crew bar events and the crew gym. With 6–9-month contracts and 10+ hour days, it is a direct lever on retention and morale.
  • How: The crew gym opens in staggered time slots published by shift; events are scheduled around main shifts in coordination with department rosters; the crew welfare officer runs the calendar, venues and supplies. Big events (holiday parties, league finals) need cross-department support and must stay in crew/public back-of-house areas without affecting the guest experience.
  • Watch out: Common failures: events clashing with night/late shifts (silently excluding half the crew), broken gym equipment with no repair follow-up, and English-only events that marginalize non-native speakers. Key checks: schedules cover all shifts, equipment repairs are closed out, and announcements go out bilingually.

📍 应用场景：船员福利日程与设施运营环节；跨部门排班协调与留任管理

环境保护与设备维修5 个术语▼

← 返回部门图谱（部门卡片内的表格术语可点击跳转到本页对应条目）

垃圾管理计划Garbage Management Plan

深度解析
  • 【是什么】依 MARPOL 公约附则 V（防止船舶垃圾污染）强制编制的船上文件，规定垃圾的分类、收集、储存、加工与排放流程；按公约要求须以船员工作语言书写并指定责任人，环境官（Environmental Officer）是船上有据可查的负责人。
  • 【怎么做】全船垃圾按类别（食物废弃物、塑料、纸类、金属、玻璃、焚烧灰渣、食用油等）分类收集、分别储存，排放严格区分「距陆地 3 海里 / 12 海里 / 25 海里 / 特殊区域」的许可条件——食物废弃物经粉碎后 3 海里外可排、塑料在任何海域禁止排放；垃圾记录簿（Garbage Record Book）逐笔记录排放与焚烧，保留 2 年备查。
  • 【易错点】高频违规：塑料混入食物废弃物袋、GARBAGE 标签缺失或桶盖不匹配、记录簿漏记或事后补记。全员责任：即使不做环境岗，每个部门倒垃圾前都须按计划分类——检查来自港口国与船旗国的随机突击。面试关联：知道「塑料任何海域都不得排海」是基本加分项。
Deep Dive
  • What: The shipboard document required by MARPOL Annex V (prevention of pollution by garbage), governing how garbage is sorted, collected, stored, processed and discharged. The plan must be written in the crew's working language and name a responsible person — on cruise ships, the Environmental Officer is the documented owner.
  • How: Garbage is sorted by category (food waste, plastics, paper, metal, glass, incinerator ash, cooking oil) and stored separately; discharge rules vary by distance and area — comminuted food waste may be discharged beyond 3 nm, plastics are banned from discharge at sea everywhere. The Garbage Record Book logs every discharge and incineration and is kept for 2 years.
  • Watch out: Top violations: plastics mixed into food-waste bags, missing or mismatched GARBAGE labels, and record-book entries logged late. Everyone shares the duty — every department sorts before disposal, since PSC and flag-state inspections are unannounced. Interview bonus: knowing "plastics are banned from discharge at sea in all areas" is baseline knowledge.

📍 应用场景：全船垃圾分类与排放合规环节；垃圾记录簿管理与港口国检查应对

油水分离器Oily Water Separator (15ppm)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】处理机舱舱底水（bilge water）的环保设备：将含油污水分离，只有含油量低于 15ppm（百万分之十五）的排出水才允许在海上排放——这是 MARPOL 附则 I 的核心排放标准。
  • 【怎么做】排放时 15ppm 报警器/监控装置必须在线运行，一旦超标自动停排并回流舱底；每次排放记录油水分离器运行时间、位置与排出水量，取样记录留档备查；含油残渣（污泥/sludge）不得排海，须留存至港口交有资质接收设施并取得接收单据。
  • 【易错点】历史上多起重大罚款案都源于「魔术管」（magic pipe）——绕过分离器直排舱底水，属刑事级别违规，涉事船员与公司均被追责；日常层面高频缺陷是报警器被旁路（bypass）或记录与油类记录簿（Oil Record Book）对不上。检查要点：排放必须经 15ppm 装置、记录簿与设备日志一致、残渣移交单据齐全。
Deep Dive
  • What: The equipment that processes engine-room bilge water by separating oil: only effluent below 15 ppm oil content may be discharged at sea — the core discharge standard of MARPOL Annex I.
  • How: The 15ppm alarm/monitoring unit must run online during discharge and auto-stops with recirculation if the limit is exceeded. Log each discharge's run time, position and volume, keep sampling records on file, and retain oily residues (sludge) for delivery ashore to a licensed facility with receipts.
  • Watch out: Major fines historically came from the "magic pipe" — bypassing the separator to pump bilge straight overboard, a criminal-level offense prosecuted against both crew and company. Day-to-day findings: alarms bypassed, and equipment logs that don't match the Oil Record Book. Key checks: all discharge goes through the 15ppm unit, logs reconcile with the Oil Record Book, residue handover receipts complete.

📍 应用场景：机舱舱底水排放合规环节；油类记录簿管理与残渣岸上移交

危废管理Hazardous Waste Handling

深度解析
  • 【是什么】船上危险废弃物的识别、储存、登记与处置体系：油漆残渣与空桶、废灯管（含汞）、废电池、废化学品、医疗废弃物、废油等均属危废，受 MARPOL、巴塞尔公约及挂靠国法规多重约束。
  • 【怎么做】分类单独储存于指定危废间（通风、防漏、远离热源），容器贴标准标签并保持密封；建立危废台账逐笔登记产生量、储存与移交；只移交岸上有资质的接收机构并取得联单/收据，随垃圾记录簿体系归档；泄漏时按危废应急预案处置并上报环境官。
  • 【易错点】高频错误：荧光灯管与普通垃圾混装、空油漆桶未按「残留容器」处理就丢弃、不同化学品混储引发反应风险。全员提示：客房与餐饮的废弃电池、消毒剂空瓶也属危废，须交指定收集点而非普通垃圾房。检查要点：危废间标签与台账一致、容器无渗漏、移交单据可追溯。
Deep Dive
  • What: The shipboard system for identifying, storing, logging and disposing of hazardous waste: paint residues and empty cans, spent lamps (mercury-containing), batteries, chemicals, medical waste and waste oil, governed by MARPOL, the Basel Convention and the rules of each port state.
  • How: Store categories separately in the designated hazardous-material room (ventilated, spill-contained, away from heat), in sealed, standard-labeled containers. Keep a waste log of quantities generated, stored and transferred; hand over only to licensed shore facilities with transfer receipts filed within the garbage-record system. Leaks follow the hazardous-spill emergency procedure and are reported to the Environmental Officer.
  • Watch out: Common errors: fluorescent tubes mixed with general garbage, paint cans discarded as "residue containers" without proper handling, and incompatible chemicals stored together. Crew note: dead batteries and empty disinfectant bottles from cabins and F&B are hazardous waste too — they go to collection points, not the regular garbage room. Key checks: labels match the log, containers leak-free, transfer receipts traceable.

📍 应用场景：危废分类储存与岸上移交环节；泄漏应急与全员收集点管理

预防性维护系统Planned Maintenance System (PMS)

深度解析
  • 【是什么】以计划保养取代「坏了再修」的数字化维护管理系统：所有设备的周期性保养任务（按运行小时/日历周期）在系统中自动生成工单，维修部门据以调度、执行与留痕；邮轮行业最常见平台是 AMOS。
  • 【怎么做】技师领取工单 → 执行保养/维修 → 记录工时、更换件与结果 → 主管验收关闭工单，形成闭环；酒店区域报修（客舱空调、灯光、洁具等）也进入同一系统，按优先级派单（影响宾客体验的为高优先级）；工单历史构成设备健康档案，用于备件计划与审计。
  • 【易错点】高频缺陷：完工不关单（系统里积压大量「待办」但实际已做完，审计视为管理失控）、代签验收、跳过安全挂牌（lock-out/tag-out）直接作业。面试关联：被问「你不熟悉 AMOS 怎么办」，要点是强调同类 PMS 系统的学习经历与现场经验可迁移。检查要点：工单闭环率、更换件与库存对账、高优先级工单响应时限达标。
Deep Dive
  • What: The digital planned-maintenance system that replaces "fix it when it breaks": recurring tasks for every equipment item (by running hours or calendar cycle) generate work orders automatically, which the technical department dispatches, executes and documents. AMOS is the industry-standard platform on cruise ships.
  • How: The flow: receive the work order → perform the maintenance → log hours, parts and results → supervisor verifies and closes the order. Hotel-area repairs (cabin HVAC, lighting, sanitary ware) enter the same system, dispatched by priority, with guest-impact items flagged high. Work-order history forms each asset's health record for spares planning and audits.
  • Watch out: Common audit findings: work completed but never closed (a backlog of "open" orders reads as loss of control), sign-offs by others, and skipping lock-out/tag-out. Interview angle: if asked about unfamiliarity with AMOS, stress transferable PMS experience and hands-on background. Key checks: work-order closure rate, parts issued reconciled with inventory, high-priority response times met.

📍 应用场景：维修工单调度与闭环管理环节；设备健康档案与备件计划

多技能工Multi-skilled Fitter

深度解析
  • 【是什么】邮轮维修岗位的基本用人特征：船上空间与人力有限，酒店维修技师通常需「木工 + 管道 + 油漆 + 基础电气」一专多能，与岸上严格按工种分工（木匠只做木工等）的模式完全不同。
  • 【怎么做】典型一日工单可能横跨多个工种：上午修客舱门锁与铰链（木/金工）、下午更换舱内漏水水龙头（管道）、傍晚为墙面补漆（油漆）；招聘时简历应突出跨工种实操证据而非单一证书；入职后由主管按 PMS 工单实际表现确认技能边界，超出边界的工作（如带电作业）必须转交持证电工。
  • 【易错点】两个极端都危险：一是「什么都能修」心态导致越界操作（如擅自处理配电箱，属严重安全违规）；二是只愿做本专业导致工单积压。面试关联：这是维修类面试的核心考点——准备 2–3 个跨工种维修实例（工具、步骤、结果量化），比罗列证书更有说服力。
Deep Dive
  • What: A defining feature of cruise maintenance staffing: with limited space and headcount, hotel maintenance fitters must combine carpentry, plumbing, painting and basic electrical — the opposite of the strict trade separation ashore, where each trade does only its own work.
  • How: A typical day's work orders can span trades: cabin door locks and hinges (carpentry/metal) in the morning, a leaking basin tap (plumbing) in the afternoon, wall touch-up paint by evening. Resumes should show cross-trade hands-on evidence rather than a single certificate; after joining, supervisors confirm skill boundaries from actual PMS work orders, and out-of-scope work (live electrical) must go to a licensed electrician.
  • Watch out: Both extremes are dangerous: the "I can fix anything" mindset leading to out-of-scope work (opening a distribution board unaided is a serious safety violation), or sticking rigidly to one trade while work orders pile up. Interview angle: this is the core test for maintenance roles — bring 2–3 cross-trade repair stories with tools, steps and measurable results; they beat certificate lists.

📍 应用场景：酒店维修技师招聘与工单执行环节；技能边界确认与面试准备

⚠️ 内容为行业通用知识参考，各船司实际操作标准以员工手册与官方培训为准。 For general reference only — always follow your cruise line's official training materials.
